### [1] [Auditing Source Exposure in Baidu and Google AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24407)
*Li, Yibo,Guan, Enci,Cai, Yuedan,Liu, Geng,Pierri, Francesco*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对 Baidu 和 Google 的 AI 搜索概览进行跨语言审计，使用 MS MARCO 英文查询及其中文翻译，考察概览触发率、中文概览中可见主机域曝光集中度、跨平台来源重叠以及匹配查询答案的嵌入语义相似度。结果显示不同平台-语言设置在概览可用性和可见来源曝光上差异显著，聚合来源清单重叠低，而匹配查询答案的中位余弦相似度为 0.701–0.813，表明答案语义相似度与来源曝光是 AI 介导搜索的两个不同评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文搜索环境下 AI 概览的触发行为、可见来源域曝光分布以及跨平台跨语言答案语义一致性尚不清楚，需要跨语言审计来揭示 AI 介导搜索中的来源可见性差异。

Method: 论文为审计研究，未披露具体 AI 概览生成系统的内部架构。审计流程大致为：从 MS MARCO 采样英文查询并翻译为中文，分别在 Baidu 和 Google 上以英文和中文查询触发 AI 概览；记录概览是否触发、引用的主机域名；计算中文概览中可见主机域名的曝光集中度与跨设置来源重叠；对匹配查询意图的生成答案使用嵌入模型计算语义相似度（中位余弦相似度 0.701–0.813）。论文未说明摘要生成模型、检索器、排序器或服务端细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "matched-query answer median cosine similarity", "value": "0.701–0.813", "setting": "跨平台-语言设置下匹配查询意图的生成答案嵌入相似度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对 Baidu 和 Google 的 AI 概览进行跨语言（英文 MS MARCO 查询及其中文翻译）审计；分析 AI 概览的触发条件在不同平台-语言设置下的差异；测量中文概览中可见主机域名的曝光集中度与来源重叠；比较匹配查询意图下生成答案的嵌入语义相似度，并提出答案级语义相似度与聚合来源曝光是 AI 搜索评估的两个不同维度

Abstract: AI-generated overviews are becoming an increasingly prominent layer of search interfaces, yet their behavior in Chinese-language search remains underexplored. We conduct a cross-lingual audit of AI overview behavior on Baidu and Google using English queries sampled from MS MARCO and their translated Chinese counterparts. Our analysis examines when overviews are triggered across platform-language settings, which host domains receive visible exposure in Chinese-language overviews, how concentrated that exposure is, and how source overlap varies across settings. We also compare the embedding-based semantic similarity of generated answers for matched query intents. The results reveal substantial differences across platform-language settings in overview availability and visible source exposure. At the aggregate level, the settings exhibit low overlap in visible host-domain inventories, while matched-query answers yield median cosine similarities ranging from 0.701 to 0.813. These findings indicate that answer-level semantic similarity and aggregate source exposure capture distinct dimensions of AI-mediated search. Evaluations of AI search should therefore consider not only the content of generated answers but also how source visibility is distributed across platforms, languages, and information environments.

</details>


### [2] [Graded-Relevance Composed Multimodal Retrieval for E-commerce Visual Search at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24152)
*Gupta, Anubhav,Mohapatra, Hrushikesh,Chandra, Prijith,Mohapatra, Asish,Garg, Anuj,Maan, Arvind,Datta, Sudip,Bulusu, Venkat,Jalan, Sitesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Walmart Global Tech 提出 GradCIR：面向大规模电商视觉搜索的组合图像检索（CIR）方法，用 VLM 自动生成查询与4级相关性标签（3.5M 分级配对），配合硬负例迭代反馈与层级感知角度损失，在 PaliGemma2 双塔上训练。受控消融显示分级监督相比二值监督 NDCG@10 提升 4.9%-5.9%，应用于其他多模态骨干可提升最高 8.5%；FashionIQ 上微调后平均召回 0.6703。系统已在 Walmart 生产环境服务真实视觉搜索流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CIR 方法将相关性视为二值（单正样本 triplet），无法建模真实目录中大量部分满足查询的候选，而跨部分匹配谱的排序质量直接决定用户视觉搜索体验。

Method: 系统以 PaliGemma2 双编码器（bi-encoder）为检索骨干，将图像与文本（组合查询）分别编码到共享嵌入空间进行检索。数据侧：用 VLM 在原始 Walmart 目录图片上执行目标检测以定位可修饰对象，并合成修饰文本生成组合查询，同时产出4级相关性标签，构成 3.5M 分级配对；无需人工标注。训练侧：采用层级感知的角度目标函数，直接在4级分级标签上优化嵌入空间的角度/相似度关系，保留部分匹配的序关系而非坍缩为二值。训练过程中引入迭代相关性反馈循环，使用当前训练中的检索器挖掘硬负例，扩充训练集并重复训练。推理侧：用户上传图片或图片+文本后，经双编码器得到查询嵌入，在商品库嵌入上做近邻检索，按学到的分级相关性谱排序返回候选，服务线上视觉搜索流量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "NDCG@10", "value": "+4.9%-5.9%", "setting": "PaliGemma2 双塔上 graded 监督 vs binary 监督的受控消融"}, {"metric": "NDCG@10", "value": "最高 +8.5%", "setting": "同一 recipe 应用于其他多模态编码器（早期融合骨干）"}, {"metric": "average recall", "value": "0.6703", "setting": "公开 FashionIQ 基准，GradCIR(PaliGemma2) 微调后，略超最强同行评审监督基线，匹配或超过所有已发表 CLIP-L 级零样本 CIR 方法"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用 VLM 自动策展训练数据：通过目标检测+修饰合成生成查询，并生成4级相关性标签，无需人工标注；迭代式相关性反馈循环：从训练中的检索器挖掘硬负例以扩充训练集；层级感知的角度目标（hierarchy-aware angular objective），直接在分级标签上训练检索器，而非坍缩为二值划分；在 PaliGemma2 双塔模型上实例化，使用从 Walmart 原始目录数据策展的 3.5M 分级配对数据；受控的 graded-vs-binary 消融实验，隔离监督粒度的影响

Abstract: Visual search on large e-commerce catalogs must serve both "similarity" queries that ask for items resembling an uploaded image and "modifier" queries that comprise an image and text describing a desired modification (e.g. a color change or style swap). The latter is the setting known as composed image retrieval (CIR). Existing CIR methods, however, treat relevance as binary and train on triplets with a single positive target - a poor fit for real catalogs where many candidates partially satisfy a user query and ranking across that partial-match spectrum drives the customer experience. We propose a methodology for training CIR retrievers on graded relevance, consisting of: (i) a VLM to curate training data, generating both queries (object detection + modifier synthesis) and 4-level relevance labels without manual annotation, (ii) an iterative relevance-feedback loop that expands the training set by mining hard negatives from the in-training retriever, and (iii) a hierarchy-aware angular objective to train the retriever directly on the graded labels rather than collapsing them to a binary split. We call this methodology GradCIR and instantiate it on a PaliGemma2 bi-encoder trained on 3.5M graded pairs curated from raw Walmart catalog data. A controlled graded-vs-binary ablation isolates the supervision granularity and shows lift of 4.9%-5.9% in NDCG@10. The same recipe applied to other multimodal encoders lifts early-fusion backbones by up to 8.5% NDCG@10. On the public FashionIQ benchmark, GradCIR (applied to PaliGemma2) reaches 0.6703 average recall when fine-tuned, slightly ahead of the strongest peer-reviewed supervised baseline we compare against, and matching or exceeding all published CLIP-L-class zero-shot CIR methods. The system is deployed in production at Walmart, where it's serving live visual-search user traffic.

</details>


### [3] [A Redundancy Reduction Approach for Controllable Sequential Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23849)
*Ivanova, Veronika,Munkhoeva, Marina,Razvorotnev, Ivan,Frolov, Evgeny*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究特征去相关作为塑造点积序列推荐器表示几何的机制，提出BT-SR——基于Barlow Twins的去相关正则化训练框架，在下一项预测上增加冗余减少项，并用标签一致的正样本对构建方式。在五个公开基准上，BT-SR一致提升下一项排序质量，去相关强度可作为控制旋钮在头部和尾部物品间重新分配精度，实现精度-曝光权衡。论文还给出几何分析并引入桶对齐集中度指标量化效果，但未报告在线A/B或生产部署结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐在长尾物品分布和流行度驱动的集中效应下，如何在不牺牲短列表精度的同时控制长尾曝光，实现精度与曝光之间的可控权衡。

Method: BT-SR基于点积序列推荐器，输入用户历史序列，通过编码器得到用户表示，并与物品嵌入做点积计算下一项预测分数。训练时在标准的下一项预测损失上增加一个辅助冗余减少项，采用Barlow Twins目标。正样本对的构建方式为：共享相同下一项目标的用户历史被配成对，而非对同一序列做合成扰动。该去相关项作用于用户表示空间，抑制表示维度间的相关性，从而减少共享的低秩方向，这些低秩方向会让流行物品获得全局打分优势。通过调节去相关强度（权重），可以控制流行物品与长尾物品的曝光分配。训练目标联合优化下一项预测精度和去相关项，推理时使用训练好的编码器计算用户表示并进行点积检索或排序。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "next-item ranking quality", "value": "持续提升", "setting": "五个公开基准数据集"}, {"metric": "精度-曝光权衡", "value": "去相关强度可重新分配头部和尾部物品的精度", "setting": "五个公开基准数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将特征去相关作为塑造点积序列推荐器表示几何的机制，并分析其对流行度驱动集中效应的影响；提出去相关正则化的训练框架，在下一项预测目标上增加辅助冗余减少项，BT-SR采用Barlow Twins目标实例化；通过共享相同下一项目标的用户历史构建标签一致的正样本对，避免合成扰动；几何分析表明去相关抑制用户表示空间中的共享低秩方向，从而削弱流行物品的全局打分优势；提出基于桶的对齐集中度指标来量化该效应，并发现去相关强度可作为简单控制旋钮在头部和尾部物品间重新分配精度

Abstract: Sequential recommendation must operate under long-tailed item distributions and popularity-driven concentration, often forcing practitioners to trade short-list accuracy against long-tail exposure. In this work, we study feature decorrelation as a mechanism for shaping representation geometry in dot-product sequential recommenders, and analyze how this, in turn, affects popularity-driven concentration. We propose a decorrelation-regularized training framework that augments next-item prediction with an auxiliary redundancy-reduction term, and instantiate it with BT-SR, which uses the Barlow Twins objective. To form label-consistent positive pairs without synthetic corruptions, we pair user histories that share the same next-item target. Beyond accuracy, we provide a geometric analysis showing how decorrelation suppresses shared low-rank directions in the user representation space that can give popular items a global scoring advantage, and we introduce a bucket-based alignment concentration metric to quantify this effect. Experiments on five public benchmarks show that BT-SR consistently improves next-item ranking quality, while the decorrelation strength acts as a simple control knob that reallocates accuracy across head and tail items, enabling accuracy-exposure trade-offs. Our analysis also reveals that the impact on head-vs-tail exposure differs across datasets, reflecting interactions between decorrelation and data temporal structure.

</details>


### [4] [UNIQUE: A Unified Retrieval and Ranking System for Large-Scale Feed Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23718)
*Liu, Zhuang,Fu, Yongkang,Yang, Zuodong,Chen, Guangxing,Wu, Zonggang,Lu, Yuqi,Qin, Shouke,Li, Shantao,Wang, Maolin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UNIQUE 是百度提出的统一检索与排序推荐框架，使用单层扁平量化和早期融合架构，在 Mobile Baidu 多场景部署，在线 A/B 测试获得总观看时长 +0.96% 和总分发量 +1.08%，P99 延迟 89 ms，推理 MFU 44.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级移动 Feed 推荐系统中，层次量化候选召回不稳定，且检索与排序阶段分离导致信息损失，损害长尾和冷启动推荐并增加服务复杂度。

Method: UNIQUE 将生成式编码检索与目标感知排序融合到一个早期融合架构中。检索部分使用生成式编码，采用单层扁平量化替代层次量化，以缓解层次量化不稳定和码本不平衡问题；引入平衡量化机制优化长尾表示。排序部分为目标感知排序模块，与检索模块共享表示并在端到端框架下联合训练，解决检索与排序阶段分离造成的信息损失。推理时，系统在共享表示下高效生成候选并进行排序，服务于 Mobile Baidu 大规模 Feed 推荐，在线 P99 延迟为 89 ms，推理 MFU 为 44.23%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "总观看时长", "value": "0.96%", "setting": "Mobile Baidu 主页 Feed、发现页和短视频推荐场景的在线 A/B 测试"}, {"metric": "总分发量", "value": "1.08%", "setting": "Mobile Baidu 主页 Feed、发现页和短视频推荐场景的在线 A/B 测试"}]}

Conclusion: 统一检索与排序的单层扁平量化框架（UNIQUE）；生成式编码检索与目标感知排序的早期融合架构；共享表示下的端到端训练；平衡量化机制以缓解码本不平衡并改善长尾表示

Abstract: Industrial mobile feed systems rely on a retrieval-ranking pipeline to serve large-scale, heterogeneous, and fast-changing content under strict latency constraints. However, existing pipelines still suffer from two critical issues: hierarchical quantization instability in candidate retrieval and information loss between separated retrieval and ranking stages. These issues hurt long-tail and cold-start recommendation and complicate efficient serving. To address them, we present UNIQUE, a unified retrieval and ranking recommendation framework with single-layer flat quantization. UNIQUE integrates generative code-based retrieval and target-aware ranking into one early-fusion architecture, enabling end-to-end training under a shared representation while preserving efficient candidate generation. A balanced quantization mechanism is further introduced to mitigate codebook imbalance and improve long-tail representation. Offline experiments evaluate UNIQUE from both retrieval and ranking perspectives, while codebook analysis shows more balanced resource allocation than hierarchical quantization. We deploy UNIQUE in the homepage feed, discovery-page, and short-video recommendation scenarios of Mobile Baidu, serving large-scale real-world traffic. Online A/B tests achieve a 0.96% gain in total watch duration and a 1.08% gain in total distribution volume, with notable improvements for new users and highly active users. Serving measurements show 89 ms P99 latency and 44.23% online inference MFU. These results show that UNIQUE provides a stable, efficient, and production-ready framework for unified retrieval and ranking in industrial recommendation.

</details>


### [5] [MuSeR: Scalable Long-sequence Recommendation with Multi-interest Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23677)
*Fu, Yongkang,Bao, Beining,Jiang, Yu,Zhao, Xiangyu,Wei, Hongyang,Chen, Guangxing,Yang, Zuodong,Li, Shantao,Wu, Zonggang,Lu, Yuqi,Qin, Shouke,Liu, Hanmeng,Wang, Maolin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MuSeR 是百度在已部署 MGS 系统上构建的超长序列多兴趣召回框架，通过层次化时间压缩、解耦多查询兴趣提取与多模态 LLM 语义对齐，在百度 APP 三个场景的在线 A/B 中取得 DAU +0.26%、总时长 +0.89% 的显著提升并降低服务延迟与成本。论文侧重系统级集成与工程实践，而非新建模原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统在严格延迟与内存预算下通常将用户历史截断到数百条，导致 10^4-10^5 量级的长期兴趣未被利用；同时用户跨新闻、问答、短视频等多模态存在异质多兴趣，稀疏 ID 嵌入难以表达。

Method: MuSeR 建立在已部署的 MGS 系统之上，作为检索框架替换/增强用户表征与召回阶段。整体输入为用户超长行为序列（10^4-10^5 交互）与物品多模态内容。流程为：(1) 层次化时间压缩模块：对最近行为保留完整分辨率的序列表示，对更早的历史按时间段进行渐进式池化（progressive pooling），在保持长期信号的同时将序列压缩至固定长度的服务预算内；(2) 解耦多查询兴趣提取：使用多个查询（query）从压缩后的序列表征中抽取多个异质兴趣向量，并施加正交性正则化（orthogonality regularization）以降低兴趣间冗余；(3) 多模态语义对齐：将大语言模型蒸馏得到的文本摘要作为物品语义表示，与稀疏 ID 嵌入结合，缓解冷启动与稀疏 ID 表达不足。训练目标以召回为导向，通过 Recall@K 在长序列与多兴趣强基线上验证。在线服务侧采用异步用户表示刷新与自适应缓存，允许用户兴趣表示在后台更新而不阻塞请求，并基于异构硬件执行层次化 beam-search 检索以生成候选。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Recall@K", "value": "consistent improvements over strong long-sequence and multi-interest baselines", "setting": "3 public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Daily Active Users", "value": "+0.26%", "setting": "Baidu APP 首页信息流、发现流、短视频场景在线 A/B 测试，p<0.05"}, {"metric": "Total Session Duration", "value": "+0.89%", "setting": "Baidu APP 首页信息流、发现流、短视频场景在线 A/B 测试，p<0.05"}, {"metric": "Serving latency and cost", "value": "reduced", "setting": "工业部署"}]}

Conclusion: 层次化时间压缩：近期行为保留全分辨率，较旧分段渐进池化，使 10^4-10^5 交互长度在固定服务预算内可表示；解耦多查询兴趣提取并引入正交性正则，缓解多兴趣间冗余；多模态语义对齐：用大语言模型蒸馏的文本摘要补充稀疏物品 ID；面向工业部署的异步用户表示刷新与自适应缓存；面向异构硬件的层次化 beam-search 检索

Abstract: Ultra-long user behavior sequences carry rich signals of stable and diverse preferences, yet industrial recommender systems typically truncate histories to a few hundred actions under strict latency and memory budgets, leaving long-term interests under-utilized. Users also pursue multiple heterogeneous intents across modalities such as news, Q&A, and short video, which sparse ID embeddings alone struggle to represent. We present Multi-interest Sequence Representation (MuSeR), a retrieval framework built on the deployed MGS system, which integrates three components: (i) hierarchical temporal compression, which retains recent actions at full resolution while progressively pooling older segments, so that per-user histories of $10^{4}$-$10^{5}$ interactions fit within a fixed serving budget; (ii) disentangled multi-query interest extraction with orthogonality regularization; and (iii) multimodal semantic alignment, which augments sparse item IDs with textual summaries distilled from a large language model. For industrial deployment, MuSeR further adopts asynchronous user-representation refresh with adaptive caching and hierarchical beam-search retrieval across heterogeneous hardware. On three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, MuSeR consistently improves Recall@$K$ over strong long-sequence and multi-interest baselines. In online A/B tests on Baidu APP's homepage feed, discovery feed, and short-video scenarios, MuSeR yields +0.26% daily active users and +0.89% total session duration (both statistically significant, p<0.05), alongside reduced serving latency and cost. Rather than proposing a new modeling primitive, our contribution is a system-level integration that makes long-term, multi-interest, and multimodal modeling jointly deployable in a real-time production pipeline, together with the engineering practices required to sustain it.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Relevance: Structured Semantic Supervision for Product Search with LLM-Augmented Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23646)
*Koushik, Girish A.,Bhosale, Swapnil,Agrawal, Samarth,Sadany, Hadeel,Orasan, Constantin,Zhu, Xiatian,Kanojia, Diptesh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文在ESCI增强子集上研究电商搜索的意图级相关性，通过LLM生成结构化查询/商品属性并配合人工验证的解释与中心性信号，用双编码器检索加MLP重排序验证；人工oracle达0.9382 nDCG@10，无人工Q+P达0.9258，合成信号达0.9150且在困难查询上增益明显，说明LLM更适合暴露与近似结构化语义监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索需要区分“仅仅相关”与“直接满足用户购物意图”的商品，传统相关性信号不足以刻画购物意图层面的语义差异。

Method: 输入为查询-商品对；系统先通过LLM为查询和商品生成结构化属性，并结合人工验证的相关性、解释和中心性判断，形成结构化语义监督信号。检索阶段使用简单双编码器（dual-encoder）retriever 得到候选，随后用MLP re-ranker对候选进行重排序。训练上分别考察人工特征oracle、无人工的Q+P配置以及人类信号的合成近似，并在ESCI增强子集上以nDCG@10评估。消融显示oracle提升主要来自后编辑解释和标注者评论而非标量中心性特征。推理时直接使用训练好的双编码器检索与MLP重排序，不需要在线人工信号。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.9382", "setting": "ESCI增强子集上的人工特征oracle"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.9258", "setting": "ESCI增强子集上无人工训练的Q+P配置"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.9150", "setting": "合成近似人类信号的总体表现，在困难低性能查询上有显著提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用LLM生成结构化查询与商品属性，并配合人工验证的相关性、解释和中心性判断，构造结构化语义监督信号。；提出人工特征oracle与无人工训练的Q+P配置进行对比，量化人类信号与合成近似信号的增益上限。；通过消融发现oracle收益主要来自后编辑解释和标注者评论，而非标量中心性特征，说明LLM更适合暴露和近似结构化语义监督而非直接替代人类判断。

Abstract: E-commerce search requires distinguishing products that are merely related to a query from those that directly satisfy the user's shopping intent. We augment query-product pairs with structured LLM-generated query and product attributes and human-validated relevance, explanations, and centrality judgments, and evaluate these signals using a simple dual-encoder retriever and MLP re-ranker. On an augmented subset of ESCI, a human-feature oracle reaches $0.9382$ nDCG@10, while a human-free trained $Q+P$ configuration reaches $0.9258$. Synthetic approximations of the human signals reach $0.9150$ overall but provide substantial gains for difficult, low-performing queries. Ablations show that most of the oracle improvement comes from post-edited explanations and annotator comments rather than the scalar centrality feature, suggesting that LLMs are most useful for exposing and approximating structured semantic supervision rather than replacing human judgment directly.

</details>


### [7] [PSD: Pseudo Self-Distillation of Memory Representation Capabilities for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23449)
*Suhail, Pirzada,Xia, Menglin,Zhang, Xuchao,Das, Mayukh,Bansal, Chetan,Rajmohan, Saravan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PSD提出伪自蒸馏框架，让单个小模型同时作为教师（接收含oracle答案的特权提示）和学生（仅接收任务提示），从而在无法访问闭源大模型内部状态的情况下，将黑盒oracle的记忆构建能力蒸馏到小模型权重中。在LoCoMo上，0.6B-4B的Qwen3模型经PSD训练后下游检索性能匹配或超过GPT-4.1-mini，且能零样本迁移到LongMemEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建和维护LLM Agent记忆系统依赖反复调用大型专有语言模型，成本高昂，难以规模化部署。

Method: PSD框架采用单一小语言模型（SLM）在两个角色间切换：教师角色接收特权提示，该提示包含一个强黑盒oracle模型针对当前任务生成的答案作为参考上下文；学生角色仅接收原始任务提示（无oracle答案）。教师基于特权提示生成输出分布，学生尝试模仿该分布。通过多阶段训练管道，学生模型逐步学习将oracle引导的答案生成行为内化到自身权重中。训练目标是最小化学生输出分布与教师输出分布之间的差异（例如KL散度），但不需要访问教师模型的logits或隐藏状态，仅通过提示传递信息。推理时，训练好的小模型仅使用任务提示即可构建分层记忆表示，无需oracle参与。该方法解决了闭源大模型无法提供内部监督信号的问题，实现了黑盒oracle知识向小模型的迁移。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游检索性能", "value": "匹配或超过GPT-4.1-mini", "setting": "LoCoMo数据集，Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B经PSD训练"}, {"metric": "跨域迁移检索性能", "value": "成功迁移", "setting": "LongMemEval数据集，仅用LoCoMo训练无LongMemEval暴露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 伪自蒸馏（PSD）框架：单个小模型同时充当教师和学生，教师接收包含oracle答案的特权提示，学生仅接收任务提示，学生模仿教师的输出分布，从而将黑盒oracle行为蒸馏进小模型权重；无需访问教师模型的logits或隐藏状态，仅通过提示传递外部oracle知识，适用于黑盒闭源模型；多阶段训练管道实现分层记忆表示构造；展示记忆构建能力从LoCoMo到LongMemEval的分布外迁移

Abstract: Memory systems are becoming a core component of LLM agents, but constructing and maintaining memory remains expensive because it relies on repeated calls to large proprietary language models. This cost creates a major barrier to deploying memory-enhanced agents at scale. In this paper, we present Pseudo Self-Distillation (PSD), a framework that enables small language models (SLMs) to construct hierarchical memory representations by distilling behavior from a strong black-box oracle through a multi-stage training pipeline. Standard distillation methods require access to teacher logits or hidden states, which closed models do not expose. Unlike conventional self-distillation settings, where supervision is derived from a model's own predictions, sampled rollouts, or aggregated outputs, PSD enables a single-model distillation setup while channeling external oracle knowledge through the prompt. PSD uses a single small model in two roles: a teacher that sees a privileged prompt containing the oracle's answer as reference context, and a student that sees only the task prompt. The student learns to reproduce the teacher's output distribution, absorbing oracle-guided behavior into its own weights without accessing the oracle's internals. On LoCoMo, PSD-trained Qwen3-0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B match or exceed GPT-4.1-mini on downstream retrieval at a fraction of the deployment cost, with off-policy PSD achieving the strongest results across most conditions. We further show that this memory-construction capability transfers out-of-distribution to LongMemEval, despite the students being trained exclusively on LoCoMo with no exposure to LongMemEval data.

</details>

*Zhong, Yunfei,Cai, Yinqiong,Su, Lixin,Qian, Haosheng,Zou, Lixin,Fan, Yixing,Xu, Sheng,Guo, Jiafeng,Shi, Daiting,He, Jingzhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文将AI搜索中的检索目标重新定义为答案导向的上下文构建，提出三阶段框架（答案支持、内容可信度、上下文组织）及工业工作流与评估协议，在检索与答案层面均报告了一致提升，但未披露公司、在线A/B或具体量化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI搜索范式下，检索目标应从按用户搜索满意度对文档排序，转变为在有限上下文预算下构建可靠上下文，以保证生成答案的正确性与鲁棒性。

Method: 论文提出一个面向AI搜索的三阶段上下文构建框架。输入为传统检索系统返回的候选文档集合；第一阶段'答案支持'识别对答案生成有信息贡献的候选文档；第二阶段'内容可信度'从来源、时间、事实三个维度评估信息是否提供可靠依据以支持正确答案；第三阶段'上下文组织'在有限上下文预算下选择、合并并结构化保留的信息，以支持一致且鲁棒的生成。此外，论文提出一个覆盖先验与后验优化的工业工作流，并建立同时评估检索侧上下文与最终答案的系统化评估协议。摘要未披露具体模块参数、训练目标或推理细节，无法进一步推断。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将AI搜索中的检索目标形式化为答案导向的上下文构建（answer-oriented context construction）；提出三阶段框架：答案支持（Answer Support）、内容可信度（Content Trustworthiness）、上下文组织（Context Organization）；建立覆盖检索侧上下文与最终答案的系统化评估协议；提出包含先验与后验优化的工业工作流

Abstract: Traditional Web search follows a human-facing paradigm in which users inspect ranked documents and synthesize information themselves. In AI Search, retrieved documents instead serve as inputs to a generation model, shifting the retrieval objective from ranking documents by Search Satisfaction to constructing reliable context for correct answer generation. We formulate this shift as answer-oriented context construction through a three-stage framework: (1) Answer Support identifies candidate documents that contribute information to answer generation; (2) Content Trustworthiness assesses whether this information provides a reliable basis for correct answers from source, temporal, and factual perspectives; and (3) Context Organization selects, consolidates, and structures retained information under a finite context budget for consistent and robust generation. We further develop an industrial workflow spanning prior and posterior optimization and establish a systematic evaluation protocol covering both retrieval-side context and final answers. Experiments show consistent improvements at both Retrieval and Answer levels, demonstrating the effectiveness of the framework and its industrial implementation.

</details>

*Yashwant, Sai,Jain, Siddhartha,Dubey, Anurag,Chatterjee, Samaroha,Thulsiram, Gantala*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文面向高流量招聘匹配业务，比较了EmbeddingGemma基础版、Cached-MNRL微调版与MPNet在统一混合检索管线中的表现。管线使用LLM解析职位描述为结构化搜索文本与关键词，候选人简历索引为语义表示，并通过RRF融合向量与全文检索。评估在部署的AI-Match评分管线和LLM-as-a-Judge下进行，同时阐述了对比微调目标的取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用嵌入模型基准与企业级候选人-职位匹配约束之间存在差距，需要在真实招聘检索条件下比较稠密嵌入策略，特别是微调目标与混合检索融合方式的选择。

Method: 系统输入分为职位端与候选人端。职位描述首先经过LLM解析模块，转换为结构化的英文搜索文本以及语言特定的关键词，用于后续全文检索与语义编码。候选人画像则被索引为语义增强的简历表示，可能包含结构化字段的文本化与嵌入。检索阶段，职位查询同时进入向量检索通道和全文检索通道：向量通道使用稠密嵌入模型（EmbeddingGemma基础版或经Cached-MNRL微调的版本）计算查询-文档相似度；全文通道使用解析得到的结构化文本与关键词计算相关性。两路结果通过倒数排名融合（RRF）合并为统一的候选排序。嵌入模型的训练目标方面，论文采用Cached Multiple Negatives Ranking Loss进行微调，并对比了AnglE/CoSENT等对比学习目标，最终保留仅Cached-MNRL的配置。评估分为两部分：微调收敛诊断以及使用部署的AI-Match评分管线和独立LLM-as-a-Judge相关性评分的批量比较评估，后者在统一混合检索协议下比较EmbeddingGemma基础版、微调版与MPNet。推理时，职位查询经过解析和嵌入，触发双通道检索，RRF融合后送入评分与排序环节。

Conclusion: 将职位描述通过LLM解析为结构化英文搜索文本与语言特定关键词，候选人简历转换为语义增强的表示。；构建统一的混合检索管线，使用倒数排名融合（RRF）融合向量相似度与全文相关性。；对EmbeddingGemma进行Cached Multiple Negatives Ranking Loss（MNRL）微调，并与基础版及MPNet进行对比。；以数学细节记录并比较多种对比微调目标（包括AnglE/CoSENT风格精调），论证仅保留Cached-MNRL适配的经验依据。；定义更广泛的评估框架，包括Recall@K、MRR、nDCG，并明确所报告指标限于微调收敛诊断与批量比较评估（部署的AI-Match分数和独立LLM-as-a-Judge相关性分数）。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of dense embedding models for semantic candidate-job matching in high-volume staffing workflows. Incoming job descriptions are converted into structured English search text and language-specific keywords through LLM-based parsing, and candidate profiles are indexed as semantically enriched resume representations. We evaluate EmbeddingGemma (base) against EmbeddingGemma fine-tuned with Cached Multiple Negatives Ranking Loss (MNRL) within a unified hybrid retrieval pipeline that fuses vector similarity and full-text relevance via reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), and benchmark both against the MPNet model on a batch comparative evaluation dataset scored through the deployed job-candidate matching scoring pipeline. We further document, with mathematical detail, the broader set of contrastive fine-tuning objectives considered during model development (including AnglE/CoSENT-style refinement) and the empirical rationale for retaining Cached-MNRL-only adaptation as the preferred configuration. To support reproducible model selection, we define a broader evaluation framework comprising standard information retrieval metrics (Recall@K, mean reciprocal rank, nDCG) under the exact hybrid-retrieval protocol; the metrics used for the evaluation reported in this paper are fine-tuning convergence diagnostics and a batch comparative evaluation using the deployed AI-Match score and an independent LLM-as-a-Judge relevance score, and we state this scope explicitly rather than implying the full framework was measured. The paper addresses the gap between general-purpose embedding benchmarks and enterprise job-candidate matching constraints, providing a structured basis for comparing embedding strategies under realistic job-candidate retrieval conditions.

</details>


### [10] [Inherit4Rec: Parameter Inheritance for Efficient Scaling of Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23111)
*Zhang, Ruihao,Chen, Bo,Wang, Xiao,Jiao, Jinlong,Hu, Tijian,Jia, Qinglin,He, Xiuqiang,Zhao, Xiangyu,Ma, Chaoyi,Tang, Ruiming,Ou, Wenwu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Inherit4Rec 提出参数继承框架，支持推荐模型的稠密到稠密扩容（混合增长+非对称训练）与稠密到稀疏转换（共激活感知划分+负载均衡损失构建 SMoE）。在 KuaiRand-1K 与工业短视频推荐数据集上，所有预测目标均优于所评估的继承基线，面向工业推荐系统的持续容量扩展与计算高效稀疏化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态演化的推荐数据场景下，如何通过参数继承实现稠密推荐模型的高效扩容（Dense-to-Dense）与向稀疏专家模型（Dense-to-Sparse）的转换，同时保持模型性能与更新连续性，并满足工业系统的服务预算。

Method: Inherit4Rec 是一个参数继承框架，包含两种路径：Dense-to-Dense（D2D）增长与 Dense-to-Sparse（D2S）转换。D2D 路径通过混合增长策略扩展稠密模型，并采用非对称训练：在扩展时保持前向函数不变，从而避免性能突降，同时维持参数更新在扩容前后的连续性。D2S 路径将稠密模型转换为 SMoE 网络：首先基于共激活（co-activation）模式进行专家划分，使经常共同激活的神经元/参数归入同一专家，随后引入负载均衡损失以促进专家激活均衡，在保留稠密模型能力的同时提升计算效率。训练目标为推荐系统预测目标（未详细披露具体目标函数形式），推理时稀疏化后的 SMoE 仅激活部分专家以降低计算量，满足服务预算。实验在 KuaiRand-1K 和工业短视频推荐数据集上验证，比较对象为继承基线，评价指标为各预测目标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测目标性能", "value": "未披露具体数值，仅报告在所有预测目标上持续优于所评估的继承基线", "setting": "KuaiRand-1K 数据集与工业短视频推荐数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Inherit4Rec-D2D：结合混合增长（hybrid growth）与非对称训练（asymmetric training），在扩容时保持前向函数并维持更新连续性。；Inherit4Rec-D2S：通过共激活感知划分（co-activation-aware partitioning）与负载均衡损失构建 SMoE 网络，在保留稠密模型能力的同时促进专家激活均衡。；一个统一支持稠密到稠密增长和稠密到稀疏转换的参数继承框架。

Abstract: Scaling model capacity has emerged as an effective approach to overcoming performance bottlenecks in industrial recommender systems. However, repeatedly training larger dense models from scratch demands substantial data and time, while their growing computation conflicts with the strict serving budgets of industrial systems. Parameter inheritance provides a promising route for both dense model growth and sparse conversion, yet existing methods are primarily designed for static corpora and can suffer sharp performance drops under dynamically evolving recommendation data. To address these challenges, we propose Inherit4Rec, a parameter-inheritance framework that supports both Dense-to-Dense (D2D) growth and Dense-to-Sparse (D2S) conversion. Inherit4Rec-D2D combines hybrid growth with asymmetric training to preserve the forward function at expansion and maintain update continuity. Inherit4Rec-D2S constructs SMoE networks through co-activation-aware partitioning and a load-balancing loss, preserving dense-model capabilities while promoting balanced expert activation. Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and an industrial short-video recommendation dataset show that both transformations consistently outperform the evaluated inheritance baselines across all prediction objectives. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Inherit4Rec for continual capacity expansion and computation-efficient sparse conversion in industrial recommender systems.

</details>


### [11] [Per-Query Gating of LLM Rerankers for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22880)
*Bacellar, Andre*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多跳检索中LLM重排器的按查询门控，在LLM调用前利用两个检索列表的27个统计特征和小查询嵌入PCA来决定是否跳过重排，并配备可执行回退。在2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue和HotpotQA的9个数据集-K单元格上，门控在平均损失1.2 pp覆盖的情况下跳过51%的调用，但有害跳过仍然存在；校准驱动的预算规则可实现42%跳过和-0.8 pp损失，但实际危害常超预算。论文还纠正了早期版本中有误的“无损节省”结论。面向检索系统工程师，该工作提供了如何在检索管线中权衡重排开销与覆盖的方法和诚实的失败分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM重排器在多跳检索中能提升最终跳top-K覆盖，但带来每个1,000查询约$0.2-0.3的成本和约1秒尾部延迟；论文研究能否用一个在LLM调用前即可计算特征的按查询门控（per-query gate）来跳过无益重排调用，同时保持覆盖并量化有害跳过。

Method: 任务背景：多跳检索管线（图增强稠密检索，类似HippoRAG2）先产生两路检索列表，然后可选地用LLM重排器提升最终跳的top-K覆盖。LLM重排带来的成本约为每1,000查询$0.2-0.3，尾部延迟增加约1秒。论文提出按查询门控，在LLM调用前决定是否执行重排，若跳过则使用一个可执行回退（fallback）直接输出结果。门控输入特征为：从两个预重排检索列表中抽取的27个分值和词法统计量（例如列表分数分布、词汇重叠等），以及一个小型查询嵌入的PCA成分；这些特征不含任何LLM调用后的信息。门控模型在训练折内进行全部选择：特征构造、回退策略、阈值选择均在训练折内完成，然后一次性应用于留出查询。训练目标为预测该查询是否存在重排提升的潜势（或危害概率），第一种规则直接学习是否跳过；第二种规则先估计“如果跳过则有害”（重排本可找到目标而回退未找到）的概率，使用Platt校准（校准前ECE 0.094，校准后0.025），然后根据训练折上预设的期望有害跳过率预算（如1 pp或0.5 pp）逐单元格设置阈值，使得预期有害跳过率不超过预算。推理时，对每个查询提取门控特征，计算是否跳过：若跳过则走回退结果，否则调用LLM重排器，最终输出top-K。评估时分别报告聚合最终跳top-K覆盖（LastHop@K）、有害跳过次数/有益跳过次数，并与随机门控在同跳过率下对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LastHop@K", "value": "在9个(数据集,K)单元格中跳过51%调用，平均留出损失1.2 pp；4个单元格达到预注册1 pp规则", "setting": "2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue、HotpotQA上的多跳检索，基于HippoRAG2图增强稠密管线，按查询门控与可执行回退，留出查询评估"}, {"metric": "有害跳过/有益跳过", "value": "有害跳过出现在8个单元格，总计190次有害对136次有益", "setting": "同上，按查询门控的留出评估"}, {"metric": "随机门控对比", "value": "在相同跳过率下，随机门控在高提升单元格损失2到11 pp", "setting": "高提升单元格，与提出的门控对比"}, {"metric": "预算规则（1 pp预算）", "value": "门控跳过42%，覆盖损失-0.8 pp，66次有害跳过，6个单元格在1 pp内；但6个单元格实际危害超过承诺，均值1.45 vs 0.83 pp", "setting": "基于Platt校准危害概率、预设期望有害跳过率预算的阈值规则"}, {"metric": "预算规则（0.5 pp预算）", "value": "实际实现约1 pp", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "校准与判别性", "value": "校准后ECE 0.025，校准前0.094；AUC范围0.16到0.70", "setting": "Platt校准危害概率"}, {"metric": "重排器最终跳top-K覆盖", "value": "在9个(dataset,K)单元格中有7个提升，最高+34.8 pp", "setting": "与图增强稠密管线HippoRAG2对比，使用LLM重排器"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出只用LLM调用前特征的按查询门控：27个分值与词法统计（来自两个检索列表）加一个小查询嵌入的PCA，并配有可执行回退（fallback）。；所有选择（包括回退与阈值）都在训练折内完成并一次应用于留出查询，避免选择乐观性。；将有害跳过（重排本可找到目标而回退未找到）与聚合覆盖并报，强调风险指标。；第二种阈值规则基于Platt校准的危害概率，并在预设的有害跳过率预算下逐单元格设置阈值；报告校准前后ECE与AUC，并量化选择乐观性。；记录并纠正早期版本中基于oracle回退与错误MuSiQue目标的虚假73%“无损”节省。

Abstract: LLM rerankers add of the order of \$0.2-0.3 per 1,000 queries and about a second of tail latency on top of a graph-augmented dense pipeline such as HippoRAG2, and on three multi-hop benchmarks they improve final-hop top-K coverage on seven of nine (dataset, K) cells, by up to +34.8 pp. We ask whether a learned per-query gate can skip the reranker where it will not help, using only features available before the LLM call (27 score and lexical statistics of the two retrieval lists plus a PCA of a small query embedding) with an executable fallback. Every choice, including the fallback and the threshold, is made inside the training fold and applied once to held-out queries, and harmful skips (the rerank would have found the target, the fallback did not) are reported next to the aggregate coverage. Across nine cells on 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue and HotpotQA the gate skips 51% of calls at an average held-out LastHop@K cost of 1.2 pp; four cells meet a pre-registered 1 pp rule, harmful skips occur in eight (190 harmful against 136 beneficial), and a random gate at the same skip rate loses 2 to 11 pp on the high-lift cells. A second rule sets each cell's threshold from a pre-specified budget on the expected harmful-skip rate over Platt-calibrated harm probabilities (ECE 0.025 after calibration, 0.094 before): at a 1 pp budget the gate skips 42% at -0.8 pp with 66 harmful skips and six cells within 1 pp, but realised harm exceeds the promise in six cells (mean 1.45 vs 0.83 pp), a selection optimism we quantify; a 0.5 pp budget realises about 1 pp. The harm probabilities are calibrated but barely discriminative (AUC 0.16 to 0.70). An earlier version reported 73% "lossless" savings; that figure rested on an oracle fallback and a wrong MuSiQue target, and we document both.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过参数化的稠密-稀疏分数/排名融合策略，在混合检索中有效结合稠密与稀疏信号，并确定可迁移的调参范围。

Method: 该方法是一个参数化混合排序器，输入为同一查询由稠密嵌入模型（BGE）生成的稠密候选列表和由稀疏检索器（词法检索）生成的稀疏候选列表。两个列表通过显式参数向量进行融合：参数 α 控制稠密列表的先验权重，λ 控制最终分数中原始分数与排名分数的混合比例，κ 为 RRF 平滑参数。可选的列表几何系数允许 α 根据每个查询的列表几何特征动态调整，从而移动 α 值。此外，一个路由 margin τ 可决定是否完全关闭稀疏搜索。融合后的分数用于排序并输出最终结果。训练过程是在 SciFact 训练集（809 个查询）上对参数范围进行网格搜索，选定参数后冻结并在测试集（300 个查询）上评估。推理时直接使用冻结的参数进行融合打分，无需额外模型训练。该架构旨在通过少量可解释参数显式控制稠密与稀疏信号的相对贡献，避免启发式等权融合的局限。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.753", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，参数字典 α=0.8, λ=0.75, κ=20"}, {"metric": "recall@10", "value": "0.889", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，参数字典 α=0.8, λ=0.75, κ=20"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.742", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，稠密 BGE 基线"}, {"metric": "recall@10", "value": "0.871", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，稠密 BGE 基线"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "0.707", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，等权 RRF 基线"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "+0.0006", "setting": "SciFact 测试集，列表条件 α 相比固定 α 的增益"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10 macro-average", "value": "0.479", "setting": "九 zip BEIR 宏平均，等权 RRF 融合相同模型"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10 macro-average", "value": "0.519", "setting": "九 zip BEIR 宏平均，稠密 BGE"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10 unit-mean", "value": "0.467", "setting": "20 个 BEIR 索引单元，重复 train-then-freeze sweep 的均值"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10 unit-mean", "value": "0.462", "setting": "20 个 BEIR 索引单元，稠密 BGE 基线均值"}, {"metric": "nDCG@10 unit-mean", "value": "0.420", "setting": "20 个 BEIR 索引单元，等权 RRF 基线均值"}], "online_gains": []}

Abstract: We study a parameterized hybrid ranker that fuses a dense embedding list and a sparse lexical list. The method has a small, explicit parameter vector: a dense prior $\alpha \in [0,1]$, a score-versus-rank mix $\lambda \in [0,1]$, an RRF smoothing parameter $\kappa > 0$, optional list-geometry coefficients that move $\alpha$ per query, and a router margin $\tau$ that can turn sparse search off. We grid-search those ranges on SciFact train (809 queries) and freeze the chosen values on SciFact test (300). The tuned rank-score mix ($\alpha = 0.8$, $\lambda = 0.75$, $\kappa = 20$) reaches 0.753 nDCG@10 and 0.889 recall@10, outperforming dense BGE (0.742 / 0.871) and equal-weight RRF (0.707 nDCG@10) on that test split. A list-conditioned $\alpha$ adds +0.0006 nDCG; a sparse-off router is rejected by the same train split (any $\tau$ that skipped approximately 50% of queries lost nDCG). These coefficients are dataset-specific. Equal RRF with the same models does not beat dense on a nine-zip BEIR macro-average (0.479 vs. 0.519 nDCG@10). Repeating the same train-then-freeze sweep independently on all 20 indexed units beats equal RRF on 20/20 and dense on 16/20 (unit-mean nDCG@10 0.467 vs. 0.462 dense vs. 0.420 RRF). Other corpora should reuse the ranges, not a copy of the SciFact point.

</details>

*Guo, Chaoran,Tong, Ding,Lee, Ting-Po,Chen, Scarlet*

Method: 论文提出一个通用评估框架，将推荐系统可观测性视为反事实测量问题。框架包含三个核心测量方法论：偏差减少（bias reduction）、相对性（relativity）和增量性（incrementality）。这些方法适用于单阶段和级联推荐系统。框架从单一测量基础出发，同时服务于内容创作者和模型开发者两类受众。通过反事实估计，可以衡量在去除特定内容项或模型决策后的系统行为和用户互动变化，从而分离内容质量、模型行为、展示偏差和受众触达的影响。该框架已在Netflix多个推荐系统的生产部署中验证有效性。

Conclusion: 将推荐系统可观测性形式化为反事实测量问题：估计在缺少特定内容项或模型决策时，推荐系统会如何表现以及会产生什么互动。；提出三个以利益相关者为中心的可观测性原则，分别面向内容创作者和模型开发者。；提出涵盖偏差减少、相对性和增量性的测量方法论，适用于单阶段和级联推荐系统，并从单一测量基础服务两类受众。

Abstract: Understanding the performance of large-scale recommender systems remains an underexplored challenge, especially for content creators and model developers. The raw engagement signals available to them, such as views and clicks, conflate content quality, model behavior, presentation bias, and audience reach, making it hard to attribute outcomes to the right cause. In this work, we present a general evaluation framework that enhances observability across multiple recommender systems at Netflix and demonstrate its effectiveness through several production deployments. The framework treats recommender-system observability as a counterfactual measurement problem: estimating what the recommender would have done, and what engagement would have followed, in the absence of a specific content item or model decision. We articulate three stakeholder-centered observability principles for content creators and model developers, and propose measurement methodologies covering bias reduction, relativity, and incrementality, applicable to both single-stage and cascading recommender systems and serving both audiences from a single measurement foundation.

### [14] [Guiding the coarse levels of semantic IDs makes the fine levels learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22227)
*Wang, Bin,Zhang, Zhengyu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Guided SID 提出通过确定性监督索引分配强制粗粒度 RQ-VAE 层级编码文本接地且任务相关的预定义属性，并采用 trie-merge 处理高基数属性，使 Semantic ID 对下游 LLM 更可读且任务相关。匹配 A/B 实验显示 recall@1 提升 1.36x、recall@10 提升 1.39x、MRR 从 0.0260 升至 0.0355，属性预测率提升 4.2x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Semantic ID 的 tokenizer 独立训练以重建 item embedding，其编码与下游 LLM 及最终推荐任务不对齐，需要额外对齐工作，且恢复的语义可能并非任务所需。

Method: Guided SID 基于 RQ-VAE 构建 Semantic ID。输入为 item embedding，由 RQ-VAE 编码器生成多层残差量化码本序列。核心改动：粗粒度层级不再使用最近邻码本选择，而是通过确定性监督索引分配，强制编码预定义的、文本接地且任务相关的类别属性（如商品类目）。为处理高基数或集合值属性，引入 trie-merge 构造，将属性映射到固定码本预算，并保持合并桶语义连贯。码本仍接收重建梯度以保持可学习性。下游 LLM 以生成的 Semantic ID 序列为条件进行自回归生成，用于推荐召回。推理时，模型自回归生成 Semantic ID 序列，再映射回候选 item。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall@1", "value": "1.36x", "setting": "匹配端到端 A/B，仅 SID 编码不同"}, {"metric": "recall@10", "value": "1.39x", "setting": "匹配端到端 A/B，仅 SID 编码不同"}, {"metric": "MRR", "value": "0.0260 -> 0.0355", "setting": "匹配端到端 A/B，仅 SID 编码不同"}, {"metric": "预定义属性预测率", "value": "4.2x", "setting": "匹配端到端 A/B，仅 SID 编码不同"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Guided SID：强制粗粒度 RQ-VAE 层级通过确定性监督索引分配编码预定义的、文本接地且任务相关的类别属性，覆盖最近邻选择，同时保留码本可学习性（仍接收重建梯度）。；Trie-merge 构造：将任意高基数或集合值属性映射到固定码本预算，同时保持合并桶的语义连贯性。；匹配的端到端 A/B 实验（仅 SID 编码不同）验证引导无 intrinsic 代价，碰撞与重建匹配或优于基线。

Abstract: Generative retrieval represents each item by a short Semantic ID and casts recommendation as autoregressive generation of that sequence. Because the tokenizer is trained independently to reconstruct an item embedding, its codes are aligned with neither the downstream LLM nor the end task. Nearly every SID system therefore spends extra effort to bridge this gap--alignment corpora, reasoning/RL, or per-token encoders to make codes legible, or learned tokenizer supervision to make them task-aware--yet the recovered meaning is content-derived and may not be the meaning the task needs. We introduce Guided SID, which instead makes the levels that matter most meaningful by construction: we force the coarse RQ-VAE levels to encode a predefined categorical attribute--chosen to be text-grounded (hence legible to the LLM) and task-relevant--by deterministic supervised index assignment (overriding nearest-neighbor selection with the attribute label) while keeping the codebooks learnable (they still receive reconstruction gradients). A trie-merge construction maps any high-cardinality or set-valued attribute onto the fixed code budget while keeping merged buckets semantically coherent. Guiding costs nothing intrinsically: collision and reconstruction match or beat the vanilla baseline despite pinning the coarse level. In a matched end-to-end A/B differing only in the SID encoding, the guided retriever improves recall@k at every list length we measure (1.36x at k=1, 1.39x at k=10), raises mean reciprocal rank from 0.0260 to 0.0355, and predicts the pre-defined attribute 4.2x more often.

</details>

### [15] [UniK: Universal Knowledge Perception for Digital and Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23971)
*Desai, Nirmit,Sawarkar, Kunal,Mahakali, Aditya,Lee, Dongkon,Park, Kevin,Song, Eric*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniK提出统一知识感知平台，通过Polymath Retrieval多索引融合检索在无需微调的情况下支持数字AI与物理AI的多模态知识生命周期，结合70B开源模型在政府、医疗、化学等领域声称匹配或超越远大规模前沿专有LLM，但缺乏公司、团队与在线部署证据，实验结果未明确测试集细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、异构、多模态的私有知识难以被现有AI基础设施可靠且高效地访问，数字AI与物理AI均受制于这一知识瓶颈。

Method: UniK是一个覆盖知识全生命周期的平台。输入包括企业私有语料中的多种模态：富文本、视频、分子数据、传感器遥测等。流程分为五个阶段：1) 摄入：将原始异构数据统一接入；2) 增强：对数据进行自动富化，生成可检索的表示，具体增强方法未完整披露；3) 索引：构建多个模态/任务相关的索引；4) 检索：Polymath Retrieval进行多索引融合检索，无需任务特定微调，依据查询从多个索引中获取相关知识；5) 持续评估：对检索与下游生成效果进行持续监控。检索结果随后条件化一个70B开源大模型完成RAG生成。训练目标或优化细节未披露。推理时，给定查询，系统并行查询多个增强索引，融合召回结果供LLM生成答案。该架构旨在解决异构知识难以可靠检索的问题，并声称相同设计可直接支持物理AI世界模型的数据索引与检索。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "RAG accuracy", "value": "76%", "setting": "政府开放数据，与GPT-5的47%对比"}, {"metric": "Medical QA accuracy", "value": "77.9%", "setting": "医学问答，无需微调"}, {"metric": "Chemistry pipeline", "value": "top all open-source", "setting": "化学领域开源管道排名第一"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Universal Knowledge Perception (UniK) 统一知识生命周期平台：覆盖摄入、增强、索引、检索与持续评估。；Polymath Retrieval：在多模态自动增强索引上进行多索引融合检索，无需任务特定微调。；同一检索基础设施可直接迁移到物理AI世界模型训练的数据策展与检索挑战。

Abstract: Two transformative classes of AI systems are reshaping how organizations operate: \textit{digital AI}, which reasons over enterprise knowledge to power chatbots and agent workflows; and \textit{physical AI}, which learns to control robots and autonomous systems from video, gameplay, and sensor telemetry. Both face the same foundational bottleneck: raw knowledge at scale, spanning heterogeneous modalities, locked in private corpora that existing AI infrastructure cannot access reliably or efficiently. We propose \textit{Universal Knowledge Perception (UniK)} as a common platform for both classes, covering the full knowledge lifecycle (ingestion, enrichment, indexing, retrieval, and continuous evaluation) across modalities from rich text and video to molecular data and sensor telemetry. We present UniK, built on Polymath Retrieval (multi-index fusion over automatically enriched indices) with no task-specific fine-tuning. Across five digital AI domains (medical literature, open-domain QA, chemistry, legal video proceedings, and government open data) UniK combined with an open-source 70-billion-parameter model consistently matches or outperforms frontier proprietary LLMs that are orders of magnitude larger: 76\% RAG accuracy on government data versus 47\% for GPT-5; 77.9\% on medical QA without fine-tuning; topping all open-source chemistry pipelines. We show that the same infrastructure directly addresses the data curation, indexing, and retrieval challenges facing physical AI world model training, where the knowledge problem is harder but structurally identical.

</details>


### [16] [Explainable Recommendations at Scale: LLM Rationales for YouTube Music Artist Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23877)
*Liu, Xiao,Song, Yanwei,Ranganathan, Srivaths,Chen, Yuan,Feng, Zheyun,Steenburgh, Parker,Klingenhoefer, Jochen,Lasche, Nathan,Varady, Gergo,Steele, Tim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Google 在 YouTube Music 上提出解耦推荐架构，将 LLM 理由生成异步离线预计算，在线直接消费候选池与理由，规避实时 LLM 推理成本。在线 A/B 实验表明，附有解释理由的 LLM 推荐显著降低新内容信任门槛，在发现场景同时提升用户探索与整体参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐流媒体推荐中利用熟悉内容与探索新艺术家之间的权衡：用户有发现欲望但因不熟悉而犹豫；LLM 生成解释性理由可降低信任门槛，但实时推理成本过高，难以直接在线部署。

Method: 系统采用解耦架构。离线阶段：LLM 推理被隔离为异步离线任务，为每个用户预计算个性化的未发现艺术家候选池，并同时生成针对每个候选艺术家的自然语言推荐理由（rationale）。在线阶段：推荐服务直接消费预计算的候选池与理由，无需调用 LLM 实时推理，从而避免高推理成本与延迟瓶颈。在线服务将 LLM 支持的推荐与解释性理由一并展示在发现界面。训练目标与优化：论文以在线 A/B 实验为主要验证手段，评估探索指标与整体参与度；摘要未披露具体模型训练目标、微调方式或数据管线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "用户探索指标", "value": "统计显著提升", "setting": "YouTube Music 发现界面在线 A/B 实验"}, {"metric": "整体参与度指标", "value": "统计显著提升", "setting": "YouTube Music 发现界面在线 A/B 实验"}]}

Conclusion: 解耦式推荐架构：将 LLM 推理隔离为异步离线任务，预计算个性化未发现艺术家候选池及其解释理由。；LLM 生成自然语言推荐理由，与候选池一起离线预计算，在线服务时直接消费，避免实时 LLM 推理。；通过大规模在线 A/B 实验验证 LLM 理由推荐在探索与整体参与度上的提升。

Abstract: Modern music streaming platforms face a persistent tradeoff: exploiting familiar content versus driving the exploration of novel items. While users frequently desire discovery, they hesitate to select unknown artists over proven favorites. Providing transparent, natural language rationales that explain why an unexplored item is recommended lowers this barrier. However, while Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at this nuanced explainability, their real-time deployment is severely bottlenecked by prohibitive inference costs and computational overhead. In this paper, we present an industry case study of a decoupled recommendation architecture that successfully scales exploration without compromising latency. Our system isolates LLM inference asynchronously offline, pre-computing personalized candidate pools of undiscovered artists alongside tailored rationales. Large-scale online A/B experiments validate our design. We demonstrate that combining LLM-backed recommendations with these explanatory rationales significantly reduces the trust barrier for new content, yielding statistically significant improvements in both user exploration and overall engagement on the discovery surfaces.

</details>


### [17] [Harness-Zero: Harness Distillation via Agent-as-Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24974)
*Ye, Haoran,Lu, Yuxing,Dong, Haonan,Su, Zhaochen,Song, Guojie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Harness-Zero，通过 agent-as-harness 实现 harness 蒸馏：利用优化 harness 引导一个 harnessing agent 在目标 harness 动作空间内纠正学生响应，生成训练轨迹并微调模型。实验覆盖知识工作、工具使用和科学领域，显示在移除专用 harness 部署时，宏观平均任务成功率从 23.3% 提升至 44.3%，甚至超过保留该 harness 的 41.7%，并平均恢复 82.3% 的 harness 诱导行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在训练时利用针对领域或实例优化的智能体 harness（外部系统）来引导模型，把 harness 诱导的行为蒸馏进模型权重，使得在部署时切换到单一固定目标 harness 后仍能保留收益，即使两个 harness 的动作空间与可用信息不同。

Method: Harness-Zero 的端到端流程围绕两个 harness 之间的行为迁移展开。输入是基础模型的初始响应以及一个已针对特定领域或实例优化过的 harness（称为优化 harness）。该优化 harness 拥有自己的动作空间和可用信息，但无法直接作为目标 harness（部署时使用的固定 harness）的监督信号，因为两者的动作空间和信息维度不一致。Harness-Zero 引入一个引导智能体（harnessing agent）作为中介：它接收基础模型的响应和优化 harness 的引导信息，在目标 harness 的动作空间内对基础模型的响应进行执行前纠正，生成修正后的响应。这些修正后的响应随后在目标 harness 中执行，产生完整的交互轨迹。轨迹被用作训练演示，对基础模型进行微调。微调目标函数未在摘要中披露，但从描述可推断为基于轨迹的行为克隆或策略梯度类目标，使模型权重内化优化 harness 所诱导的行为。推理/部署时，仅使用目标 harness，不再需要优化 harness 或引导智能体；模型直接生成响应并交由目标 harness 执行。该架构解决的核心问题是：在 harness 动作空间异构的情况下，将训练时引导转化为可用的监督信号，并在部署时消除对专用 harness 的依赖。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro-average task success", "value": "23.3% -> 44.3%", "setting": "移除专用 harness 部署，Harness-Zero 微调后的基础模型"}, {"metric": "macro-average task success", "value": "44.3% vs 41.7%", "setting": "移除专用 harness（44.3%）对比保留专用 harness 部署（41.7%）"}, {"metric": "harness-induced behavior recovery", "value": "82.3% 平均恢复率", "setting": "三个领域、28 种模式"}, {"metric": "agent-as-harness vs code-as-harness", "value": "agent-as-harness 更优", "setting": "前沿 LLM 使用同一 evolved harness"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 agent harness distillation 问题设定：将训练时优化 harness 的收益转移到目标 harness 下部署的模型权重中。；提出 agent-as-harness 机制：由引导智能体在目标 harness 的动作空间内对学生的响应进行执行前纠正，将 harness 引导转化为可监督的训练演示。；通过在生成轨迹上微调，使模型内化 harness 诱导行为，从而在部署时可移除专用 harness。；跨知识工作、工具使用、科学三个领域的实验证明方法有效性，并给出行为恢复率度量。

Abstract: Agent harnesses, the external systems that mediate model-environment interaction, can substantially improve agent performance, but their gains remain tied to the harness at deployment. Because the best harness varies across domains, instances, and models, a general-purpose agent must either settle for a suboptimal shared harness or route among an ever-growing set of specialized ones. We therefore study agent harness distillation: using a domain- or instance-optimized harness as training-time guidance and transferring the behaviors it induces into model weights, so that its gains survive under a single fixed target harness. The challenge is that the two harnesses differ in action space and available information, so guidance from the optimized harness cannot serve directly as supervision for the target one. We introduce Harness-Zero, which enables harness distillation through agent-as-harness. Guided by the optimized harness, a harnessing agent corrects student responses before execution in the target harness's action space, turning harness guidance into training demonstrations. Fine-tuning on the resulting trajectories internalizes harness-induced behavior into the model, so the specialized harness can be removed at deployment. Our experiments spanning knowledge work, tool use, and science domains show that: (1) For frontier LLMs using the same evolved harness, agent-as-harness outperforms code-as-harness. (2) With the specialized harness removed at deployment, Harness-Zero improves the base model's macro-average task success from 23.3% to 44.3%, even exceeding the 41.7% it reaches with that harness still attached. (3) Harness-Zero recovers harness-induced behaviors absent from the base model, with 82.3% average recovery across 28 patterns in the three domains.

</details>


### [18] [GRUET: Quantifying Uncertainty of Agentic Reasoning-and-Acting Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24831)
*Liang, Shuang,Hu, Xin-Yu,Zhang, Shao-Qun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRUET 提出对 ReAct 式 LLM Agent 轨迹进行不确定性量化：把每轮推理的潜在分支空间建模为图并以图复杂度近似该轮推理不确定性，再用简单聚合策略得到整条轨迹的不确定性，用于选择性生成；在九种 LLM 与五个基准上用 AUROC、AUPRC、AUARC 评估有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct 式 LLM Agent 在多轮轨迹中表现出显著不确定性：相同任务产生发散轨迹，高不确定性轨迹常伴随不可理解行为，损害 agent 可信度；如何量化并聚合turn级推理不确定性与轨迹级不确定性以评估轨迹可信度。

Method: GRUET 面向 ReAct（Reasoning-and-Acting）式的 LLM Agent 轨迹。输入是单条多轮任务轨迹：每一轮中 LLM 交替产生推理链（reasoning chain）与任务相关动作（action）。方法分为两级。第一级为 turn 级推理不确定性量化：作者假设某一轮的 LLM 推理会表现为若干发散的推理链分支以及由这些分支引出的动作集合，因此把该轮潜在推理分支所张成的推理空间建模成一张图，用图的复杂度来近似推理空间复杂度，从而得到该轮的推理不确定性度量。第二级为轨迹级不确定性聚合：将各轮的 turn 级不确定性按简单聚合策略累积成整条轨迹的整体不确定性，作为该轨迹可信度的量化指标。该轨迹级分数用于选择性生成（selective generation）——即决定是否接受该轨迹的输出，从而在高不确定性时避免不可理解的行为。论文在九个 LLM 与五个基准上进行经验评估，以 AUROC、AUPRC、AUARC 衡量选择性生成性能。摘要未披露模型参数量、训练数据、硬件、延迟或吞吐等系统细节，也未说明具体图构造方式与聚合函数形式。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC / AUPRC / AUARC（选择性生成性能）", "value": "未提供具体数值（摘要仅报告在九种 LLM、五个基准上验证有效性）", "setting": "九个 LLM、五个基准上的经验评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 GRUET 方法，将 turn 级推理不确定性量化与轨迹级不确定性聚合结合；把潜在推理分支张成的推理空间建模为图，用图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度以量化推理不确定性；使用简单聚合策略将由 turn 级不确定性累积得到的整体轨迹不确定性进行量化，用于选择性生成

Abstract: Agents have attracted considerably increasing attention due to the power of executing both Reasoning and Acting (ReAct) in open and dynamic environments. The ReAct process typically exhibits a multi-turn trajectory in which one drives Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate both reasoning chains and task-specific actions in an interleaved manner. However, agents often suffer from significant uncertainty, where identical tasks yield divergent trajectories; trajectories with higher uncertainty often produce incomprehensible behaviors, severely undermining agent credibility. This work conjectures that such trajectory-level uncertainty frequently stems from cumulative turn-level reasoning uncertainty induced by LLMs; the latter often exhibits a collection of branches of divergent reasoning chains and their resulting actions. Built upon this, we present the Graph-based Reasoning UncErtainty in Trajectories (GRUET) method for the uncertainty quantification of ReAct, comprising turn-level reasoning uncertainty quantification and trajectory-level uncertainty aggregation; the former precisely quantifies reasoning uncertainty via modeling the reasoning space spanned by potential reasoning branches as a graph and then approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity, while the latter employs simple aggregation strategies for quantifying the overall trajectory credibility. Empirical evaluations across nine LLMs and five benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our proposed GRUET in terms of selective generation performance, measured by AUROC, AUPRC, and AUARC.

</details>


### [19] [World State Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24744)
*Jeong, Sungheon,Yun, Sanggeon,Masukawa, Ryozo,Alimohamadi, Haleh,Imani, Mahdi,Imani, Mohsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WSG将语言智能体的规划转化为世界可检查的状态序列，利用226K条合成世界轨迹训练模型，使计划能根据运行时失败动态调整，在7个基准上显著提升成功率，达到接近专有模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在规划时，计划未与世界规则绑定，导致步骤被拒绝后无法有效修复，浪费尝试预算。

Method: 论文提出World State Generator (WSG)，该模型将计划表示为世界状态序列，而非自然语言步骤。系统构建了7个领域的合成世界，每个世界有程序强制执行的规则和可达目标验证。智能体在这些世界中运行，产生约226K条轨迹，每条轨迹包含验证过的失败状态及其修复动作（修复后状态被世界认证）。WSG在这些轨迹上训练，学习在运行时当某个状态失败时，将该失败视为世界给出的规则，并重写剩余状态以符合该规则。推理时，WSG生成初始状态序列，执行时若遇到失败则根据失败信息调整后续状态，使计划持续适应世界约束。训练目标是最小化重写状态与修复后认证状态之间的差异，最终在7个公开基准上提升端到端成功率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将计划表达为世界可检查的状态序列，而非自然语言描述。；构建合成世界（7个领域）自动提取训练数据，包含约226K条带验证失败与修复的轨迹。；训练世界状态生成器（WSG），在运行时根据失败状态重写剩余计划状态，使计划逐步适应世界约束。

Abstract: Language agents solve complex tasks through plans and actions. A single step the world refuses puts the goal out of reach, and what the agent does next decides the task. Prompted planners fail at exactly this point, rewriting the refused step in new words, meeting the same refusal, and burning the attempt budget without moving. They fail because the plan was never tied to the world, so a refusal has nothing in the plan to attach to. A world is where a task runs, and it has its own rules, its own admissible actions, and its own constraints. We build synthetic worlds across 7 domains and extract training data from them. A program enforces each world's rules and grades its goal, and every world is admitted only if its goal is reachable from its initial state. Agents run inside and leave verified failures paired with repairs that carried the run to a state the world certified, a record of about 226K trajectories. On this record we train the World State Generator, a model that writes a plan as checkable states of the world and keeps that plan aligned with the world it runs in. That alignment is what a plan written in language lacks, since the world it runs in has physical limits, logical dependencies, and required orders the language never states, and the plan encounters these rules only when a state fails. WSG takes that failure as the rule the world has stated and rewrites the remaining states to obey it, so the plan bends to the world as the run goes on. Across 7 public benchmarks, WSG raises end-to-end success for two open models near 30B parameters over prompting and brings to the level of proprietary model.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Endpoint Performance: Process-Level Evaluation of Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24663)
*Lin, Hongqiang,Liu, Chao,Bai, Xiaofan,Jin, Xuan,Li, Yuhong,Zheng, Nenggan,Cao, Xipeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoPathBench是一个过程级评估基准，用于追踪自演化LLM代理在工件迭代更新过程中目标能力的出现与变化。它固定基础模型与工具，在连续检查点冻结演化工件并在留出情节上评估泛化、保持和规则适应三类能力。实验表明相似任务增益在分布偏移下常被削弱，保持损失集中于少数路径，且没有方法实现可靠规则适应；候选工件虽有显著留出增益，但选择更新未能兑现潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化代理通过交互反馈生成持久化工件（如记忆、技能）并迭代更新，仅看端点性能无法判断目标能力何时出现、后续更新是否增强/保持/削弱该能力，因此需要过程级能力评估。

Method: EvoPathBench的架构围绕工件级自演化过程展开：输入是经验流与公开交易数据构造的校准轨迹；基础模型和工具集被固定，以减少外部变化对能力评估的干扰。在演化过程中，代理将交互反馈转化为持久化工件（如记忆或技能），这些工件在连续检查点被冻结并保存。评估时，在每个检查点使用冻结的工件集在留出情节上测试目标能力，从而追踪同一能力随演化路径的变化。基准定义三类能力：泛化到未见任务、无关学习后的保持、以及基于新证据的规则适应。通过比较不同检查点的能力表现，可识别目标能力何时出现以及后续更新对其增强、保持或削弱的效果。实验还比较生成候选工件与最终选择更新的效果，发现候选工件具有较高留出增益但选择机制未能兑现该潜力，由此将候选评估与选择作为关键改进目标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出EvoPathBench基准，在工件级自演化过程中跟踪单个能力，而非仅评估最终性能。；固定基础模型与工具，在连续检查点冻结演化工件，并在留出情节上评估目标能力，隔离演化路径的影响。；使用公开交易数据和校准轨迹，评估泛化到未见任务、无关学习后的保持、以及规则适应三种能力。；揭示自演化过程的关键现象：相似未见任务上的增益在分布偏移下常被削弱，保持损失集中在少数演化路径，且没有方法实现可靠的规则适应。；发现自演化能生成具有显著留出增益的候选工件，但所选更新持续未能实现该潜力，将候选评估与选择定位为改进关键。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents convert interaction feedback into persistent artifacts, such as memories or skills, which in turn guide subsequent decisions. As these artifacts are iteratively updated throughout an experience stream, the capabilities they support may evolve. Consequently, endpoint performance alone offers an incomplete view of self-evolution. Process-level evaluation is therefore essential to identify when a target capability emerges and whether later updates strengthen, preserve, or weaken it. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{EvoPathBench}, a benchmark that tracks individual capabilities during artifact-level self-evolution. EvoPathBench fixes the base model, tools, freezes evolving artifacts at successive checkpoints, and evaluates the target capability on held-out episodes. This benchmark evaluates agent self-evolution using public trading data and calibrated trajectories. It tests three capabilities: generalization to unseen tasks, retention after unrelated learning, and rule adaptation to new evidence. Experimental results show that gains on similar unseen tasks often weaken under distribution shift, retention losses are concentrated in a minority of evolution paths, and no method achieves reliable rule adaptation. Moreover, while self-evolution enables agents to generate candidate artifacts with substantial held-out gains, the selected updates consistently fall short of realizing this potential. Together, these findings establish capability-level process evaluation as a foundation for analyzing self-evolution, identifying candidate evaluation and selection as key targets for improvement.

</details>


### [21] [DUMA-Bench: A Dual-Control Multi-Agent Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agent Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24662)
*Aleksandrov, Ivan,Kochnev, German,Sadiekh, Sabrina,Rogoza, Yaroslav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DUMA-Bench是首个针对LLM智能体双控制交互的安全评测基准，扩展τ²-bench并覆盖八类漏洞。实验表明双控制交互使攻击成功率从26.9%升至41.1%，揭示智能体安全是模型、用户与环境交互的涌现属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全评估大多假设用户被动且环境静态，忽略了智能体与用户交互中双方的动态控制对安全性的影响。

Method: DUMA-Bench基于τ²-bench扩展，构建对抗环境以模拟智能体与用户均可影响共享环境状态的双控制交互。环境包含八种漏洞类别的注入点（如RAG投毒、跨智能体操纵、不安全输出处理）。评测协议定义多种用户行为模式，并在八个领域上对14个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、Qwen、Z.ai）进行测试。通过测量攻击成功率评估模型在交互动态下的安全表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "攻击成功率", "value": "26.9%", "setting": "静态控制基线"}, {"metric": "攻击成功率", "value": "41.1%", "setting": "双控制交互设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出DUMA-Bench，一个双控制交互下的多智能体安全评测基准与协议，扩展τ²-bench并加入对抗环境。；覆盖八类漏洞，包括RAG投毒、跨智能体操纵、不安全输出处理等。；设计多种用户行为模式，并在八个领域评测14个来自五个模型家族的模型。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly operate in environments where they interact with users, tools, and external systems. Yet most security evaluations assume passive users and static control, ignoring the interactive dynamics that shape real agent behavior. We introduce \textbf{DUMA-Bench}, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for measuring agent security under \emph{dual-control} interaction, where both the agent and the user can influence the shared environment state. DUMA-Bench extends $\tau^2$-bench ~\cite{barres2025tau} with adversarial environments covering eight vulnerability classes, including RAG poisoning, cross-agent manipulation, and unsafe output handling. We evaluate \textbf{14 models from five model families} (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, Qwen, and Z.ai) across eight domains and multiple user-behavior regimes. Across our experiments, introducing dual-control interaction increases the attack success rate from \textbf{26.9\%} to \textbf{41.1\%}. These results show that agent security is not solely a property of the model but emerges from the interaction between the model, the user, and the environment. DUMA-Bench provides a missing evaluation layer for studying security in realistic agent deployments.

</details>

*Cho, Hyunjoong,Jang, Jinhyeok*

TL;DR: SERA提出一种任务自适应的谱模型合并方法，根据每个任务向量的奇异值能量比例分配秩，避免均匀秩分配的次优性。在标准视觉模型合并协议下，SERA在不增加总秩预算的前提下提升多任务合并性能，并分析了谱集中度与自适应秩分配效果的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱模型合并方法为所有任务分配相同的秩容量，忽略了不同任务向量谱复杂度的异质性，导致共享合并空间的次优使用。

Method: SERA以多个从同一预训练模型微调得到的任务模型为输入，提取每个任务相对于预训练模型的更新量（任务向量）。对每个任务向量进行奇异值分解（SVD），得到奇异值谱，并依据奇异值能量结构计算该任务所需的比例秩。能量更集中（谱更复杂或任务更孤立）的任务获得更多秩（更多奇异方向），能量紧凑的任务则分配较少秩，同时所有任务的总秩预算与现有谱合并方法保持一致。分配后的低秩任务向量通过基于SVD的合并方法（如谱合并框架）组合，生成一个多任务模型。训练目标不涉及额外的联合训练，完全在合并阶段通过秩分配优化最终多任务性能。推理时使用合并后的多任务模型进行标准前向传播，无需任务特定分支或额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 提出SERA（Spectral Energy-proportional Rank Allocation），一种基于任务向量奇异值能量结构进行任务自适应秩分配的策略。；将SVD-based模型合并从均匀容量合并扩展到任务相关容量分配，在保持总秩预算不变的情况下提升多任务合并性能。；分析任务级谱集中度与自适应秩分配效果的关系，解释SERA何时及为何有效。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple fine-tuned models derived from a common pretrained model into a single multi-task model without additional joint training. Recent spectral merging methods improve over simple weight averaging by exploiting low-rank structures of task-specific updates, but they commonly assign the same rank capacity to every task. This uniform allocation ignores that task vectors can have heterogeneous spectral complexity, causing the shared merging space to be used suboptimally. In this paper, we propose Spectral Energy-proportional Rank Allocation (SERA), a simple task-adaptive strategy that allocates ranks according to the singular-value energy structure of each task vector. By assigning richer spectral capacity to complex or isolated tasks and fewer directions to compact tasks, SERA extends SVD-based model merging from uniform-capacity merging to task-dependent capacity allocation. Experiments under standard vision model merging protocols show that SERA improves multi-task merging performance while preserving the same total rank budget as existing spectral merging methods. Further analysis demonstrates that task-level spectral concentration is closely related to the per-task effect of adaptive rank allocation, providing insight into when and why SERA is effective.

</details>


### [23] [VLM-in-Sandbox: Visual Workspaces for Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24362)
*Yang, Hexiong,Chen, Mingrui,Cao, Jie,He, Ran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLM-in-Sandbox 提出训练无关的 Visual Workspace，通过图像账本和有界活跃视觉上下文管理多模态智能体产生的图像证据，在七个基准、四种基础 VLM 上取得最高平均准确率，并在 vLLM 前缀缓存下降低 token 消耗与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理产生的中间图像证据（裁剪、掩码、叠加、放大区域、分析渲染）需要在多模态上下文中持续可寻址，同时避免上下文无界增长。

Method: VLM-in-Sandbox 是一个训练无关的框架，用于在受控计算机环境中进行智能体多模态推理。其核心组件是 Visual Workspace，负责管理视觉证据的状态。流程如下：沙箱工具执行视觉证据生成（生成裁剪、掩码、叠加、放大区域、分析渲染等图像产物）；这些产物被注册到图像账本中；系统维护一个有界的活跃视觉上下文，而不是让所有图像产物无限累积在多模态上下文中；模型可以显式地将选定的证据从账本提升到活跃上下文，以便后续检查。这样，视觉证据的生成（由沙箱工具执行）与视觉证据的管理（由 Visual Workspace 执行）被分离。训练目标：无，该框架是训练无关的。推理路径：模型基于当前活跃视觉上下文进行推理，按需提升证据，生成的视觉产物被记录并可有界保留。本地 vLLM 研究确认，更小的请求负载配合前缀缓存减少了未缓存 token，并改善了首 token 时间与端到端延迟。

Abstract: Sandboxed computer environments support multi-step reasoning with tools, executable programs, and persistent files, yet their extension from language models to vision-language models (VLMs) introduces a distinct state-management problem. Visual reasoning produces intermediate image-valued evidence---crops, masks, overlays, zoomed regions, and analytic renderings---that must remain addressable without accumulating unboundedly in multimodal context. We introduce VLM-in-Sandbox, a training-free framework for agentic multimodal reasoning in controlled computer environments. Its Visual Workspace registers generated artifacts in an image ledger, maintains a bounded active visual context, and lets the model explicitly promote selected evidence for subsequent inspection. This separates visual evidence generation, performed by sandbox tools, from visual evidence management. Across seven benchmarks and four base VLMs, VLM-in-Sandbox achieves the highest sample-weighted average accuracy among Vanilla VLM, Append-only Sandbox, and the proposed method. A compiler-matched $2\times2$ study on 1,260 examples further separates model-directed visibility from bounded retention: VLM-in-Sandbox reaches 66.27% accuracy with 18.6% fewer total tokens than the automatic, retain-all control. Over all 6,350 submitted GPT-4.1-mini examples, it produces 302 rescues and 142 regressions relative to Original Append-only. A local vLLM study with prefix caching confirms that the smaller request workload also reduces uncached tokens, time to first token, and end-to-end latency. These results identify explicit visual evidence state as a central abstraction for sandboxed VLM agents.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现few-shot演示能促使LLM在上下文中构建并使用编码图结构的世界表征，线性探针和干预实验表明该表征在隐藏状态中发生近正交偏移且更具预测效用，并在ARC-AGI、web agent和Othello上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体难以从上下文中学习并使用潜在的世界表征（如数据生成背后的图结构）进行下游预测；本文研究few-shot演示如何改变LLM内部世界表征的构建和使用。

Method: 论文使用标准LLM作为智能体，输入为图追踪任务中的观测序列（上下文）。模型需在上下文中推循生成数据背后的图结构，并利用该潜在状态空间进行后续预测。作者用线性探针从隐藏状态中提取低维世界表征，发现few-shot演示（每个演示来自不同世界）使该表征发生近正交方向偏移，且干预该子空间比干预其他子空间更显著地损害性能，表明模型增加了对该表征的预测依赖。方法不涉及新模型架构或训练，而是通过探针与干预分析内部机制，并在ARC-AGI-1&2、web agent和Othello上验证泛化。

Conclusion: 将图追踪任务扩展到few-shot设置，演示来自不同世界且图拓扑可同可异，显著提升预测性能。；通过线性探针发现隐藏状态中存在低维世界表征编码图信息。；发现few-shot演示使世界表征发生近正交偏移，并增加其预测效用。；对世界表征子空间的干预比其它子空间干预更显著地损害性能，证明其因果作用。；在ARC-AGI-1&2、web agent任务和Othello上验证few-shot演示的泛化增益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), when acting as agents, are expected to take observed data in context, infer the latent state space underlying the world, and leverage it for downstream prediction. However, prior work demonstrated that LLMs struggle to use representations learned in context on a graph tracking task, where the model needs to construct a representation of the graph governing data generation process and use it for subsequent predictions. In this paper, we show that extending this to few-shot settings, where each demonstration is generated from a different world with either the same or different graph topologies, enhances its prediction on 6 models from 4 model families. To understand this improvement, we linearly probe a low-dimensional world representation that encodes graph information in the hidden states. Notably, we find that few-shot demonstrations relocate the world representation and increase its predictive use. Specifically, for each model, these world representations shift in directions nearly orthogonal to their original subspace, and interventions on these representations selectively impair performance more than interventions on other subspaces. Consistent with this insight, we show that few-shot demonstrations with observations from different worlds improve performance on ARC-AGI-1&2, web agent tasks, and Othello. Our findings elucidate the role and internal mechanisms of few-shot demonstrations in in-context world modeling. More broadly, our work advances our understanding of how LLM agents learn from in-context observations and provides implications for their further improvement.

</details>


### [25] [LADDER: Graph-Guided Diffusion Language Models for Efficient Multi-Hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24346)
*Zhang, Senlei,Luo, Linhao,Zhang, Qian-Wen,An, Siyu,Dong, Junnan,Zhang, Shuhao,Sun, Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LADDER将扩散语言模型的并行解码与GraphRAG结合，提出事件驱动自时钟检索与不完整查询图传播模块，在多跳QA上平均EM从39.6%升至45.2%，延迟降低4.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG框架依赖自回归语言模型，顺序生成严重限制推理效率；在离散扩散空间加速GraphRAG面临部分去噪草稿动态不确定导致图接地困难，以及原始去噪状态噪声大导致同步图检索与多跳聚合计算代价过高两大挑战。

Method: LADDER将扩散语言模型（DLM）的连续精炼并行解码与图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）结合，用于多跳推理。输入为问题与上下文，DLM以并行方式生成部分去噪的草稿。核心模块一：事件驱动自时钟检索。该模块监控部分去噪状态中可图链接实体的出现，当可链接实体集合扩展时动态触发图检索，而非每步同步检索。依据是88%的目标实体在早期部分去噪状态出现，此机制平均使最终承诺提前5.7-9.6步，无需学习门控或启发式阈值。核心模块二：不完整查询图传播。当新实体查询出现时，使用专用图基础模型处理这些查询，在多跳图上传播并聚合证据，将聚合结果反馈以锐化并行预测，加速解码收敛。两个模块交互：检索事件由草稿状态触发，图传播输出增强后续去噪步骤的条件，形成异步自时钟的闭环。训练目标未明确披露，但框架采用DLM的扩散去噪目标联合图传播模块优化。推理时，DLM并行生成草稿，事件驱动检索增补图证据，图传播聚合多跳信息，循环直至最终承诺，实现4.1倍延迟降低。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average exact match", "value": "39.6% -> 45.2%", "setting": "三个挑战性多跳QA基准"}, {"metric": "average latency reduction", "value": "4.1x", "setting": "三个挑战性多跳QA基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 事件驱动自时钟检索：基于88%目标实体在部分去噪早期出现的发现，仅在可图链接实体集合扩展时动态触发图检索，避免学习门控或启发式阈值，平均提前5.7-9.6步完成最终承诺。；不完整查询图传播模块：使用专用图基础模型处理新出现的实体查询，持续聚合多跳证据以锐化并行预测并加速解码收敛。；将扩散语言模型的并行解码与GraphRAG桥接，实现图引导的并行解码框架。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) has remarkably enhanced large language models on complex reasoning by leveraging structured entity topologies. However, existing frameworks heavily rely on standard autoregressive language models where the nature of inherent sequential generation severely hinders overall inference efficiency. Inspired by Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) that offer massive parallelism via continuous refine-in-parallel decoding, we aim to accelerate GraphRAG in the discrete space. However, it remains non-trivial for two challenges. First, partially denoised drafts are highly dynamic and uncertain, making dynamic graph grounding non-trivial. Second, raw denoising states are inherently noisy and unstable, making synchronous graph retrieval and multi-hop aggregation computationally prohibitive. To this end, we present LADDER, a novel framework that bridges diffusion language modeling with GraphRAG through graph-guided parallel decoding. Specifically, (i) we propose an event-driven self-clocking retrieval, inspired by our key insight that 88% of target entities emerge early in the partially denoised state, leading final commitment by an average of 5.7-9.6 steps. This mechanism dynamically triggers graph retrieval only when the set of graph-linkable entities expands, yielding an asynchronous self-clocking policy that bypasses learned gates or heuristic thresholds. (ii) An incomplete-query graph propagation module is designed to process the newly emerging entity queries using a specialized graph foundation model, continuously aggregating multi-hop evidence to sharpen parallel predictions and accelerate overall decoding convergence. Extensive experiments on three challenging multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LADDER raises average exact match from 39.6% to 45.2% while achieving a 4.1x latency reduction.

</details>


### [26] [When and How Should an Agent Clarify? CIGAsk: Teaching LLMs to Clarify via Counterfactual Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24290)
*Li, Yunxiang,Wu, Xixin,Meng, Helen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIGAsk 提出一种无需 critic 的多轮 GRPO 强化学习配方，通过反事实信息增益和非对称歧义奖励分别教 LLM 如何提问与何时提问；7B 模型在三个澄清基准上超越更强基线，并具备跨数据集迁移和分布外单轮 QA 保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调 LLM 面对欠指定查询时倾向于强行回答而非请求澄清，且提示工程无法同时解决“何时提问”与“如何提问以恢复缺失信息”这两个耦合技能。

Method: CIGAsk 采用多轮 GRPO 强化学习循环，同时训练策略模型执行两个耦合行为：判断是否需要澄清（when）以及生成能恢复缺失信息的澄清问题（how）。训练时策略模型在多轮对话中与用户交互；反事实信息增益（CIG）模块冻结一个参考模型，分别比较在包含用户回复与不包含用户回复的条件下黄金答案的对数似然，二者差值作为每轮奖励信号，激励模型提出能够最大化信息恢复的澄清问题。非对称歧义奖励模块在对话终止 token 处，根据黄金歧义标签给出带符号奖励：对确实需要澄清的场景提问给予正向奖励，对不需要澄清的场景提问给予负向奖励，从而校准“何时提问”的决策边界。两种奖励在 GRPO 循环中组合，无需额外训练 critic 模型。推理时，策略模型根据当前查询和对话上下文依次决定是否输出澄清问题、生成具体问题，并在获得用户回复后继续回答，形成端到端的澄清问答流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体性能", "value": "CIGAsk-7B 在三个澄清基准（表格、段落、开放域 QA）上超越最强外部基线", "setting": "使用更小 backbone，跨数据集迁移无需逐数据集调参，并保持分布外单轮 QA 性能"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 反事实信息增益（CIG）：利用冻结参考模型对比有无用户回复时黄金答案对数似然，为每轮提供信用分配以指导如何提问。；非对称歧义奖励（Asymmetric Ambiguity Bonus）：基于黄金歧义标签在终止 token 处施加带符号奖励，指导何时提问。；将两种互补奖励信号统一到多轮 GRPO 循环中，无需单独训练 critic 即可同时学习两项技能。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned LLMs faced with underspecified queries often commit to a single interpretation rather than ask for clarification, producing confidently wrong answers. In our experiments, prompting alone is insufficient: models either ask for clarification on every query or ask vague questions that fail to recover the missing information. Addressing this failure requires learning two coupled skills: when to ask rather than answer and how to ask a question that recovers the disambiguating information. Existing recipes either address only one of these skills or require a separately trained critic. We propose CIGAsk, an RL recipe that teaches both skills through two complementary reward signals within a multi-turn GRPO loop. Counterfactual Information Gain (CIG) compares the gold-answer log-likelihood under a frozen reference model with and without the user response, providing per-turn credit that guides how to ask. The Asymmetric Ambiguity Bonus assigns a signed reward at the terminal token based on the gold ambiguity label, guiding when to ask. Across three clarification benchmarks spanning table, passage, and open-domain QA, CIGAsk-7B outperforms the strongest external baseline despite using a smaller backbone. It also transfers across datasets without per-dataset tuning while preserving single-turn QA performance on out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [27] [Self-Healing Harness for Runtime Oversight of Agent Self-Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24130)
*Tayebati, Sina,Kumar, Divake,Darabi, Nastaran,Krishnan, Ranganath,Trivedi, Amit Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM智能体自修改形式化为准入控制问题，提出模型无关的自愈Harness，通过外部运行时门控控制行为规则的持久化。在16对匹配运行中，Harness的任务完成分数和重复试验可靠性均优于Baseline，且门控拒绝了383个回放决定提案，其中55%会改善触发失败但导致其他用例退化，凸显了外部准入控制的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体能够改变自身未来行为，需要确定哪些自生成变更应被允许持久化，即自修改的准入控制问题。

Method: 该自愈Harness围绕未修改的智能体运行Detect、Notice、Heal、Validate循环。智能体在外部工作区编写候选行为规则，这些规则在评估期间获得临时执行权限。外部运行时门控控制规则持久化：候选规则需在触发失败上测量到改进，且在受保护用例上无超过固定余量的回归，才能获得跨回合持久权限。验证证据来源优先级：回放提供匹配证据（当可用时），前向试验提供较弱后备。语料级防护重新测试累积的活动规则集。由于适应修改的是诱导策略的上下文，模型权重保持不变，因此被准入的变更保持可检查、可回滚，并兼容闭权重模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务完成分数", "value": "Harness在全部16对比较中更高，其中两个配对bootstrap区间排除零", "setting": "16对匹配Baseline与Harness运行，覆盖AppWorld、Terminal-Bench、τ²-Bench"}, {"metric": "重复试验可靠性", "value": "12对更高，4对持平，0对更低", "setting": "16对匹配Baseline与Harness运行，覆盖AppWorld、Terminal-Bench、τ²-Bench"}, {"metric": "门控拒绝的回放决定提案数", "value": "383", "setting": "16对匹配运行"}, {"metric": "被拒绝提案中改善触发失败但导致先前正常用例退化的比例", "value": "211/383（55%）", "setting": "16对匹配运行"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将LLM智能体自修改形式化为准入控制问题，由外部运行时门控控制规则持久化；提出模型无关的自愈Harness，实现Detect、Notice、Heal、Validate循环；候选行为规则在外部工作区编写，获得临时执行权限，仅在触发失败上测量到改进且受保护用例无固定余量外回归后才获得跨回合持久权限；回放提供匹配证据，前向试验提供较弱后备，语料级防护重新测试累积的活动规则集

Abstract: LLM agents can change their own future behavior, raising a basic control question of which self-generated changes should be allowed to persist. We formulate this as admission control for self-modification. The agent may propose changes to its operating instructions, while an external runtime gate controls persistence. We implement this principle as a model-agnostic self-healing harness that runs a Detect, Notice, Heal, Validate loop around an otherwise unmodified agent. The agent authors candidate behavioral rules in an external workspace, where they receive provisional execution authority during evaluation and acquire persistent cross-episode authority only after measured improvement on the triggering failure without regression beyond a fixed margin on protected cases. Replay provides matched evidence when available, forward trials provide a weaker fallback, and a corpus-level guard re-tests the accumulated active rule set. Across 16 matched Baseline and Harness runs spanning AppWorld, Terminal-Bench, and $\tau^2$-Bench, the gate rejected 383 replay-decided proposals. Of these, 211 (55%) improved their triggering failure while degrading a case that previously worked. This shows that locally beneficial self-modifications can introduce collateral regressions often enough to materially affect gate decisions, providing direct empirical motivation for external admission control. Task-completion score is higher under the Harness in all 16 pairs, with two paired bootstrap intervals excluding zero, while repeated-trial reliability is higher in 12 pairs, tied in 4, and lower in none. Because adaptation modifies the policy-inducing context while leaving model weights fixed, admitted changes remain inspectable, reversible, and compatible with closed-weight models.

</details>


### [28] [EDGEGEN: Improving Tool-Calling Agents Beyond Happy Paths with Synthetic Edge Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24115)
*Abichandani, Harshavardhan,Chong, Penny,Shen, Jiyuan,Singh, Gunraj,Hathidara, Ashutosh,Yu, Marcus Duigan Xing,Lo, Jane,Ghosh, Atin,Li, Yipeng,Dahlmeier, Daniel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EdgeGen是一个从智能体规范中提取合规规则并生成数据库接地边界用例的合成任务生成框架，与现有合成数据技术结合形成全自动闭环系统，用于工具调用LLM智能体的微调和harness优化。在tau2bench airline domain上，微调带来2%至42%的平均进展提升，harness优化相比人工策划和基础harness分别提升10%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成任务生成方法通常生成通用任务，忽略智能体的底层状态或数据库，无法反映真实使用多样性；高质量、多样化的任务数据集因隐私等约束难以获取，导致工具调用LLM智能体的评估和优化不充分。

Method: EdgeGen是一个合成任务生成框架，其输入为智能体规范（agent specification）和底层数据库。首先，框架从智能体规范中提取合规规则（compliance rules），这些规则定义了智能体在工具调用过程中必须遵守的约束。然后，基于这些规则生成边界用例任务（edge-case tasks），这些任务的目的是故意违反这些合规规则，从而测试智能体在异常或边界情况下的行为。生成的任务是数据库接地的（database-grounded），即任务依赖于智能体的底层状态或数据库，确保任务与真实环境一致。EdgeGen可以与现有的合成数据生成技术结合使用，形成一个全自动的闭环系统。该闭环系统无需人工标注，能够持续生成任务数据，并用于两种优化路径：一是对智能体进行微调（finetuning），二是进行harness优化（harness optimization）。微调使用EdgeGen生成的数据，旨在提升智能体在边界情况下的表现；harness优化则调整智能体执行框架或评估框架中的参数或提示，以更好地引导智能体处理边界任务。整个流程不依赖人类注释，自动生成任务、优化模型和评估框架。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean progress", "value": "2% to 42% improvement", "setting": "tau2bench airline domain, finetuning on data generated by EdgeGen"}, {"metric": "mean progress", "value": "10% improvement over human-curated harness", "setting": "harness optimization for Gemma-4-e4b model"}, {"metric": "mean progress", "value": "30% improvement over base harness", "setting": "harness optimization for Gemma-4-e4b model"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: EdgeGen框架：从智能体规范中提取合规规则，并基于规则生成旨在违反规则的数据库接地边界用例任务。；将EdgeGen与现有合成数据生成技术结合，形成无需人工标注的全自动闭环系统，支持微调和harness优化。

Abstract: Tool-calling LLM agents are increasingly deployed in enterprise applications. However, effective evaluation and optimization require high-quality, diverse task datasets that are often difficult to obtain due to privacy and other constraints. Existing synthetic task generation methods often produce generic tasks that ignore an agent's underlying state or database and fail to reflect real-world usage diversity. We propose EdgeGen, a synthetic task generation framework that extracts compliance rules from an agent's specification and uses them to generate database-grounded edge-case tasks designed to violate these rules. When combined with existing synthetic data generation techniques, EdgeGen enables agent improvement through finetuning and harness optimization. The resulting pipeline forms a fully automated closed-loop system that requires no human annotation. Finetuning on data generated by EdgeGen yields a consistent mean progress improvement of 2 percent to 42 percent on tau2bench airline domain, while other baseline methods show degradation for some models. On the other hand, for harness optimization, our method shows a mean progress improvement of 10 percent and 30 percent over the human-curated and base harnesses, respectively, for the Gemma-4-e4b model.

</details>

*Wang, Haixin,Wang, Xiaoxuan,Zhang, Junkai,Zhang, Han,Sun, Renliang,Taylor, Alexander K,Shi, Yidan,Deng, Haoran,Wang, Chenguang,Cong, Jason,Sun, Yizhou,Wang, Wei*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业部署的通用智能体需要整合多个训练阶段获得的不同能力，但缺乏Agent持续学习（ACL）的成熟方案，且对现有整合范式（多教师在线策略蒸馏、自蒸馏微调、模型合并）的权衡理解不足，尤其面临灾难性遗忘和新能力保持的挑战。

Method: 论文提出ACLArena框架，用于研究智能体持续学习（ACL）。整体架构为多阶段顺序训练流水线：首先在不同阶段依次训练模型获得多种能力（如推理、智能体任务），每个阶段可能采用监督微调、强化学习或蒸馏等方式。为分析遗忘与泛化，作者从模型层面（参数变化、表示漂移）和令牌层面（token概率、损失分布）进行诊断。在此基础上，系统比较三种能力整合范式：1）多教师在线策略蒸馏：使用多个教师模型在策略采样数据上蒸馏；2）自蒸馏微调：模型自身生成数据再微调；3）模型合并：合并不同阶段模型参数（如LoRA合并）。最后提出新的ACL配方：离线回放（replay）高质量轨迹以巩固旧能力，同时维护一个由多个LoRA专家组成的路由网络，每个专家通过强化学习（RL）专门化于特定领域；推理时根据输入动态路由到相应专家，从而在保持新能力的同时恢复旧能力。训练目标包括各阶段的原始损失（如RL目标、蒸馏损失）以及持续学习中的回放损失。评估在四个推理和智能体任务上进行，涵盖域内和域外设置。由于未披露具体模型规模、训练数据量、硬件等，架构细节基于摘要和常见ACL范式描述，未添加未披露内容。
