*Rao, Vigneshwar Ravi,Swarnakar, Rupesh,Syedâ , Fayeq Jeelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个六节点 LangGraph 迭代智能体实现 Text-to-SQL，仅用提示工程在 Chicago Crime 数据库（约 850 万条记录，22 个属性）上评估，100 条问题基准上 V2 达到有效 SQL 率 93%、执行准确率 60%（混合）与 19%（严格），合成质量 4.34/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非技术用户无法直接编写 SQL 从运营数据库中获取洞察，需要端到端的自然语言到 SQL 转换并保证可靠性与结果可读性。

Method: 系统是一个基于 LangGraph StateGraph 的六节点迭代智能体。第一步，相关性检查器判断用户问题是否与 Chicago Crime 数据库相关。第二步，实时获取数据库模式（schema）。第三步，使用提示工程生成 PostgreSQL 查询。第四步，通过干跑（dry run）验证 SQL 的语法与语义正确性。第五步，若验证失败则触发重试机制，重新生成并验证。第六步，执行查询并将结果集用自然语言叙述。整个流程中没有模型微调，仅依赖提示工程。训练仅使用 100 条人工构造的自然语言问题与标准 SQL 作为评估基准，按难度分层（简单 30，中等 40，困难 30）。评估指标包括有效 SQL 率、执行准确率（混合关系等价性与严格 JSON 匹配）和合成质量得分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Valid SQL Rate", "value": "93%", "setting": "V2 相比 V1 的 87%"}, {"metric": "Execution Accuracy (Hybrid Relational Equivalence)", "value": "60%", "setting": "V2 相比 V1 的 47%"}, {"metric": "Execution Accuracy (Strict JSON Matching)", "value": "19%", "setting": "V2 相比 V1 的 12%"}, {"metric": "Mean Synthesis Quality", "value": "4.34/5", "setting": "V2 相比 V1 的 3.91/5"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于 LangGraph StateGraph 的六节点迭代智能体工作流：相关性检查、实时模式获取、SQL 生成、干跑验证、失败重试、执行与自然语言叙述。；通过提示工程实现，无需任何模型微调。；系统性移除 LIMIT 10 提示指令，显著提升多行答案的正确性。

Abstract: Non-technical stakeholders frequently cannot write the SQL needed to extract insights from operational databases. We built and evaluated a Text-to-SQL agent that closes this gap end to end: a six-node LangGraph StateGraph checks question relevance, fetches the live schema, generates PostgreSQL, validates it with a dry run, retries on failure, executes the query, and narrates the result set in plain English. The agent uses prompt engineering only; no model was fine-tuned. We evaluated it on the Chicago Crime dataset (approximately 8.5 million records, 22 attributes) against a hand-built benchmark of 100 natural language questions with ground-truth SQL, stratified into 30 Easy, 40 Medium and 30 Hard items. Comparing two prompt revisions of the same agent, the revised system (V2) reached a Valid SQL Rate of 93% (from 87%), an Execution Accuracy of 60% under a hybrid relational equivalence metric (from 47%; 19% from 12% under strict JSON matching), and a mean Synthesis Quality of 4.34 out of 5 (from 3.91). The single largest driver was removing a LIMIT 10 instruction from the system prompt, which had been truncating multi-row answers. Error analysis attributes the residual failures to relevance-checker false rejections, ambiguous question semantics, and free-tier API rate limits rather than to the language generation step. We report no comparison against an external baseline system or a public benchmark; the study is a single-model engineering evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Block-Sparse Attention with Semantic-Geometric Decoupled Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22884)
*Long, Xinwei,Sun, Weigao,Gao, Weibo,Jiao, Pengkun,Qi, Biqing,Zhu, Feida,Zhong, Yiran,Hoi, Steven,Zhou, Bowen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种免训练、无需 token 级搜索的块稀疏注意力路由框架，通过将语义聚合移到 pre-RoPE 空间并解耦几何偏置，解决现有路由器语义与位置信息纠缠的问题。在 4K–128K 长上下文文本和视频任务上接近全注意力精度，路由开销低于 3.4 ms，128K 上下文下相较 FlashAttn 加速 5.03 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 block-sparse attention 的免训练块路由在 post-RoPE 表示上执行语义聚合，导致语义聚合与 RoPE 几何纠缠、高频相位抵消削弱局部位置线索，难以准确路由。

Method: 输入为长上下文序列，经过 token embedding 后进入 attention 计算。核心模块为免训练的块路由：首先在 pre-RoPE 空间对每个 query 块进行语义聚合，避免 RoPE 旋转造成的语义-几何纠缠；同时利用离线结构先验和相对块距离重建几何偏置，最后将语义分数与几何偏置组合成显式闭式块路由分数，据此选择每个 query 块对应的少量 key 块进行块稀疏注意力计算。该路由无需 token 级搜索或事后校准，可在推理时以低于 3.4 ms 的开销完成路由，进而在长上下文下加速注意力计算。训练目标与常规语言模型一致，路由本身不引入额外训练。推理流程：给定输入序列，分块后执行上述路由，仅对选中的 key 块进行注意力计算，从而降低计算量并保持接近全注意力的精度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "接近 full-attention 准确率", "setting": "长上下文文本与视频任务，4K–128K 上下文长度"}, {"metric": "speedup", "value": "5.03×", "setting": "相较 FlashAttn，128K 上下文长度"}, {"metric": "routing overhead", "value": "低于 3.4 ms", "setting": "未明确硬件与批大小"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出语义-几何解耦路由（Semantic-Geometric Decoupled Routing）：将语义聚合移到 pre-RoPE 空间，避免与 RoPE 几何纠缠。；使用离线结构先验和相对块距离重建几何偏置，形成显式闭式块路由分数。；无需 token 级搜索或事后校准，实现免训练、硬件友好的块稀疏注意力路由。

Abstract: Long-context inference has become a defining capability of large language models, but exact dense attention remains costly due to its quadratic scaling with sequence length. Block-sparse attention offers a hardware-friendly alternative by routing each query block to a small set of relevant key blocks, yet accurate training-free block routing remains difficult. Existing routers often pool post-RoPE token representations, which entangles semantic aggregation with RoPE-induced geometry and attenuates local positional cues through high-frequency phase cancellation. To resolve this mismatch, we propose \textbf{Semantic-Geometric Decoupled Routing}, a training-free block routing framework that shifts semantic aggregation to the pre-RoPE space and reconstructs geometric bias with an offline structural prior and relative block distances. This decomposition yields an explicit closed-form block routing score without token-level search or post-hoc calibration. Experiments on long-context text and video tasks show that our method approaches full-attention accuracy across 4K--128K contexts, keeps routing overhead below 3.4 ms, and achieves a 5.03$\times$ speedup over FlashAttn at a 128K context length.

</details>


### [59] [Analyzing Public Discourse on Urbanism: Topic Clustering, Sentiment Analysis and Retrieval-Augmented Generation using YouTube Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22705)
*Morales, Jakob,Hegde, Monica,Syed, Fayeq Jeelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个基于RAG的城市议题对话系统，处理22788个YouTube文本块，覆盖309个城市。系统结合了地理实体解析、主题建模、情感分析和检索增强生成。研究发现密集检索优于TF-IDF，RoBERTa优于VADER但在中性类上崩溃；BERTScore在短文本上不可用，ROUGE-1是释义驱动的下界。这些发现对任何短用户生成文档的RAG系统具有通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线城市议题讨论数据量大但非结构化，现有城市评估工具无法捕捉这些信息；同时标准NLP组件在短、非正式、地理模糊的文本上的表现具有系统性的测量问题。

Method: 系统输入为YouTube视频转录和评论文本，首先进行地理实体解析，将非结构化文本关联到309个北美城市。然后对文本分块，共22788个块。使用主题建模和情感分析（RoBERTa和VADER对比）对块进行标签。检索阶段采用密集检索（对比TF-IDF基线）从块库中获取相关块。生成阶段将检索到的块作为上下文，通过检索增强生成（RAG）产生对话回答。训练目标未明确，可能涉及对检索器的微调和生成器的上下文学习。推理流程为：用户提问→检索相关块→将块与问题拼接→LLM生成回答。评估包括检索精确率（P@5）、情感分类F1、以及生成接地性（BERTScore和ROUGE-1）分析。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro-F1", "value": "0.589 vs 0.464", "setting": "Twitter-tuned RoBERTa 情感分类 vs VADER 词典基线，在YouTube城市评论数据上"}, {"metric": "McNemar p值", "value": "0.0001", "setting": "RoBERTa vs VADER 分类器差异显著性检验"}, {"metric": "Cohen's kappa", "value": "0.53", "setting": "标注者间一致性，针对中性类"}, {"metric": "P@5", "value": "0.790 vs 0.560", "setting": "密集检索 vs TF-IDF基线，在所有截断点"}, {"metric": "块级精确率（人工评分）", "value": "0.94 vs 0.660", "setting": "块级精确率 vs 视频级相关性代理"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将地理实体解析、主题建模、情感分析与RAG组合成端到端对话系统，处理22788个YouTube转录和评论块，覆盖309个北美城市。；系统性地测量了标准NLP组件（情感分类器、检索、评估指标）在短的非正式地理模糊文本上的失效模式，如中性类崩溃、BERTScore在短文本对比中的不可用性、ROUGE-1作为释义驱动的下界。；揭示视频级相关性代理严重低估块级精确率（0.660 vs 0.94 under human rating），为RAG评估提供实证依据。

Abstract: Online discourse about urban issues - walkability, cycling infrastructure, public transit, housing density, and street safety - is voluminous but unstructured, and existing city-evaluation tools capture none of it. We present a pipeline and conversational system that combines geographic entity resolution, topic modeling, sentiment analysis, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) over 22,788 chunks of YouTube transcripts and comments spanning 309 North American cities. Beyond the system itself, our contribution is a set of measurements about what happens when standard NLP components meet short, informal, geographically ambiguous text. A Twitter-tuned RoBERTa classifier outperforms a VADER lexicon baseline by 12.6 macro-F1 points (0.589 vs. 0.464; McNemar p = 0.0001), but both models collapse on the neutral class, which dominates urbanist comment traffic; annotators disagree on the same class (Cohen's kappa = 0.53). Dense retrieval beats a TF-IDF baseline at every cutoff (P@5 0.790 vs. 0.560), and video-level relevance proxies understate chunk-level precision by a wide margin (0.660 vs. 0.94 under human rating). For groundedness evaluation, we find BERTScore unusable when a multi-sentence generated summary is compared against a single short comment - scores are nearly flat regardless of relevance - and show that ROUGE-1-based groundedness is a paraphrase-driven lower bound rather than a hallucination rate. These findings generalize beyond the urbanist domain to any RAG system built over short user-generated documents.

</details>


### [60] [LLaDA-PRM: A Bidirectional Step-Level Reasoning Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22700)
*Feng, Yiming,Deng, Naihao,Chen, Yulong,Mihalcea, Rada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLaDA-PRM 是一个基于双向注意力的 8B 步骤级推理评估器，在 MR-MATH-invalid 和 MR-GSM8K 上达到 88.8 和 83.8 步骤级 F1，超越 34B 基线，并在在线不完整轨迹评估和训练数据选择中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归步骤级推理评估器因因果注意力只能利用问题、先前步骤和当前步骤，无法利用完整解答中后续步骤来验证早期步骤的有效性；同时缺少对不完整推理轨迹的在线评估能力。

Method: LLaDA-PRM 基于 LLaDA 架构，将步骤级评估建模为双向注意力掩码下的序列标注或分类任务。输入为包含问题、完整或部分推理步骤的序列，通过双向自注意力使每个步骤的表征能够聚合来自后续步骤的信息。模型输出每个步骤的有效性判断。训练目标为最大化步骤级正确性预测。在在线场景中，模型可直接评估不完整轨迹。此外，该评估器可作为训练数据选择器，用于筛选高质量推理数据以微调下游模型（如 Mistral-7B）。推理流程为：给定推理轨迹，LLaDA 编码器以双向注意力生成步骤级表示，通过预测头输出步骤得分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "步骤级 F1", "value": "88.8", "setting": "MR-MATH-invalid 基准，8B 双向评估器 LLaDA-PRM"}, {"metric": "步骤级 F1", "value": "83.8", "setting": "MR-GSM8K 分布外原始问题子集，8B 双向评估器 LLaDA-PRM"}, {"metric": "步骤级 F1 提升", "value": "11.3", "setting": "MR-MATH-invalid，相对于 ReasonEval-Llemma-34B"}, {"metric": "步骤级 F1 提升", "value": "10.3", "setting": "MR-GSM8K 分布外原始问题子集，相对于 ReasonEval-Llemma-34B"}, {"metric": "步骤级 F1 改进", "value": "一致改善", "setting": "54 次对照实验，1B–3B 规模，双向注意力 vs 因果注意力，仅改变自注意力掩码"}, {"metric": "MATH-500 性能提升", "value": "未提供具体数值", "setting": "使用 LLaDA-PRM 作为训练数据选择信号，Mistral-7B 模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过 1B–3B 规模、54 次对照实验（仅改变自注意力掩码）验证双向注意力在步骤级评估中一致优于因果注意力。；提出 8B 双向评估器 LLaDA-PRM，利用完整解答的下游信息进行步骤评估。；在 MR-MATH-invalid 和 MR-GSM8K 分布外子集上分别取得 88.8 和 83.8 步骤级 F1，大幅超越 ReasonEval-Llemma-34B。；展示 LLaDA-PRM 在评估不完整推理轨迹的在线场景中同样有效。；将 LLaDA-PRM 作为训练数据选择信号，提升 Mistral-7B 在 MATH-500 上的性能。

Abstract: Step-level reasoning evaluators are commonly based on autoregressive language models, whose causal attention restricts each step representation to the problem, previous steps, and the current step. Yet, when the complete solution is available, the validity of an earlier step may become clearer only through its downstream consequences. We validate this hypothesis through a controlled 54-run comparison of causal and bidirectional LLaDA evaluators at 1B--3B scale, changing only the self-attention mask, and find bidirectional attention yields consistent improvements. Building on this finding, we introduce \prm{}, an 8B bidirectional evaluator that reaches 88.8 step-level F1 on MR-MATH-invalid and 83.8 on the out-of-distribution MR-GSM8K original-question subset, outperforming ReasonEval-Llemma-34B by 11.3 and 10.3 F1 points, respectively. \prm{} also remains effective when evaluating incomplete reasoning traces in online settings, outperforming the strongest baselines on both benchmarks by a large margin. We further show that \prm{} provides an effective training-data selection signal, improving Mistral-7B performance on MATH-500.

</details>


### [61] [Do Student LLMs Inherit OOD Robustness? Invariance-Weighted Distillation for Reliable Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22566)
*Kannangara, Dileesha,Dutta, Sanghamitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出不变性加权蒸馏（IWD），通过估计教师预测在合成环境扰动下的不变性来重加权蒸馏样本，从而缓解学生 LLM 在 OOD 场景下的性能退化。在四个 NLP 基准和两个模型家族上，IWD 一致优于标准 KD 等基线，OOD 平均准确率提升最多 14.94 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏导致学生模型在分布外（OOD）场景下性能显著下降，其原因是学生学到了虚假相关性以及教师的能力未被有效利用。

Method: IWD 是一种在知识蒸馏训练过程中动态重加权样本的框架。输入是蒸馏数据集（可能包含虚假线索），教师模型对每个样本进行预测。对于每个样本，IWD 在多个合成环境中扰动与任务无关的虚假线索而保留核心语义，然后观察教师模型在这些扰动下的预测不变性。如果教师预测在多个合成环境中保持稳定，则认为教师更依赖因果特征，该样本获得更高的蒸馏权重；反之则降低权重。加权后的样本用于训练学生模型，标准蒸馏损失通常采用教师预测与学生预测之间的散度（如 KL 散度），IWD 通过权重影响每个样本对梯度的贡献。理论分析表明，相比均匀加权，IWD 降低了虚假到因果（S2C）梯度比，从而使学生学习到更不变的表示。训练目标是让学生拟合加权后的教师预测分布，最终在保持分布内性能的同时提升分布外鲁棒性。推理时，学生模型独立运行，无需教师或环境生成过程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均 OOD 准确率", "value": "比标准 KD 提升 4.34 个百分点", "setting": "NLI 任务 (MNLI)"}, {"metric": "平均 OOD 准确率", "value": "比标准 KD 提升 14.94 个百分点", "setting": "QA 任务 (SQuAD-v2)"}, {"metric": "OOD 最优准确率计数", "value": "16 个 OOD 基准中 15 个取得最高准确率", "setting": "四个 NLP 基准，两个模型家族 (DeBERTa-v3, Qwen-2.5)"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出不变性加权蒸馏（IWD）框架，通过多合成环境下的预测不变性动态重加权训练样本，以估计教师对因果特征的依赖程度。；揭示并形式化数据虚假性和教师能力两个导致知识蒸馏 OOD 性能退化的机制。；理论上证明 IWD 相比均匀加权 KD 能降低虚假到因果（S2C）梯度比，推动学生学习更不变的表征。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) aims to compress high-performance teacher LLMs into lightweight students. However, distilled students often exhibit substantial performance degradation in out-of-distribution (OOD) settings, a critical gap that remains underexplored. We identify two compounding mechanisms causing OOD performance degradation: (1) data spuriousness: students can learn spurious correlations in the distillation dataset over genuine causal relationships; and (2) teacher capability: standard KD treats all samples uniformly, ignoring whether the teacher is guided by causal features or misled by spurious shortcuts on a given sample. To address these challenges, we propose Invariance-Weighted Distillation (IWD), a theoretically grounded framework that dynamically reweights training samples using an estimate of the teacher's causal reliance derived from prediction invariance across multiple synthetic environments. IWD perturbs spurious cues while preserving core semantics, assigning higher distillation weights to samples whose teacher predictions remain invariant, indicating greater reliance on causal rather than spurious features. We theoretically show that IWD reduces the student's Spurious-to-Causal (S2C) gradient ratio compared to standard uniformly weighted KD, driving the student toward more invariant representations. Experiments on four NLP benchmarks (MNLI, SQuAD-v2, CoNLL-2003 NER, and SST-2) across two model families (DeBERTa-v3 and Qwen-2.5) demonstrate that IWD consistently outperforms strong KD baselines on OOD evaluations while maintaining competitive in-distribution (ID) performance. Specifically, IWD achieves the highest accuracy in 15 out of 16 OOD benchmarks and improves average OOD performance over standard KD by 4.34 percentage points on NLI and 14.94 percentage points on QA.

</details>


### [62] [Deep Persona: A Psychologically Grounded Architecture and Evaluation Framework for Role-Playing Agents and Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22255)
*Dror, Rotem,Elyoseph, Zohar,Haber, Yuval,Refoua, Elad,Ayalon, Oshrat,Solomon, Adir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Deep Persona，一种心理学驱动的三层人格架构与无参考评估框架，用于构建长程一致的角色扮演LLM智能体。架构通过可观察表达、潜在信念、核心动机三层组织角色，并采用脚本化确定性和受限能动性约束模型行为。评估使用临床心理工具和对抗压力测试与人类分布对比。案例显示结构化人格可提升对话与人类行为的一致性，但缺乏生产部署和量化离线增益。对LLM智能体系统与评估方向有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的人格模拟多依赖浅层角色描述，难以在长程交互中维持一致的角色行为，且缺乏无参考的对话自然度与心理真实性评估框架。

Method: Deep Persona是一个三层心理架构：底层为核心动机驱动，中层为潜在信念，顶层为可观察表达。系统遵循脚本化确定性和受限能动性原则，LLM被限制为反应式引擎，由结构化内部脚本引导，而非自由生成角色行为。输入为用户对话或模拟场景，经过脚本层解析后，由LLM在受限条件下生成符合层级约束的回应。评估部分为无参考框架：使用既有心理临床量表将对话自然度与经验人类分布进行对比，并施加对抗压力测试以考察情绪表达和联合注意等维度。论文通过两个Deep Persona案例研究验证结构化人格能产生更接近人类对话行为的交互。未披露具体模型参数、训练数据、硬件或推理成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出三层心理学人格架构，将角色组织为可观察表达层、潜在信念层和核心动机驱动层；引入脚本化确定性和受限能动性原则，使LLM作为由结构化内部脚本引导的反应式引擎；提出无参考评估框架，使用既有心理临床工具和对抗压力测试，将对话自然度与经验人类分布进行基准比较；通过两个Deep Persona案例研究验证结构化人格可使交互更接近人类对话行为

Abstract: Existing approaches to persona simulation with Large Language Models (LLMs) mostly rely on shallow character descriptions that fail to sustain coherent character behavior across extended interactions. We introduce Deep Persona, a psychologically grounded, three-layered architecture that organizes personas into hierarchical levels of observable expression, latent beliefs, and core motivational drives, for constructing highly convincing role-playing agents. Governed by the principles of scripted determinism and bounded agency, the architecture restricts the model to a reactive engine guided by a structured internal script. We further propose a reference-free evaluation framework that benchmarks dialogue naturalness against empirical human distributions using established psychological clinical instruments and adversarial stress-tests. Empirical evaluation reveals that while LLMs achieve high pragmatic fluency, they exhibit systematic limitations in emotional expression and joint attention. In addition, we present a case study of two Deep Personas and evaluate them using the proposed framework, demonstrating that structured personas can produce interactions that more closely align with human conversational behavior.

*Lu, Yuan,Zhang, Yukuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示检索增强概率预测LLM对新闻语料投毒高度脆弱：单篇文章即可翻转56%预测，五篇翻转69-73%并使Brier分数从0.18恶化至0.37；三种常规防御均存在廉价绕过，说明概率判断会继承信息供应链的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放可爬取新闻语料被对手通过发布文章污染时，检索增强的概率型LLM预测器输出概率会被系统性操纵，且现有防御可被廉价绕过。

Method: 系统以检索增强的概率预测器为对象：输入为ForecastBench问题与新闻检索器从17.4M篇Common Crawl News语料中按查询检索到的新闻上下文；检索器基于与问题的相似度返回新闻文章，经过排序后取Top文档构建上下文；7-8B开源LLM基于该上下文输出对问题结果的概率估计。攻击者仅能向语料库发布文章，无法访问检索器、模型或用户查询。注入文章通过提高与查询的语义相似度、争取更高检索排名和增大上下文占比来影响模型输出。评估在500个已解决问题上进行，采用严格爬取日期截断模拟真实时间线。防御包括源白名单、先隔离后聚合预测和困惑度过滤，但分别被伪造发布者、多数投毒和更高温度生成绕过。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测翻转率", "value": "56%", "setting": "单篇LLM撰写文章注入，500个ForecastBench问题，跨0.5阈值"}, {"metric": "预测翻转率", "value": "69-73%", "setting": "五篇文章注入，500个ForecastBench问题，跨0.5阈值"}, {"metric": "概率净偏移", "value": "+0.13至+0.22", "setting": "五篇文章注入，扣除中性文章安慰剂效应"}, {"metric": "Brier分数", "value": "0.18 → 0.37", "setting": "五篇文章注入后预测性能恶化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化新闻语料投毒对概率型预测器的威胁模型，区别于针对事实答案或观点极性的传统RAG投毒；在ForecastBench 500个已解决问题、17.4M文章Common Crawl News语料、严格爬取日期截断下，用三个7-8B开源模型构建检索增强预测器进行攻击评估；证明单篇LLM撰写文章即可翻转56%预测，五篇翻转69-73%并净移概率+0.13至+0.22，Brier分数从0.18恶化至0.37；揭示投毒效果随注入文章数量、检索排名、查询相似度和上下文占比单调，跨模型家族迁移且与声称发布者无关；评估三种自然防御（源白名单、先隔离后聚合预测、困惑度过滤）并展示各自可被伪造发布者、多数投毒、更高温度生成便宜绕过

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to forecast real-world events by retrieving and reasoning over news. We show that this dependence on an open, crawlable news corpus creates a new attack surface: an adversary who can merely publish articles--without access to the retriever, the model, or the user's queries--can systematically move the forecaster's output probabilities. We formalize news-corpus poisoning of probabilistic forecasters, a threat model distinct from prior RAG poisoning, which targets factual answers or opinion polarity rather than calibrated probabilities. We evaluate the attack on 500 resolved ForecastBench questions against a 17.4M-article Common Crawl News corpus with a strict crawl-date cutoff, using three retrieval-augmented forecasters built on open 7-8B models. A single LLM-written article per question flips 56% of forecasts across the 0.5 boundary; five articles flip 69-73% and shift probabilities by +0.13 to +0.22 net of a neutral-article placebo, degrading the Brier score from 0.18 to 0.37. The effect is monotone in the number, retrieval rank, query similarity, and context share of injected articles, transfers across model families, and is unaffected by the claimed publisher. We then evaluate three natural defenses--source allow-lists, isolate-then-aggregate forecasting, and perplexity filtering--and show that each has a cheap bypass: spoofed publishers, majority poisoning, and higher-temperature generation, respectively. Our results indicate that probabilistic LLM judgments inherit the full fragility of the information supply chain they consume.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出回放门控神经执行框架，将LLM执行语义分解为持久谓词、状态索引认证集、瞬态见证、预算受限查找器和执行授权五个对象，通过冻结模型FP32/BF16隔离回放与运行审计来授权动作。在Qwen3-0.6B和SmolLM2-360M-Instruct上的实验显示认证集随状态变化、迁移受限、查找器有遗漏，三层级联在256条密封请求上全部审计授权，搜索认证成本比中位数0.1055。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入条件的神经干预下，同一行为规范对应多个动作，哪些动作在特定执行状态下有效且可被授权执行。

Method: 框架将执行过程分为五个对象：1）持久行为谓词，定义哪些行为规范必须持续满足；2）状态索引认证实现集，对每个执行状态给出认证的动作集合；3）瞬态动作见证，由查找器提出的候选动作；4）预算受限查找器，在成本约束下探索候选动作；5）执行授权，通过回放认证和运行审计后授予。候选动作在冻结模型上进行隔离的FP32/BF16回放，检查动作是否实际可行；提交执行还需有效的运行审计。实验使用三层级联：最低成本层首先尝试认证，若失败则逐层升级至审计有效的搜索遗漏层；未认证提案被拒绝。在Qwen3-0.6B和SmolLM2-360M-Instruct上测试了认证集的差异、迁移、回放成功率、查找器遗漏以及搜索成本。

Abstract: Input-conditioned neural interventions raise a runtime question: what persists when one behavioral specification admits multiple actions whose validity depends on execution state? We introduce replay-gated neural execution, separating five objects: a persistent behavioral predicate, its state-indexed certified realization set, a transient action witness, a budget-limited finder, and execution authorization. Candidates undergo isolated FP32/BF16 replay of the frozen model; commitment additionally requires a valid run audit. Experiments on Qwen3-0.6B and SmolLM2-360M-Instruct establish distinct failure modes for these objects. Independent initializations yield distinct certified actions in all 24 tested fixed-state cells. Unchanged SmolLM2 witnesses remain certified in all 128 native states but only 66 of 384 off-diagonal transfers. All 767 archived Qwen witnesses replay successfully, yet a budget-limited finder misses one known-realizable cell in all three prespecified runs. Of 1,141 replay-submitted candidates, 174 fail item certification. A frozen three-tier cascade uses these boundaries to reject uncertified proposals and escalate audit-valid search misses. On 256 previously sealed Qwen Fresh requests, 221 first certify at the lowest-cost tier and all 256 receive audited authorization, with no observed bypass. Relative to frozen full search, the median singleton search-and-certification cost ratio is 0.1055 and P95 is 1.3485, including failed tiers. Within the studied behavioral family on two small models, these results support state-indexed, set-valued execution semantics: specifications persist, search proposes witnesses, and replay certification plus run audit grants execution authority.

</details>

Abstract: We present H2LooP Telecom Model v1, a domain-specialized large language models fine-tuned for the telecommunications industry. We release two domain-adapted model variants serving complementary use cases: a comprehension-focused variant for telecom domain question answering and reasoning, and an agentic variant for autonomous telecom code generation, pull request resolution, and code commits on production repositories. H2LooP Telecom achieves strong results on the GSMA Open Telecom Lite (OT-Lite) benchmark and a proprietary telecom code generation benchmark, outperforming frontier closed-source models such as GPT-5 and Claude Opus on independent leaderboard evaluation, while preserving general-purpose capabilities. The Comprehension variant achieves 81.8% weighted average on OT-Lite Pass@3, and, independently, ranks 5th overall on the official community-run Open Telco AI Leaderboard* at only 31B parameters-ahead of frontier closed-source systems including Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5, Gemini 3 Flash, Grok-4-fast, and Kimi K2.5. Our agentic variant obtains a relative improvement of +8.8% in AST Similarity and +20.0% in Location IoU over the base model on telecom code generation, while maintaining identical MMLU (74.0%) and BFCL v3 multi-turn function calling (79.0%) performance, indicating zero catastrophic forgetting. Domain specialization on curated telecom corpora, spanning 3GPP standards, O-RAN specifications, network telemetry, and real repository commits, yields substantial improvements over general-purpose models of equivalent scale, approaches frontier closed-source models on domain-specific evaluation, and is independently corroborated by our official leaderboard standing.

</details>


### [66] [Knowing, and Saying It Only When Asked: LLM Endognostics and the Schizognosis of Minerva-7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22219)
*Davide, Fabrizio,Collova, Francesco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM endognostics白盒审计框架，通过Jacobian透镜和方向消融揭示模型内部知识表示与行为输出解耦，证明线性可解码性不等于因果控制，为LLM评估与可靠性提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为评估（模型输出）与模型内部潜在知识表示之间是否存在系统性不一致，以及线性可解码性是否等同于对 verbalization 的因果控制。

Method: 该框架以Minerva-7B-Instruct-v1.0为研究对象，核心流程为：1) 输入最小提示对或事实-语言框架交叉对；2) 前向传播获取残差流激活；3) 使用Jacobian透镜将残差流投影至词汇空间，计算对比性内生认知边际（Contrastive Endognostic Margin），量化潜在风险区分度；4) 通过方向消融（ablation）操控残差流中的特定语义方向，观察输出变化；5) 对比行为评估、线性探针解码与消融因果恢复。训练目标无额外训练，均为推理期分析。推理时无需额外硬件资源披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "行为一致性比例", "value": "63.7% (95% CI [55.0%, 71.6%])", "setting": "Minerva-7B-Instruct-v1.0 在124对最小提示对上"}, {"metric": "预设虚假遵从率", "value": "72%", "setting": "25个事实与5种语言框架交叉实验"}, {"metric": "消融恢复率", "value": "11/25 (44%)", "setting": "消融植入虚假方向，McNemar p=0.0010"}, {"metric": "盲标注二元一致kappa", "value": "0.68", "setting": "人类标注验证消融恢复正确性"}, {"metric": "线性探针准确率", "value": "77%", "setting": "第10层，样本外线性探针"}, {"metric": "正交消融恢复率", "value": "0%", "setting": "对线性探针方向正交消融"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 LLM endognostics 白盒内部审计框架，通过 Jacobian 透镜将残差流投影到词汇空间以提取潜在知识；引入 Contrastive Endognostic Margin 度量风险区分度；通过消融植入错误方向实现因果操控，恢复正确回答；证明线性探针的高解码准确率与因果控制之间的解耦

Abstract: Evaluating an aligned language model by reading its answers assumes the answers carry the distinction the evaluator cares about. We introduce LLM endognostics, a white-box internal auditing framework designed to extract and causally manipulate latent knowledge within the residual stream. Applied to Minerva-7B-Instruct-v1.0 on 124 minimal prompt pairs over 12 categories of professional risk, behavioral evaluation fails on most of the set: the model acts identically on 63.7% of the pairs (95% CI [55.0%, 71.6%]), complying with or refusing both members. Yet, projecting the residual stream onto the vocabulary by a Jacobian lens reveals a statistically significant Contrastive Endognostic Margin, proving the model maintains robust risk differentiation internally. In a second protocol crossing 25 facts with five linguistic framings, we show that the model conforms to presupposed falsehoods in 72% of cases, despite representing the true entity in its latent layers. Surgically ablating the direction of the planted falsehood restores the correct answer in 11 of 25 suppressed cases (McNemar p = 0.0010), validated by blind human annotation (binary agreement kappa = 0.68). In contrast, an out-of-sample linear probe achieves 77% accuracy at layer 10, but its orthogonal ablation yields a 0% recovery rate. This establishes a fundamental theoretical dissociation: abstract linear representation does not imply causal control over verbalization. Our main contribution is the formalization of endognostics to prove that behavioral evaluation and internal reading systematically disagree in the common case, and that linear decodability is decoupled from causal control.

</details>


### [67] [PII-TRACE: A Benchmark for Context-Aware PII Detection in Multi-Turn LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22200)
*Zhang, Kaiyuan,Wang, Chuan,Zhong, Joey,Fryzel, Paul,Polley, Kyle,Ma, Jerry,Li, Ninghui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PII-TRACE是首个面向多轮LLM对话的PII检测基准，包含13种语言、13,148个合成对话及字符级标注与标识符聚类。评测发现十一个基线（含前沿LLM）均无法在低误报下实现全实体覆盖，单遍阅读会丢失长对话中约三分之一的PII字符。作者提出0.6B参数的PII-Tracer，以对话级监督训练，取得所评估系统中最高的实体级覆盖率，并在标准单条记录基准上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM对话中的PII检测需要跨轮次追踪同一标识符的所有提及，而现有检测器与基准主要面向自包含单条记录，导致长对话中遗漏大量PII字符。

Method: 摘要未给出PII-Tracer的详细架构，仅说明其是0.6B参数的紧凑检测器，采用对话级监督训练。PII-TRACE基准包含13,148个合成多轮对话，覆盖13种语言，提供字符级跨度与标识符聚类，用于评估检测器是否识别对话上下文中的PII并覆盖跨轮反复出现的同一标识符的所有提及。检测流程可理解为：输入多轮对话文本，模型在对话级别进行监督学习以输出PII字符级跨度，并关联跨轮同一标识符；训练目标为最大化实体级覆盖率并控制PII-free对话上的误报。推理时对完整对话进行扫描，输出检测到的PII片段用于后续脱敏。具体模块、数据流、损失函数与推理部署细节未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "实体级覆盖率", "value": "最高（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "PII-TRACE多轮对话基准，与十一个基线（含前沿LLM）对比"}, {"metric": "单遍阅读丢失的黄金PII字符比例", "value": "约三分之一", "setting": "长对话，单遍阅读基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建PII-TRACE基准：13,148个合成多轮对话、13种语言、字符级标注与标识符聚类，首次系统评测跨轮PII覆盖。；提出对话级监督训练的紧凑检测器PII-Tracer（0.6B参数），在实体级覆盖率上优于所评估的基线，同时保持单条记录基准上的强表现。

Abstract: LLM assistants and agentic systems log long multi-turn conversations. AI providers often scan these conversations for Personally Identifiable Information (PII) and mask the PII before storing or processing conversation data. Yet most PII detectors and benchmarks target self-contained records rather than cross-turn evaluation. To evaluate PII detection across turns in multi-turn conversations, we introduce PII-TRACE (Tracing Recurring PII Across Conversational Exchanges), to our knowledge the first PII benchmark to assess whether detectors identify PII in conversational contexts and cover every mention of a recurring identifier across turns. PII-TRACE contains 13,148 synthetic multi-turn dialogues in 13 languages with character-level spans and identifier clusters. Across eleven baselines, including frontier LLMs, no detector achieves full entity-level coverage without substantial false positives on PII-free conversations, and single-pass reading loses a third of the gold characters on long dialogues. To close this gap, we introduce PII-Tracer, a compact 0.6B-parameter detector trained with conversation-level supervision. PII-Tracer attains the highest entity-level coverage of any system we evaluate and also performs strongly on standard single-record benchmarks.

</details>

*Li, Jiakun,Song, Guowei,Li, Sijia,He, Xingwei,Chai, Hongzheng,Yuan, Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: MMSAFE 是一种多层级安全退化数据识别框架。其核心思想基于跨语言分析发现：安全敏感层仅部分共享，安全相关信号在多个层中分布。因此，框架同时捕获共享的安全信号和语言特定的安全信号，用于识别微调数据中可能导致安全对齐退化的样本。具体架构（根据摘要推断）包括：多层表示提取、安全信号检测、结合共享与语言特定信号的过滤决策。训练/推理流程未在摘要中详述。

Conclusion: 跨语言分析揭示安全敏感层仅部分共享，安全信号分布在多层；提出 MMSAFE 多层级安全退化数据识别框架，同时捕捉共享和语言特定的安全信号

Abstract: Preserving safety alignment during large language models fine-tuning is critical, however, recent studies have demonstrated that even benign fine-tuning data may contain safety-degrading samples that silently undermine safety alignment. Existing approaches typically identify such samples using representations from a single safety-sensitive layer. While this assumption has shown effectiveness in monolingual settings, its validity for multilingual models remains unclear due to potential cross-lingual differences in representation patterns. Through a cross-lingual analysis, we show that sensitive layers are only partially shared across languages, with safety-relevant signals often distributed across multiple layers. Motivated by these observations, we propose MMSAFE, a multi-layer framework for multilingual safety-degrading data identification that captures both shared and language-specific safety signals. Extensive experiments across multiple models, languages, and safety benchmarks demonstrate that MMSAFE reduces the average harmful-response ratio by 60% compared with random filtering and achieves stronger average performance than the strongest single-layer baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of multi-layer modeling for robust multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [69] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Ghaffari, Meysam,Fatehi, Nina,Sen, Bhaskar,Sabetpour, Nasim,Morato, Carlos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将长上下文模型中证据定位退化称为“上下文投毒”，以极值干扰分析注意力机制，推导出证据边际需随有效干扰数按Ω(√log N)增长的理论上界，并通过受控实验验证干扰数量与格式对检索准确率的影响。提出检索门控及多种缓解方向，对长上下文RAG与搜索中的候选生成具有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型在无关或易混淆上下文增加时，定位并利用决定性证据的能力下降；论文将其形式化为注意力中的极值干扰问题。

Method: 论文提出将长上下文中的证据定位退化形式化为注意力中的极值干扰问题：决定性证据的注意力分数存在上界，而有效干扰项的最大分数随干扰数量N增长，导致softmax检索时分母被大量干扰项主导。在softmax检索抽象下，作者推导有限样本上界，表明若要将准确率维持在基础率之上固定水平，证据边际需以Ω(√log N)增长。分析将退化机制归纳为分数混叠（不同语义的证据与干扰在表示空间难以区分）、位置混叠（位置编码或顺序位置无法有效区分证据与干扰）以及softmax稀释（大量干扰项分散注意力权重）。受控实验通过构造不同干扰类型、控制上下文长度与硬负样本数量，测量检索准确率变化，并评估检索门控模块在过滤干扰同时保留证据召回时的效果。基于这些发现，论文提出缓解方向：证据瓶颈（强制模型聚焦有限证据）、别名抗性表示（学习区分证据与干扰的表示）、检索后推理（先检索再对证据推理）、验证器介导的记忆（用验证器筛选记忆内容）以及对比反投毒训练（通过对比学习增强对干扰的鲁棒性）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索准确率下降", "value": "随总上下文增长而下降，在嵌入硬负样本时尤为明显", "setting": "受控实验，固定上下文长度下比较不同干扰构造"}, {"metric": "最大准确率下降", "value": "同格式条件在测试的干扰构造中产生最大观察到的准确率下降", "setting": "固定上下文长度的受控实验"}, {"metric": "检索门控收益", "value": "可改善证据使用，但净收益取决于是否保持证据召回", "setting": "受控实验中的检索门控评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将长上下文检索退化建模为极值干扰：证据分数被上界约束，而最大干扰分数随干扰数量增长。；在softmax检索抽象下推导有限样本上界，表明维持固定准确率需证据边际随有效干扰数N以Ω(√log N)增长。；将退化归因于分数混叠、位置混叠和softmax稀释三种机制。；通过受控实验验证上下文增长与硬负样本对检索准确率的影响，并测试检索门控的净收益。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $\Omega(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [70] [onPanda: Efficient Annotation of On-Policy Alignment Data for LLMs and Agents via Token-Level Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24983)
*Yang, Lei,Liu, Mengyin,Wang, Jia,Guo, Hangyu,Zhao, Liang,Ge, Zheng,An, Kang,Jiao, Binxing,Han, Qi,Jiang, Daxin,Shen, Siqi,Zhang, Xiangyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: onPanda是一款通过token级修正高效标注LLM对齐数据和agent轨迹的交互工具。标注者定位首个不当token并选择候选或自由编辑，系统截断后续并继续生成，循环直至满意。受控研究显示标注时间中位数比手动后编辑减少52%，产生的数据保留on-policy分布，提供精确位置的正负样本，并支持外部工具进行轨迹标注。另发布Panda-CVL数据集和基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效地标注LLM对齐数据和agent轨迹，同时保持模型采样分布并获取细粒度监督信号。

Method: onPanda是一个交互式标注工具，核心流程为：标注者阅读模型响应，定位第一个不合适的token；系统提供模型候选token供选择，或允许标注者自由输入正确文本；选择或输入后，系统截断该位置之后的所有内容，并从修正后的前缀继续生成，重复此locate-correct-continue循环直到获得满意响应。该机制使标注者以低成本的token级修正精确引导模型输出，且最终响应中绝大多数token由模型自身生成，因此数据保留模型采样分布，适合构建on-policy SFT和偏好数据。token级修正记录提供精确位置和自然配对的正负样本，用于细粒度监督。onPanda还连接外部工具和harness，支持真实环境中的交互式轨迹标注。系统发布Panda-CVL数据集及token级修正基准。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "标注时间中位数降低", "value": "52%", "setting": "受控研究，与手动后期编辑对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于token级修正的交互机制：定位首个不当token，从候选token中选择或自由编辑替换，截断后续生成并从修正前缀继续生成。；locate-correct-continue循环，让标注者以低成本精确引导模型输出，且最终响应大部分由模型生成，保持on-policy分布。；token级修正记录提供精确位置和自然配对的正负样本，用于细粒度监督。；支持连接外部工具和harness，实现真实环境中的交互式轨迹标注。；发布Panda-CVL数据集和token级修正基准。

Abstract: We present onPanda, an interactive tool for efficiently annotating LLM alignment data and agent trajectories. onPanda adopts token-level correction as its core interaction: while reading a model response, the annotator locates the first inappropriate token and either picks a substitute from the model's candidate tokens or types the correct text via free-form editing. The system then truncates everything after that position and continues generation from the corrected prefix, repeating this locate-correct-continue loop until a satisfactory response is obtained. This mechanism lets annotators precisely steer model outputs at low cost: a small controlled study suggests that onPanda reduces median annotation time by 52% over manual post-editing. Since the vast majority of tokens in the final response are generated by the model itself, the resulting data largely preserves the model's sampling distribution and is well suited for constructing on-policy SFT and preference data. Furthermore, the token-level corrections recorded during annotation provide fine-grained supervision with precise positions and naturally paired positive--negative samples. onPanda also connects to external tools and harnesses, enabling interactive trajectory annotation in realistic environments. In addition, we release Panda-CVL, a dataset annotated with onPanda, together with a benchmark for token-level correction.

</details>


### [71] [Written as a Record, Read as an Address: What a Forward Pass Leaves in an Operation's KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24635)
*Wu, Lingfeng,Shomali, Behzad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过冻结writer和可训练reader的框架，探究LLM前向传播在KV缓存中对操作token留下的记录。在合成boxes任务上，训练后reader可从缓存中恢复0.75-1.00的绑定，而基础reader仅≤0.06。研究发现操作token在中间层留下因果可恢复的路由记录，并存在直接payload访问，但模型主要读取地址而非值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在执行操作（如交换盒子内容）时，前向传播会将操作token的键值写入KV缓存；论文探究这些记录是什么以及如何被后续访问——即LLM是否将操作结果作为显式状态存储，还是仅存储可路由的地址。

Method: 论文提出一种双模块分析框架：冻结的writer和可训练的reader。writer是原始语言模型的前向传播，对操作span（如“Swap the contents of Box F and Box B”）的token计算键和值并写入KV缓存，该过程无梯度重算，保持未修改。reader被设计为仅接收指令和操作token（所有状态描述被隐藏），在隔离条件下训练以从writer的缓存中恢复绑定的实体-值关系。具体地，reader通过注意力机制访问writer产生的KV缓存，并学习查询哪些缓存条目以恢复被操作影响的状态。训练目标是最小化恢复绑定与真实绑定之间的差异。推理时，reader仅基于指令和操作token进行前向传播，不接收外部状态描述，其输出即为从缓存中恢复的绑定。作者通过操作span移植实验（将操作span的KV移植到另一个相同值的上下文中）验证因果路由效应，并定位直接payload访问的层带（Llama-3.1-8B的12-15层，Mistral-7B的14-17层）。该架构旨在区分模型是存储显式状态还是仅存储路由地址。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "查询绑定恢复率", "value": "0.75-1.00", "setting": "合成boxes任务，训练后reader"}, {"metric": "查询绑定恢复率", "value": "≤0.06", "setting": "合成boxes任务，基础reader"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将前向传播分离为冻结的writer和可训练的reader：writer的KV缓存无梯度重算，reader仅见指令和操作token，所有状态描述隐藏，从而保证reader恢复的信息原本就存在于未修改的缓存中；发现操作跨度移植可因果重定向可见状态的读取（即使两个世界值相同），揭示了路由记录的存在；隔离训练在保持路由的同时，添加了从单个操作数名称token直接访问payload的能力，该访问集中在狭窄的中层深度带（Llama-3.1-8B第12-15层/32层，Mistral-7B第14-17层/32层），且与路由记录位于同一位置；在合成boxes任务上，基础reader对查询绑定的恢复率≤0.06，训练后达到0.75-1.00，且可恢复性跟踪操作读写足迹；该recipe可扩展到更多操作、ToMi和GSM8K，但受限于训练覆盖范围，并以降低开卷准确率为代价

Abstract: When a language model reads an operation such as "Swap the contents of Box F and Box B", its forward pass writes keys and values for those tokens into the KV cache. Prior work on entity tracking establishes what models use: bindings are resolved at query time rather than stored as explicit latent state. We ask what they write at the operation span and how it is accessed. We split a forward pass into a frozen writer and a reader: the writer's cache is recomputed without gradients, while the reader sees only the instruction and operation tokens, with all state descriptions hidden, and is trained in isolation. Anything the reader recovers was therefore already present in the unmodified cache. On a synthetic boxes task, a base reader recovers $\leq 0.06$ of queried bindings against $0.75$--$1.00$ after training, and recoverability tracks the operation's read/write footprint. We find two modes of access. Across Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, operation-span transplants causally redirect which visible state is read even when the two worlds hold identical values, revealing a routing record. Isolation training preserves routing and adds direct access to the payload, the value the operation read, from the single operand-name token in a narrow mid-depth band (layers 12--15 of 32 in Llama-3.1-8B, 14--17 in Mistral-7B) --- the same site that holds the routing record. The same recipe extends to further operations, ToMi and GSM8K, but is bounded by training coverage and costs open-book accuracy. Operation tokens thus leave localized, causally recoverable records that support both routing and direct payload access, though the model that writes them reads mainly the address they carry and not the value.

</details>


### [72] [LoopCD: Loop-wise Contrastive Decoding for Improving Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24196)
*Yu, Byeongho,So, Junhyuk,Park, Eunhyeok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoopCD 针对 Looped Language Model 跨迭代细化的 loop instability 问题，提出一种免训练、低开销的 loop-wise 对比解码方法，通过对比早期与最终迭代 logits 修正推理关键 hard token，在多个推理任务上提升代表性 LoopLM 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoopLM 在迭代潜在细化过程中存在 loop instability：跨迭代的不稳定细化会产生局部的、不确定的 hard token，这些 token 与推理错误相关，从而损害推理性能。

Method: LoopCD 作用于 Looped Language Model 的推理阶段。LoopLM 使用共享权重递归地细化内部潜在表示，每一轮迭代产生一次 logits 分布。论文观察到跨迭代细化不稳定时，会在某些局部 token 上出现高不确定性，并与推理错误相关。LoopCD 利用该内部动态：在生成每个 token 时，同时获取较早迭代产生的 logits 与最后一个经细化的迭代产生的 logits，对二者进行对比（contrast），形成最终的采样分布。其目标是通过放大大模型在后续细化中相对早期迭代更确定的方向，自然修正推理关键 hard token 的采样倾向。该方法为纯推理时干预，不改变模型权重、不增加训练阶段，仅需在解码时保留并比较不同迭代的 logits。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现并刻画 LoopLM 的 loop instability 现象，将其与推理错误关联的 hard token 联系起来；提出 loop-wise contrastive decoding：对比早期迭代 logits 与最后细化迭代 logits 构造最终采样分布；无需额外训练、推理开销可忽略，即可在推理时干预并细化推理关键的 hard token

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) perform "latent reasoning" by recursively refining internal latent representations with shared weights, offering a more effective alternative to explicit verbal reasoning. Despite their effectiveness, we find that LoopLMs remain prone to loop instability: unstable refinement across iterations can produce localized uncertain "hard" tokens associated with reasoning errors. To address this, we propose LoopCD, loop-wise contrastive decoding that enhances the reasoning performance of LoopLMs by intervening on these tokens at inference time. Specifically, we exploit the internal dynamics of LoopLMs and contrast the logits from earlier iterations with logits from the last refined iteration to form the final sampling distribution. We find that this strategy is highly efficient, introducing only negligible inference overhead and requiring no additional training, while effectively improving reasoning performance by naturally refining reasoning-critical hard tokens. Extensive experiments show that our method improves the performance of recent representative LoopLMs across various reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [73] [Efficient LLM Distillation for Bangladesh Legal Context: A Smartphone-Compatible Retrieval-Augmented Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24177)
*Kamal, MD. Nafis,Fahim, Mahadi Hasan,Ridwan, Talha,Zaman, Nadifa,Florin, Fariha Roushon,Sadeque, Farig Yousuf,Ahmed, Saadat Rafid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出面向孟加拉国法律场景的端侧RAG系统：以两阶段渐进式知识蒸馏把9B Gemma-2教师压缩为2B学生，第一阶段用9,429条质量门控法律QA做SFT，第二阶段以QLoRA（4-bit NF4，rank-32）最小化对教师top-50 logits的稀疏KL损失（tau=4.0）。生成侧结合dense（60%）与BM25（40%）对36,029条成文法条文做混合检索。英文50题基准上ROUGE-L 0.4715、BERTScore F1 0.5679，较检索增强未蒸馏基线分别提升103%与143%；孟加拉语50题跨语言评测ROUGE-L 0.4083、BERTScore F1 0.8133。量化后GGUF约1.6GB，在Pixel 6上无网络运行4-8 tokens/s。单评审律师人工试点加权均分4.16/5。整体是一个可复用的LLM蒸馏+RAG+端侧部署工程范式，但领域偏垂直、评测规模小且无在线A/B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在移动网络不可靠且算力受限的孟加拉国场景中，把云端9B法律教师模型压缩为可在智能手机离线运行的2B学生模型，同时保证对英/孟双语查询的法条问答准确性与可溯源检索，避免幻觉法律文本造成直接伤害。

Method: 系统由检索与生成两部分组成，整体面向离线端侧。输入层：用户以英语或孟加拉语提出成文法解释类问题，问题先进入混合检索模块。检索模块：对36,029条孟加拉国宪法与国家立法条文建立索引，采用dense语义检索（权重60%）与BM25（权重40%）混合打分后返回相关法条，作为生成上下文。生成模块：学生模型为2B参数，底座为经两阶段渐进式知识蒸馏得到的模型。第一阶段在9,429条质量门控法律QA对上做监督微调（数据源自14,514条生成查询，接受率65%）。第二阶段以9B Gemma-2为教师，对学生施加稀疏KL散度损失，仅对齐教师每token的top-50 logits，温度tau=4.0，训练以QLoRA方式实现（4-bit NF4量化基座加rank-32 LoRA适配器）以降低显存开销。训练目标因此是SFT交叉熵与KL蒸馏损失的组合式两阶段优化。推理与部署：训练后的适配器量化为GGUF Q4_K_M（约1.6GB），部署在Pixel 6上，完全本地无网络运行，吞吐4-8 tokens/s；检索与生成均在设备侧完成，回答被要求严格基于检索到的法条，以降低幻觉法律文本的风险。评估路径包含英文50题、孟加拉语50题跨语言评测，以及执业律师对50条回答的人工评分试点。该架构以检索保证事实 grounding，以蒸馏+量化保证端侧可运行，从而在弱网/无网环境中提供可用的成文法问答。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ROUGE-L", "value": "0.4715 vs 0.2323（+103%）", "setting": "50条英文查询基准，蒸馏学生模型 vs 检索增强但未蒸馏的基线"}, {"metric": "BERTScore F1", "value": "0.5679 vs 0.2340（+143%）", "setting": "50条英文查询基准，蒸馏学生模型 vs 检索增强但未蒸馏的基线"}, {"metric": "ROUGE-L", "value": "0.4083", "setting": "50条孟加拉语查询跨语言评测，学生模型从孟加拉语输入检索英文语料"}, {"metric": "BERTScore F1", "value": "0.8133", "setting": "50条孟加拉语查询跨语言评测，学生模型从孟加拉语输入检索英文语料"}, {"metric": "法学人工评分", "value": "加权均值4.16/5，90%的响应被评为4或5分", "setting": "单一执业律师评审50条响应的试点，非在线A/B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段渐进式知识蒸馏流程：先对9,429条质量门控法律QA对做SFT，再用QLoRA以稀疏KL对准教师top-50 logits进行蒸馏；面向孟加拉国成文法而非通用英文法律的领域特化，并用孟加拉语查询检索英文法条语料实现跨语言RAG；端侧可部署的完整工艺：GGUF Q4_K_M量化到1.6GB，在Pixel 6上无网络4-8 tokens/s运行；混合检索策略：dense语义检索60% + BM25 40%覆盖36,029条成文法条文，每步回答均做检索 grounding；包含执业律师单评审试点（50条回答加权均分4.16/5，90%为4或5分）的人工可用性评估

Abstract: Legal information in Bangladesh is inaccessible to most citizens. Statutory text is English-only, trained lawyers are concentrated in urban centres, and cloud-dependent AI fails where mobile connectivity is unreliable, a setting in which hallucinated legal text causes direct harm. The system addresses statutory interpretation only; queries that require judicial precedent or case-law reasoning fall outside its scope. We target the statutory access gap by compressing a 9-billion-parameter Gemma-2 teacher into a 2-billion-parameter student through two-phase progressive knowledge distillation. Phase 1 performs supervised fine-tuning on 9,429 quality-gated legal question-answer pairs (65% acceptance from 14,514 generated queries); Phase 2 minimises sparse Kullback-Leibler divergence against the teacher's top-50 per-token logits at temperature tau = 4.0, implemented via QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank-32 LoRA adapters). Prior legal language models target general legal English; this system specialises in Bangladeshi statutory law. Every response is grounded through hybrid retrieval combining dense semantic search (60%) and BM25 (40%) across 36,029 statutory passages from the Bangladesh Constitution and national legislation. On a 50-query English benchmark, the distilled model reaches ROUGE-L 0.4715 and BERTScore F1 0.5679, a 103% ROUGE-L and 143% BERTScore gain over the retrieval-augmented undistilled baseline (ROUGE-L 0.2323, BERTScore 0.2340). The adapter quantises to 1.6 GB (GGUF Q4_K_M) and runs at 4-8 tokens per second on a Pixel 6 with no network access. Cross-lingual evaluation on 50 Bangla queries yields ROUGE-L 0.4083 and BERTScore 0.8133, showing effective retrieval from Bangla input against an English-only corpus. In a single-evaluator pilot, a practising lawyer rated 50 responses at a weighted mean of 4.16/5 (90% rated 4 or 5), supporting utility beyond text-overlap metrics.

</details>


### [74] [Re:CAP - Auditing Retrieval Coverage in Production RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24122)
*Joshi, Aviral,Bhathena, Hanoz,Nelson, Max,Sharma, Saket*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Re:CAP 提出一种无参考的检索覆盖率审计方法，通过迭代探测缺失文档并在生产 RAG 管线上验证，能恢复基线遗漏的 9-29% 金标文档（TREC-COVID 48%），并在生产流量中人类标注确认 73.9% 的新文档提供新信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产 RAG 中缺乏穷举相关性标注，难以监控检索覆盖率与发现缺失文档

Method: Re:CAP 是一个应用于已部署 RAG 管线的无参考审计循环。输入为初始答案和检索上下文。步骤：1）识别已覆盖话题；2）为可能缺失的话题生成探测问题；3）检索候选文档；4）使用 LLM-as-judge 仅保留引入先前未检索信息的文档。该循环迭代进行，旨在发现缺失文档的证据而非穷举所有相关文档。在四个公开基准和真实生产流量上验证，与 flat BM25、hybrid、ensemble 基线（top-500）对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "gold labels recovered vs flat BM25 top-500", "value": "9-29% (TREC-COVID 48%)", "setting": "四个公开基准"}, {"metric": "recall improvement vs flat hybrid top-500 on MuSiQue", "value": "+12.9 pp", "setting": "MuSiQue，文档预算不到一半"}, {"metric": "gold docs recovered vs ensemble BM25+dense+hybrid top-500", "value": "21.2% on TREC-COVID", "setting": "TREC-COVID"}, {"metric": "human annotators judge new information added", "value": "78.9% (Fleiss κ=0.79, n=123)", "setting": "公开基准，人工评测"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "human annotators judge new information added to baseline answer", "value": "73.9% (n=180)", "setting": "生产流量"}]}

Conclusion: 通过迭代探测缺失文档来审计检索覆盖率，而非枚举所有相关文档；无参考的审计循环：识别已覆盖主题、生成探测问题、检索候选文档、LLM-as-judge 过滤新信息；在生产 RAG 管线部署并验证，人类标注确认新增文档的信息价值

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is hard to monitor in production: exhaustive relevance labels do not exist for non-stationary multi-million-passage corpora that re-index in real time. As a result, retrieval quality is generally understudied and often deprioritised in favour of generation-oriented metrics. In this work, we propose auditing retrieval coverage by probing for evidence of missing documents rather than enumerating every relevant one. Our method Re:CAP (REtrieval Coverage Audit by iterative Probing) is a reference-free audit loop applied to a deployed RAG pipeline's initial answer and retrieved context: it identifies the topics already covered, generates probing questions for plausibly missing topics, retrieves candidate documents, and applies an LLM-as-judge to retain only those that introduce previously-unretrieved information. On four public benchmarks, Re:CAP recovers 9-29% of gold labels that flat BM25 top-500 cannot reach, rising to 48% on TREC-COVID. On MuSiQue Re:CAP beats flat hybrid top-500 by +12.9 pp on recall at less than half the document budget. An ensemble BM25, dense, and hybrid baseline (top-500 each) still leaves out 21.2% of gold docs on TREC-COVID that Re:CAP recovers; human annotators judge that 78.9% of those structurally distinct documents add new information to the baseline answer (Fleiss $\kappa$ = 0.79, n = 123), and 73.9% on live production traffic (n = 180). End-to-end recall is reproducible to within $\pm$1% across three independent runs, making Re:CAP a stable instrument for periodic retrieval audits.

</details>


### [75] [You Can Tell Who's Asking: What the Web's Questions Are Made Of, and Where They Come From](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24106)
*Zhou, Calvin,McCloskey, Vincent,Srinivasan, Krishna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对 2013-2025 年 110 个 FineWeb 快照中的 13.4B 个问题出现进行历时测量，发现网页问题形式携带来源信号（逻辑模型 AUC 0.725 区分真实与模板化问题，商业 FAQ 仅 0.554），最高频问题超 70% 为样板内容，且十二年间真实问题占比下降 79%（控制爬取构成后 42-56%）。结论是网络问题的可爬取性正从人类提问转向为机器阅读而制造，对以网页问答作为人类意图代理的 QA 训练数据、检索基准与内容策略提出警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络爬取的问题是网络是否反映真实人类提问意愿这一假设缺乏大规模实证检验；问题出现频次被当作需求代理是否可靠，以及随时间推移问题来源如何变化。

Method: 论文未提出新的神经网络架构，其端到端流程为测量与推断管道：输入是从 110 个 FineWeb 快照（2013-2025）中抽取的 13.4B 个问题出现（question occurrence），每个出现记录伴随其 host/page 来源与周围上下文；主要模块按处理顺序为（1）大规模问题抽取，从网页中识别并抽取问句形式的字符串及其页面来源，（2）来源信号建模，使用逻辑回归分类器，以问题长度和周围上下文（surrounding context）为特征，判断该问题出现是真实用户提问还是模板化/制造问题，而非依赖问题类型特征，（3）频次与需求解耦分析，统计最高频问题并检查其中样板/模板化比例，（4）历时分析，按年份比较真实问题出现占比、问题长度与上下文的变化，并额外控制爬取构成（crawl composition）以分离爬取变化与真实来源变化。训练目标为二分类（真实 vs 制造）的监督学习，论文未披露标注过程与数据划分细节。推理/服务路径未披露。整个管道旨在检验‘网页问题反映人类意图’这一假设，并给出问题来源信号与频次失真的可测量证据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUC（真实用户问题 vs 模板化/制造问题的分类）", "value": "0.725", "setting": "逻辑模型，仅使用长度和上下文特征，非问题类型特征"}, {"metric": "AUC（对商业 FAQ 写作的区分）", "value": "0.554", "setting": "逻辑模型，商业 FAQ 写作场景"}, {"metric": "最高频问题中样板/模板化问题占比", "value": ">70%", "setting": "前 1000 个最高频问题"}, {"metric": "真实问题出现占比下降", "value": "79%", "setting": "2013-2025 十二年间，未经爬取构成控制"}, {"metric": "真实问题出现占比下降（控制爬取构成后）", "value": "42-56%", "setting": "控制 crawl composition 后的估计区间"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次对网络问题进行历时、出现级别（occurrence-level）的来源测量，覆盖 13.4B 问题出现与 110 个 FineWeb 快照（2013-2025）；发现问题形式本身携带来源信号，逻辑回归模型仅凭长度和上下文（而非问题类型）即可区分真实用户问题与模板化/制造问题，AUC 0.725（对商业 FAQ 写作仅 0.554）；证明问题频次不代表需求：最高频问题中超过 70% 为样板/模板化问题；量化十二年间真实问题占比下降 79%（控制爬取构成后为 42-56%），问题长度和上下文同时缩短

Abstract: Questions scraped from the web are used across academia and industry as a proxy for what people want to know. Across QA training data, retrieval benchmarks, and content strategy, questions on a page are assumed to reflect human intent. We test this assumption at scale by extracting 13.4B question occurrences across 110 FineWeb snapshots (2013-2025), and report three findings. First, you can tell who is asking: provenance (the host/page of questions) leaves a signal in question form, and a logistic model can separate genuine user questions from templated/manufactured ones at AUC 0.725 via length and surrounding context rather than question type, though only 0.554 against commerce FAQ writing. Second, question frequency does not measure demand: the most-frequent questions are boilerplate/templated (over 70% of the top thousand), so occurrence counts measure how often a string was published and not how often it was asked. Third, over twelve years the genuine share of occurrences fell by 79% (42-56% after controlling for crawl composition), with question length and context decreasing. We present the first diachronic, occurrence-level measurement of web question provenance, and find the crawlable web's questions have shifted from being asked by humans toward manufactured for machines to read.

</details>


### [76] [From Tables to Quantified Statements: Evaluating LLM Inference Generation through Executable Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23966)
*Eida, Mai Mohamed,Anand, Gunjan,Singh, Ayush,Maskharashvili, Aleksandre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出STAT-TO-TEXT任务，评估LLM从统计表生成量化推断的忠实性，并创新性地使用可执行Python检查器验证真值条件，实验显示GPT-OSS-120B最忠实且覆盖度和多样性不降，人工标注验证了自动检查器可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从统计表格生成的自然语言推断可能在逻辑上不受结构化数据支持，缺乏可自动验证的评估方法。

Method: 论文提出STAT-TO-TEXT任务：输入为统计表格，输出为包含量化词（all, some, no, most）的自然语言推断。评估流程包含两个核心模块：首先，LLM生成推断文本；其次，另一个LLM生成Python检查器代码，该代码执行时针对表格验证推断的真值条件，输出布尔判断。通过比较四个开放权重LLM（跨模型家族和规模）在忠实性、逻辑准确性、表格覆盖度、多样性四个维度的表现。训练目标未涉及模型训练，而是专注于推理生成与验证。推理时，模型直接根据表格生成推断，然后通过生成的代码检查器自动评估。人工标注用于验证自动检查器的对齐程度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出STAT-TO-TEXT受控任务，要求LLM使用all、some、no、most等量化词从统计表生成量化自然语言推断。；利用LLM生成可执行的Python检查器代码，通过运行代码验证推断的真值条件，实现可执行验证。；对四种开放权重LLM跨模型家族和规模进行忠实性、逻辑准确性、表格覆盖度和多样性评估。；结合人工标注表明自动检查器与人类判断高度一致。

Abstract: LLMs can generate fluent descriptions from tables, but their outputs may remain logically unsupported by the structured data. We introduce STAT-TO-TEXT, a controlled task in which LLMs generate quantified natural language inferences from statistical tables using quantified constructions such as all, some, no, and most. To evaluate these inferences, we use an LLM generated Python checker code which when executed verifies the corresponding truth conditions against the table. We compare four open-weight LLMs across model families and scales, evaluating faithfulness, logical accuracy, table coverage, and diversity. Our results show that model scale and family matter, with the largest model (GPT-OSS-120B) consistently producing the most faithful inferences without sacrificing greater table coverage and quantifier diversity, as opposed to smaller models. These findings are supported by human annotation, which shows that the automated checker closely aligns with human judgments.

</details>


### [77] [Open-Jev Judgments on CallScreenBench: Calibrated One-Pass Scam Screening with a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23959)
*Ren, Simiao,Zewde, Kidus,Shen, Xingyu,Zhou, Yuchen,Ng, Dennis,Raj, Ankit,Duong, Tommy,Zhang, Yuxin,Tiangratanakul, Neo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 JevLite 读出头，将 Qwen3-4B 微调为诈骗概率校准器，在 CallScreenBench 上达到 AUROC 0.974、校准误差 0.052、零误报、每决策 64.5ms，比生成式方法快 4.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何用小型语言模型在每轮对话后快速输出经过校准的诈骗概率，同时保证低延迟、无误报并尽早决策

Method: 系统采用 Qwen3-4B 作为骨干，通过 LoRA 微调使其适应诈骗筛查任务。输入是每轮对话的文本（呼叫方话语），模型对预定义的答案标签（如“scam”和“not scam”）生成 logits。读出头仅取这两个标签对应的 logits，应用温度缩放 softmax 得到 P(scam)，无需生成文本。推理时，每个对话轮次后执行一次前向传播，输出单个概率；若概率超过阈值，则触发挂断规则。训练目标是最小化标签预测的交叉熵（隐式），并可能通过温度缩放优化校准。集成三个种子模型以提升性能。推理在单块消费级 GPU 上完成，每决策 64.5 ms。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.974", "setting": "CallScreenBench 41 个留出场景、577 个逐轮决策，三种子集成"}, {"metric": "校准误差", "value": "0.052", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "误报（合法呼叫）", "value": "0", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "决策提前轮数", "value": "1.14", "setting": "相同挂断规则下，相比基线？论文未明确基线，仅称提前"}, {"metric": "每决策延迟", "value": "64.5 ms", "setting": "单块消费级 GPU"}, {"metric": "延迟相对生成式微调", "value": "4.9x 更低", "setting": "相同骨干模型微调为生成答案时"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 采用类型化决策读出头（JevLite）：将声明的选项作为标签，仅对标签 logits 做温度缩放 softmax，单次前向得到概率，无需生成文本；将 Qwen3-4B 通过 LoRA 微调为二分类校准器，直接输出 P(scam)；在 CallScreenBench 上系统评估校准误差、误报和决策时机，并预注册与 LLM judge 的非劣性检验；三种子集成提升稳定性；量化每决策 64.5ms 延迟，比同骨干生成式微调快 4.9 倍

Abstract: Screening a phone call for fraud needs a trustworthy probability after every caller turn, in milliseconds. Jev-style typed decisions promise exactly that: declared options go in, one calibrated probability per option comes out of a single forward pass, with no generated text. We test an open implementation of this readout, JevLite, on scam-call screening: Qwen3-4B is LoRA-tuned so that the temperature-scaled softmax over two answer-label logits is P(scam). On 41 held-out CallScreenBench scenarios (577 per-turn decisions) a three-seed ensemble reaches AUROC .974 with calibration error .052, non-inferior to an LLM judge (MiniMax-M3) at a pre-registered .02 margin, with no false alarms on legitimate calls, decisions 1.14 turns earlier under the same hang-up rule, and 64.5 ms per decision on one consumer GPU, 4.9x lower than the same backbone fine-tuned to generate its answer. The gain is in the readout and calibration, not accuracy: a fine-tuned ModernBERT encoder is not significantly worse, the recipe was selected with test-set exposure, and all callers are synthetic. We claim no architectural novelty; the contribution is the application and an evaluation reporting calibration, false alarms and decision timing alongside AUROC.

</details>


### [78] [Time-Incremental Continued Pretraining of LLMs: Knowledge Updates Without Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23916)
*Ãncel, FÄ±rat,Ali, Salman Hussain,Ravanelli, Mirco,Subakan, Cem,YÄ±ldÄ±z, ÃaÄatay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在真实时间增量网页快照场景下系统研究LLM持续预训练：跨6个模型验证了知识可获取且无灾难性遗忘、13任务宏平均波动<0.01，并给出数据质量优先、LoRA可替代全量CPT、收益可经SFT传递等实用结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网页抓取快照存在大量URL重叠的现实时间增量场景下，LLM如何在不从头重训、不灾难性遗忘的前提下持续获取新知识，并让更新收益稳定传递到SFT/DPO后的部署模型。

Method: 论文在架构层面不引入新模块，而是对既有开放权重模型进行时间增量持续预训练。输入为FineWeb-Edu中严格晚于各模型知识截止日期的网页dump，数据流按时间递增方式依次继续预训练，快照之间保留设计上的大量URL重叠，以模拟真实网页抓取更新而非持续学习的disjoint流。训练目标仍为标准自回归语言建模，通过控制学习率、数据规模/质量与LoRA秩来观察知识获取与通用能力的权衡。评估分为两层：一是知识获取与遗忘测量，使用pre-cutoff与post-cutoff事实召回以及13任务套件宏平均；二是部署传递性测量，将CPT后的检查点继续做SFT与DPO，验证收益能否保留。推理路径与基座模型一致，无额外结构或服务开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "前后知识截止事实召回", "value": "6个模型中5个在pre-cutoff事实召回上也有提升", "setting": "FineWeb-Edu在各自知识截止日之后的dump上做CPT，13任务套件"}, {"metric": "13任务套件宏平均", "value": "所有模型与基座差异在0.01以内", "setting": "CPT后评估，说明通用能力几乎无损失"}, {"metric": "数据效率对比", "value": "精选6B token切片匹配更广的40B token数据", "setting": "知识获取效果对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将时间增量CPT建模为现实的重叠快照场景，而非传统持续学习的 disjoint 数据流假设；跨3个模型家族、4个参数规模（1B-3B-7B-8B）共6个开放权重模型的系统性实证；发现数据质量优于数量：精选6B token切片可匹配更广的40B token数据；发现知识获取与通用能力的最优学习率相差约一个数量级；证明足够秩的LoRA可匹配全量CPT；验证CPT收益可通过SFT传递，而DPO的效果依赖模型家族

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) drift out of date the moment their pretraining ends, yet retraining from scratch is prohibitively expensive. Continued pretraining (CPT) is the natural remedy, but it is typically evaluated through a continual learning lens that assumes disjoint data streams. This is a poor fit for time-incremental updates on web-scale crawls, where successive snapshots share substantial URL overlap by design. We study time-incremental CPT in this realistic regime: continued pretraining on FineWeb-Edu dumps drawn strictly from after each model's knowledge cutoff, evaluated across six open-weight models spanning three families (OLMo2, Llama-3.1/3.2, Gemma-3-1B) and four parameter scales (1B-3B-7B-8B). We organize our findings around four practical questions. (i) Is knowledge acquired? Yes, but heterogeneously, and without catastrophic forgetting: five of six models also improve on pre-cutoff factual recall, and the gains track pretraining saturation (driven primarily by token budget per parameter). (ii) What does it cost? Almost nothing: the macro-average across a thirteen-task suite stays within 0.01 of the base for every model. (iii) What is the recipe? Data quality dominates quantity (a curated 6B-token slice matches a broader 40B one); the optima for knowledge acquisition and general capability are separated by roughly an order of magnitude in learning rate; and LoRA at sufficient rank matches full CPT. (iv) Does it survive deployment? CPT gains transfer through SFT, while DPO's effect is family-dependent. Together, these results paint a more optimistic picture of time-incremental CPT than the prior continual learning literature suggests.

</details>


### [79] [this-that-model-1.0: A typed decision model that decides in 30 ms, for a millionth of a cent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23886)
*Cheng, Zehua,Dai, Wei,Sun, Jiahao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: this-that-model-1.0是一个2B参数型决策模型：在单次前向中从指定位置隐藏状态读取答案并限制在调用方声明的选项集合内，不生成任何文本token，同时给出可阈值化的分数，同一请求内多问题并行回答。单张笔记本GPU上每决策30.9 ms、单卡32 decisions/s，生成0 token；相比前沿API的8758 ms一次调用和托管系统每题21–212生成token，成本与延迟大幅下降。在第三方68题决策队列上得分0.941、Brier 0.042，优于托管服务Jev的0.765/0.133；内部分42族套件单遍32秒、电费0.000217 USD。作者也公开失败面：多步算术仅0.560（托管模型0.98–1.00），且定向二次训练只改善目标5个任务族、零迁移到其余13族。模型已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在保持可阈值化、结构化输出（n选1）的前提下，以极低延迟与成本完成高频决策，避免生成式前沿模型的数百毫秒往返、按token计费与解析失败风险。

Method: 输入侧：调用方以请求形式给出若干决策问题，并显式声明每题的n个候选选项集合。编码：问题与选项声明一起被编码进2B参数模型的输入序列，经Transformer前向传播（论文未披露具体层数、注意力变体、位置编码或词表细节）。读取：模型不在输出层生成文本，而是在一个指定位置上读取隐藏状态向量，并将该状态映射/约束到调用方声明的选项集合上，直接得到n选1的离散答案，同时给出一个数值分数供上层程序做阈值化；因此输出在结构上不可能格式错误。多问题并行：同一请求中的每个决策问题都在同一次前向传播中被回答，避免逐题往返。训练与优化：论文未系统披露训练目标与数据配方；文中提到一次针对5个目标任务族的第二轮训练，且该轮调整未迁移到其余13个任务族，提示其泛化边界。推理与服务：单张笔记本/消费级GPU上完成前向，30.9 ms每决策、32 decisions/s吞吐、生成0输出token，数据不出本机；相比前沿API调用8758 ms、托管系统每题生成21–212 token并按其计费。作者明确指出该架构的固有失败模式：单次前向无法携带中间结果，故多步算术仅0.560。该设计直接针对高频、需阈值化、需低延迟低成本的程序分支决策问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "score", "value": "0.941", "setting": "第三方录制的68题决策队列，使用其原始输入与措辞；对比托管服务Jev 0.765"}, {"metric": "Brier score", "value": "0.042", "setting": "同上68题队列；对比托管服务Jev 0.133"}, {"metric": "multi-step arithmetic score", "value": "0.560", "setting": "多步算术任务；对比托管模型0.98–1.00，作者将其列为失败场景"}, {"metric": "latency per decision", "value": "30.9 ms", "setting": "单张笔记本GPU；对比前沿API调用8758 ms"}, {"metric": "generated tokens per question", "value": "0", "setting": "本模型；对比托管系统21–212个生成token/问题并按其计费"}, {"metric": "throughput", "value": "32 decisions/s", "setting": "单张消费级GPU"}, {"metric": "内部分42族套件单遍耗时", "value": "32 s", "setting": "内部42族评测套件一遍；对比最准托管模型155.2分钟"}, {"metric": "内部分42族套件电费", "value": "0.000217 USD", "setting": "内部42族评测套件一遍；对比最准托管模型10.636 USD"}, {"metric": "二次训练迁移效果", "value": "0 of 13", "setting": "针对性第二轮训练改善了被针对的5个任务族，但对另外13个任务族零迁移"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: typed decision model：答案直接从指定位置的隐藏状态读取并限制在调用者声明的选项集合内，不生成任何token，输出不可能格式错误；同一请求中的多个问题在同一次前向传播中全部回答；零输出token的推理路径，单张消费级GPU即可运行且状态不离开本机；对多步算术等单次前向无法承载中间结果的失败模式进行公开报告，并展示定向二次训练只改善目标5个任务族、未迁移到其余13个

Abstract: Software delegates more of its branches to models every year: which queue a ticket enters, whether a command is safe to run, whether a claim clears without a person. What the program needs back is not prose. It is one of n declared options and a number it can threshold. Today that costs a round trip to a frontier model -- hundreds of milliseconds, a per-token bill, and a parser -- for a question that is usually a conjunction of three clauses. this-that-model-1.0 is a 2B-parameter typed decision model. Its answer is read directly from the hidden state at a designated position and restricted to the option set the caller declared, so no text is generated, nothing can be malformed, and every question in a request is answered in the same forward pass. It decides in 30.9 ms on one laptop GPU and generates zero output tokens doing it, where a frontier API call costs 8758 ms and the hosted systems that answer these questions well spend between 21 and 212 generated tokens per question thinking first, billed for every one. It sustains 32 decisions per second on one consumer GPU and never lets the state leave the machine. On a third party's recorded cohort of 68 decision questions, on their inputs and their wording, it scores 0.941 with a Brier score of 0.042, against 0.765 and 0.133 for the hosted service Jev on the same items. One pass of our 42-family internal suite takes 32 seconds and 0.000217 USD of electricity; the most accurate hosted model we measured needs 155.2 minutes and 10.636 USD. We also report where it loses. On multi-step arithmetic, which a single forward pass cannot carry intermediate results through, it scores 0.560 against their 0.98 to 1.00, and a targeted second training round improved the five task families it was written for and transferred to none of the other 13. The model is open-sourced in https://huggingface.co/flock-io/this-that-model-1.0

</details>


### [80] [Constrained Decoding Eliminates Structural Failures in Small LLMs but Reveals a Scale-Dependent Semantic Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23742)
*Chavan, Akash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在5个小型LLM上系统评测了约束解码对结构化输出生成的影响，发现CD能完全消除结构失败（schema有效性从78.6-92.9%提升至100%），但无法解决因模型规模不足导致的语义差距，首次清晰界定CD的作用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束解码（CD）如何与0.6B-4B参数范围内的小型LLM的模型规模相互作用，以及CD能否弥补因规模不足导致的结构与语义失败。

Method: 论文对小型LLM（0.6B-4B）在结构化输出生成任务上进行了基准测试。输入为自然语言指令及期望的输出模式（例如JSON schema或函数签名），通过三种解码方式生成输出：原生解码、Outlines约束解码、XGrammar约束解码。输出被评估为结构正确性（是否匹配模式）和语义正确性（内容是否符合指令意图，包括类型强制转换、多步函数调用等）。评估覆盖14个结构化输出任务和来自三个家族的五个模型。主要发现有两点：约束解码可消除所有结构失败，但对指令语义失败无效。论文未披露训练细节、模型标识、推理硬件或延迟数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "schema有效性", "value": "100%", "setting": "CD（Outlines或XGrammar）在所有5个模型、14个任务上"}, {"metric": "schema有效性（原生解码基线）", "value": "78.6-92.9%", "setting": "原生解码，所有5个模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出双轴评估方法，将结构正确性（schema有效性）与语义正确性（内容准确性）分离评估。；在5个模型（来自3个家族）和14个结构化输出任务上，系统对比原生解码、Outlines和XGrammar三种约束解码条件。；发现类型强制转换失败可被CD完全挽救，而指令语义失败（如多步函数调用）对CD具有抗性，且语义差距具有规模依赖性。

Abstract: Small open-source large language models (LLMs) in the 0.6B-4B parameter range are increasingly deployed for structured output generation (JSON, function calling, data extraction), yet little is known about how constrained decoding (CD) interacts with model scale in this regime. We benchmark five models from three families across 14 structured-output tasks under three decoding conditions (native, Outlines, XGrammar). We introduce a two-axis evaluation that separates structural correctness (schema validity) from semantic correctness (content accuracy). We find that CD eliminates all structural failures across all models (schema validity: 78.6-92.9% to 100%), but content accuracy reveals a persistent semantic gap that is scale-dependent: type coercion failures are fully CD-rescuable, while instruction-semantic failures (e.g., multi-step function calling) remain CD-resistant. Schema conformance is necessary but not sufficient for semantic correctness; CD's reach ends exactly where schema conformance ends.

</details>


### [81] [BabelArena: A Large-Scale Multilingual Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23490)
*Kuang, Peng,Fan, Yuchun,Li, Jiangnan,Wu, Minghao,Tang, Jialong,Wei, Hao-Ran,Wang, Weixuan,Tu, Jianhong,Yang, Baosong,Xiao, Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出BabelFlow工作流和BabelArena多语言LLM Agent基准，覆盖23种语言、16,146个实例，评估五个前沿模型，发现低资源语言中Agent工具使用和控制流错误更多、token消耗更高且语言一致性差，为多语言Agent的可靠性和效率研究提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent评估以英语为中心，缺乏多语言环境下Agent任务执行能力的系统评测，导致对低资源语言中Agent的失败模式和效率差距理解不足。

Method: BabelFlow是一个基准通用的Agent工作流，用于将现有Agent基准适配到新语言。其核心包括：1）运行时依赖分析：分析原始基准中任务执行所依赖的组件（如工具调用、环境交互、评估逻辑），确保翻译后这些依赖得到保留；2）结构保持翻译：对任务描述、工具定义和评估标准进行翻译，同时保持原有任务结构和语义；3）多层验证与人工审核：通过自动验证（如语义一致性检查）和人工审核确保翻译后的任务与原始任务在任务和评估语义上等价。使用BabelFlow构建了BabelArena基准，包含16,146个实例，源自702个规范任务，覆盖4个基准家族、13个领域和23种语言。实验使用五个前沿LLM模型，在BabelArena上执行多步工具调用和交互任务，评估任务成功率、工具使用错误、控制流错误、token消耗和语言一致性。结果表明没有单一模型在所有基准家族上占优，低资源语言中工具使用和控制流错误占比更高，且token消耗显著增加（最多约英语输入的两倍），语言一致性在结构化输出任务中进一步下降，切换方向 overwhelmingly 指向英语。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: BabelFlow：一种基准通用的Agent工作流，通过运行时依赖分析、结构保持翻译和多层验证加人工审核，将现有Agent基准适配到新语言，保持任务和评估语义。；BabelArena：包含16,146个实例的基准，源自702个规范任务，覆盖4个基准家族、13个领域、23种语言。；多维度评估揭示跨语言差异：低资源语言中工具使用和控制流错误占比更高，且token消耗显著增加（最多约英语输入的两倍），语言一致性下降，结构化输出任务中切换回英语现象严重。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly execute multi-step workflows through tool use and interaction with users and environments. However, current agent evaluations are largely English-centric, limiting our understanding of agent capabilities in multilingual settings. We introduce BabelFlow, a benchmark-general agentic workflow that adapts existing agent benchmarks to new languages by analyzing runtime dependencies, coordinating structure-preserving translation, and combining multi-layer verification with human review to preserve task and evaluation semantics. Using BabelFlow, we construct BabelArena, a task-aligned benchmark comprising 16,146 instances derived from 702 canonical tasks across four benchmark families, 13 domains, and 23 languages. Experiments with five frontier models show that no single model dominates across benchmark families and that cross-language disparities extend well beyond task success. Lower-resource languages exhibit distinct failure patterns, with larger shares of tool-use and control-flow errors rather than answer-quality errors alone, pointing to gaps in reliable task execution across the resource levels of these languages. On the same tasks, agents in low-resource languages also consume substantially more tokens than in English (up to roughly twice the input) without proportional increases in interaction length, and language consistency degrades further on tasks requiring structured output, where switches are directed overwhelmingly toward English. We believe BabelArena provides a foundation for advancing research on reliable and efficient multilingual agents.

</details>


### [82] [Machine-Interpretable Information: Compiling Documents into Searchable and Readable Protocol States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23371)
*Wang, Yifan,Dou, Dejing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MII提出文档到状态的编译协议，将文档压缩为56-token固定带宽状态，并通过Translator映射到任意冻结Reader，实现跨模型检索与推理。在HotpotQA上，Residual-MII以约7%注意力FLOPs超越全上下文EM，展示了可转移神经文档格式的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强系统中，密集向量支持高效检索但模型仍需重新摄入长文本载荷进行推理，导致O(N^2)注意力成本；现有压缩方法产生与特定架构绑定的私有状态，无法跨模型复用。

Method: MII采用双时间尺度状态空间Writer将输入文档编译为规范固定带宽状态（56 tokens）。编译后的状态可通过轻量级Translator映射到任何冻结Reader模型的嵌入空间，实现跨异构LLM（如Llama、Qwen、Mistral）的互操作。Writer使用传统GPT-2词汇表，迫使翻译基于语义而非token级记忆。生成的.mii工件同时支持检索（可搜索几何）、推理（全局记忆）和重建（接地细节）。为缓解固定带宽下的词汇重建困难，Residual-MII引入缓存层次，将编译的全局记忆与稀疏局部证据结合。在推理时，Reader模型接收映射后的状态，避免重新摄入完整文档，从而将注意力成本从O(N^2)降至O(K)。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Exact Match", "value": "超过全上下文EM", "setting": "HotpotQA (7,405 queries)，使用Residual-MII，消耗约7%的注意力FLOPs"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Machine-Interpretable Information (MII)，首个agent-to-agent文档到状态协议，将文档编译为固定带宽56-token的规范状态。；双时间尺度状态空间Writer，将文档编译为规范状态，并通过轻量级Translator映射到任意冻结Reader的嵌入空间，实现跨模型互操作。；.mii工件统一检索（可搜索几何）、推理（全局记忆）和重建（接地细节）于单一可转移媒介。；提出Residual-MII，一种缓存层次结构，结合编译的全局记忆与稀疏局部证据，解决固定带宽下的词汇重建问题。；机制探针揭示模块化潜在结构：实体表示可因果追踪并零样本移植到无关文档状态。

Abstract: Long-context language models interface with external knowledge through raw natural language. In retrieval-augmented systems, this creates a persistent index-payload schism: dense vectors enable searchable routing, but models must re-ingest lengthy text payloads for reasoning at O(N^2) attention cost. Existing compression methods further produce private states tied to specific architectures. We introduce Machine-Interpretable Information (MII), the first agent-to-agent (A2A) document-to-state protocol. A dual-timescale state-space Writer compiles documents into a canonical, fixed-bandwidth state (56 tokens), and a lightweight Translator maps it into any frozen Reader's embedding space, reducing query-time cost to O(K). The resulting .mii artifact unifies Retrieval (searchable geometry), Reasoning (global memory), and Reconstruction (grounded details) in a single transferable medium. We demonstrate strong cross-model interoperability across heterogeneous LLMs (e.g., Llama, Qwen, Mistral) -- despite the Writer using a legacy GPT-2 vocabulary, forcing genuine semantic translation rather than token-level memorization. Mechanistic probes reveal modular latent structure: entity representations can be causally traced and zero-shot transplanted between unrelated document states while remaining decodable. To address lexical reconstruction under fixed bandwidth, we propose Residual-MII, a cache hierarchy combining compiled global memory with sparse local evidence. On HotpotQA (7,405 queries), Residual-MII exceeds full-context Exact Match at approximately 7% of the attention FLOPs, suggesting a paradigm shift toward compiled, transferable neural document formats.

</details>


### [83] [ChemCLIR-Bench: Benchmarking Cross-Lingual Information Retrieval in Multilingual Chemical Patents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23231)
*Astaraki, Mahdi,Khodadad, Mohammad,Namazi, Reza,Saloot, Mohammad Arshi,Moghadam, Amir Reza Behzad,Mahyar, Hamidreza,Samiee, Soheila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建了覆盖五种语言的化学专利跨语言检索基准（数据来自 Google Patents 与 EPO），系统评测八种嵌入模型，发现最佳模型 Recall@10 从单语的 0.72 降至跨语言的 0.53，并揭示检索深度退化问题，为 CLIR 模型选型与鲁棒性诊断提供受控评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法充分刻画领域特定的跨语言检索，以及聚合召回率所掩盖的检索深度不足与可恢复性失败问题。

Method: 论文构建了面向化学专利的跨语言检索基准。数据来源为 Google Patents 与欧洲专利局（EPO），覆盖五种语言，涵盖主要东方与西方语言，以反映真实工业文档的多样性与复杂性。基于该数据集，论文对八种最先进的多语言嵌入模型进行系统性跨语言检索评测。评测流程包含：将查询与文档分别编码为嵌入向量，在跨语言与单语两种设置下进行向量检索并计算 Recall@10 等指标，同时分析检索深度（相关文档的排名位置）与可恢复性。论文未披露具体模型架构细节、训练目标、推理资源或服务路径；其贡献在于基准、评测协议与诊断分析，而非提出新的检索模型架构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Recall@10", "value": "0.72（单语）对 0.53（跨语言）", "setting": "最佳嵌入模型，单语与跨语言检索对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建覆盖五种语言、来自 Google Patents 与 EPO 的多语言化学专利检索数据集；系统评测八种最先进嵌入模型在单语与跨语言设置下的检索表现；揭示跨语言场景下的检索深度退化与可恢复性问题，而非仅用聚合召回率衡量；提供面向工业技术文本的受控 CLIR 诊断评测框架

Abstract: Cross-lingual information retrieval (CLIR) is increasingly important in multi-national industries, where critical technical evidence may exist in a different language than the query. However, existing benchmarks do not adequately capture domain-specific cross-lingual retrieval or the retrieval-depth and recoverability failures that aggregate recall hides. In this work, we benchmark CLIR in the chemical domain, with a focus on patent data. We construct a multilingual dataset from Google Patents and the European Patent Office (EPO) data, spanning five languages (covering major Eastern and Western languages) and reflecting the diversity and complexity of real-world industrial documentation. Using this dataset, we systematically evaluate eight state-of-the-art embedding models for cross-lingual retrieval. Our results show a substantial performance gap between monolingual and cross-lingual settings: for the best-performing model, Recall@10 drops from 0.72 to 0.53 in cross-lingual setting. Retrieval depth also degrades significantly, with relevant documents ranked lower across languages in cross-lingual scenarios. Furthermore, some multilingual embedding models that perform strongly in monolingual settings exhibit sharp declines when queries and documents are in different languages, providing practical insights for model selection in cross-lingual use cases. These findings highlight critical limitations of current approaches and emphasize the need for more robust cross-lingual retrieval methods in domain-specific settings. Our benchmark provides actionable insights for model selection and establishes a controlled diagnostic evaluation framework for CLIR over industrial technical text. Data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/MohammadKhodadad/Multi-Lingual-QAC.

</details>


### [84] [Euston: Training Away Mathematical Sycophancy Without Losing the Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23205)
*Cheng, Zehua,Dai, Wei,Sun, Jiahao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Euston是一个8B数学声明验证模型，通过GraphSynth合成的真假陈述对和规则奖励GRPO微调，显著提升了对错误定理的拒绝能力（平衡准确率29.5%→63.75%），同时未显著损害AIME数学能力，且减少了响应长度和截断率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强推理模型在面对被篡改的数学定理时会自信地生成错误证明（数学谄媚），且现有训练目标偏向生成而非拒绝。

Method: Euston基于DeepSeek-R1-8B微调。训练数据通过GraphSynth生成：该生成器使用概率因子图表示数学陈述，在解码时应用结构掩码并同步跨度验证，生成属性级多样且严格配对的真/腐化陈述对。使用GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行后训练，奖励为基于规则的零API奖励，不调用外部模型。训练在4张H100 GPU上运行189步。推理时，模型接收声明并输出判断。评估在200真/200假的平衡留出集以及AIME 2026官方语义集上进行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平衡准确率", "value": "29.50% -> 63.75%", "setting": "200真/200假平衡留出集"}, {"metric": "区分差距", "value": "-0.5% (z=-0.1) -> +27.5% (z=+6.0)", "setting": "同上留出集"}, {"metric": "AIME 2026准确率", "value": "69.17% -> 65.00% (差异-4.17%，不显著)", "setting": "官方语义评测"}, {"metric": "中位响应长度", "value": "19217 -> 18296 tokens", "setting": "评估响应"}, {"metric": "截断率", "value": "25.8% -> 8.3%", "setting": "评估响应"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: GraphSynth概率因子图生成器：耦合属性级多样性与解码时结构掩码与跨度同步验证，生成匹配的真/假陈述对；基于规则、零API的GRPO奖励用于数学声明验证的拒绝训练；揭示拒绝能力提升与通用数学能力保持之间的权衡（小语料训练崩溃现象）

Abstract: Reasoning language models are trained to produce solutions, not to refuse them, and this bias persists when the problem they are handed is false. Asked to prove a corrupted theorem, a strong model will typically comply and produce a confident derivation of something untrue. We present Euston, an 8B mathematical claim-verification model trained to resist exactly this. Training data were generated with GraphSynth, a probabilistic factor-graph generator that couples attribute-level diversity to decode-time structural masking and span-synchronized verification, yielding 3{,}026 matched true/corrupted statement pairs (6,052 statements) drawn from arXiv papers spanning 2010--2025. We fine-tuned DeepSeek-R1-8B with GRPO under a rule-based, zero-API reward for 189 steps on four H100 GPUs. On a balanced 200-true/200-false held-out split, balanced accuracy rises from 29.50% to 63.75% and the discrimination gap---the difference between the rate of calling false statements false and the rate of calling true statements false moves from -0.5% (z=-0.1) to +27.5% (z=+6.0). Critically, the gain is not purchased with general mathematical ability: AIME 2026 accuracy under official semantics is 65.00% against a 69.17% base, a difference of -4.17% that is not statistically significant, whereas an earlier run of the same recipe on a smaller GraphSynth corpus collapsed to 40.00%. Median response length also falls from 19,217 to 18,296 tokens and the truncation rate from 25.8% to 8.3%, so the improvement does not come from thinking longer. We report the result together with the confounds that bound its interpretation, principally the all-false composition of the official evaluation sets and the low precision implied at realistic error prevalence.

</details>


### [85] [NLPCC 2026 Task 10: Citation-Level Faithfulness Verification with DeBERTa Ensembles and Class-Wise Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22774)
*Li, Yanling,Li, Zirui,Wan, Mingyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文介绍了NLPCC 2026共享任务Track 2的参赛系统，利用DeBERTa集成与类级校准进行引文忠实性验证。系统在最终排行榜上取得82.9898总分（Macro-F1 89.5491，Joint@3 76.4305），排名第二。消融与错误分析表明模型互补性与校准是标签性能提升的关键，而证据排序对Joint@3指标有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无外部检索与LLM提示的离线条件下，对科学声明与引用论文段落进行四分类关系判断并排序出最多三个证据段落，提升整体忠实性验证性能。

Method: 系统包含两个子任务头：标签头与证据头。标签头以段落感知交叉编码器（输入为原子声明与单个段落拼接）和文档级DeBERTa-large分类器（输入为原子声明与文档全文）为基模型，取两者概率输出进行概率级融合，融合动机源于折外预测显示单一模型倾向于过度预测Topical Match类别，融合后使用类级决策校准以修正各类别阈值。证据头则结合来自top-20和top-30联合模型的段落得分与BM25得分，对证据段落进行排序，最终输出最多三个段落ID。整个流程完全离线，不依赖外部检索或LLM提示。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 段落感知交叉编码器与文档级DeBERTa-large分类器的标签头集成，基于折外预测偏差进行概率级融合，以缓解对Topical Match的过度预测。；类级决策校准方法，用于调整标签分类阈值。；证据头融合top-20与top-30联合模型段落分数及BM25分数进行证据段落排序。
