Abstract: This paper presents our system for Track 2 of the NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 10 on citation-level faithfulness in AI-assisted scientific reporting. Given an atomic scientific claim and the structured full text of its cited paper, the task requires both a four-way relation label and up to three evidence paragraph identifiers. The label head ensembles a paragraph-aware cross-encoder with a document-level DeBERTa-large classifier, followed by class-wise decision calibration. Probability-level fusion is motivated by an out-of-fold tendency to over-predict Topical Match. The evidence head combines paragraph scores from top-20 and top-30 joint models with BM25 scores. The system runs fully offline without external retrieval or LLM prompting. On the final leaderboard, our system achieved 82.9898 overall (89.5491 Macro-F1 and 76.4305 Joint@3), ranking second in Track 2. Ablations and error analysis show that model complementarity and calibration drive the label gains. Gold-evidence inference changes label Macro-F1 negligibly, whereas evidence ranking remains important for Joint@3.

</details>


### [86] [Seeing Through Conflicts: Improving Instruction Hierarchy Alignment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22234)
*Sansoterra, Nicholas,Zheng, Zishuo,Kumar, Sachin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型的指令层级对齐问题，将多模态指令冲突视为推理任务，采用基于规则奖励的强化学习进行训练。实验表明，纯文本监督部分迁移到多模态攻击，图像监督更鲁棒，混合模态监督效果最佳，且能泛化到真实图像和Web agent安全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在输入存在跨模态冲突时，难以正确遵循指令层级（即优先执行更高层级指令），尤其是在指令嵌入图像、跨模态拆分、视觉变换或Web agent交互场景中。

Method: 论文未公开具体的模型架构细节。根据摘要可知，作者使用强化学习（RL）训练视觉语言模型，奖励函数基于规则（rule-based rewards）。训练数据包含纯文本、纯图像以及混合模态的指令层级冲突样本。模型需要在这些冲突场景下学会优先执行更高层级指令。评估覆盖合成排版攻击、真实图像攻击以及Web agent安全任务，并测试了通用多模态能力的保持情况。具体网络结构、参数规模、训练数据量及硬件资源均未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将多模态指令层级对齐形式化为推理问题，并采用基于规则奖励的强化学习（RL）进行训练；系统对比了纯文本、纯图像和混合模态监督对多模态攻击鲁棒性的影响；验证了图像监督优于纯文本监督，混合模态监督效果最佳；证明了在合成排版训练上获得的鲁棒性可泛化到真实图像和Web agent安全任务，同时基本保持通用多模态能力

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy (IH) alignment teaches language models to prioritize higher-level instructions when inputs conflict. While studied primarily in text-only settings, vision-language models (VLMs) introduce new challenges for IH: instructions may be embedded in images, split across modalities, visually transformed, or encountered during agentic tasks. Positing multimodal IH alignment as a reasoning problem, we train VLMs using reinforcement learning with rule-based rewards, comparing text-only, image-only, and mixed-modality supervision. We find that text-only IH training partially transfers to multimodal attacks, failing when models must decode, reconstruct, or reason over instructions across modalities. Image-based training improves robustness beyond text-only supervision, while mixed-modality training performs best overall. Importantly, the benefits generalize beyond the synthetic typographic training setting to real-image and web-agent safety tasks, while largely preserving general multimodal capability, showing that lightweight, verifiable supervision can meaningfully improve VLM robustness under adversarial, cross-modal, and interactive instruction conflicts.

</details>


### [87] [EvalMem: An Operation-Level Diagnostic Framework for Long-Term Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22231)
*Liu, Zeyu,Zhong, Jian,Han, Rongduo,Wu, Ziyang,Tang, Shunye,He, Chenghao,Yang, Yaxuan,Qiu, Yihang,Wang, Ailing,Liang, Xiao,Xie, Guohuan,Xue, Xiaokang,Li, Gongchen,Zhang, Haining,Wang, Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvalMem将长期记忆系统的端到端QA错误拆解为编码、检索、生成三层缺陷码，定位检索为主要失败层，并用召回优先检索与MemWiki辅助结构提升召回与准确率；对搜索/推荐系统的检索召回诊断与离线评估体系设计有直接参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统评估只报告端到端QA准确率，无法定位错误究竟来自记忆编码、检索还是生成，导致系统优化缺乏可操作的诊断信号。

Method: EvalMem以每条查询为诊断单元，构建三个并行Examiner形成端到端归因链路。输入为各记忆系统的记忆存储导出与查询；Encoding Examiner检查目标事实是否已被写入记忆存储；Retrieval Examiner调用系统原生检索器，判断其是否返回可用证据；Generation Examiner在给定oracle证据的条件下测试模型能否正确回答。三路输出组合成细粒度多标签缺陷码，从而把端到端QA错误分解为编码、检索、生成三类。为提升存储级诊断可靠性，框架将agentic RAG改为召回优先：检索时同时使用原始查询与源证据文本构造检索输入，扩大已存证据的命中范围。诊断结果指导构建MemWiki——一种从每个系统的记忆导出中抽取的搜索友好辅助结构，用于补强检索层。评估覆盖LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S与动态DynaMem-Bench上的七个记忆系统，以缺陷码分布作为优化依据而非仅看最终准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "已存证据召回率（LoCoMo）", "value": "70.2% → 95.6%", "setting": "采用召回优先agentic RAG，使用查询与源证据联合检索"}, {"metric": "平均准确率（LoCoMo）", "value": "+2.5个百分点", "setting": "引入MemWiki辅助结构"}, {"metric": "平均准确率（LongMemEval-S）", "value": "+2.3个百分点", "setting": "引入MemWiki辅助结构"}, {"metric": "默认LoCoMo上检索缺陷占比", "value": "22.1%（编码7.7%，生成6.5%）", "setting": "七个记忆系统的EvalMem诊断统计"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出EvalMem操作级诊断框架，含Encoding Examiner、Retrieval Examiner、Generation Examiner三个并行检查器，为每个查询输出细粒度多标签缺陷码；将agentic RAG改造为召回优先（recall-first）策略，同时用查询与源证据进行检索，提高已存证据的召回；基于各系统记忆导出的MemWiki搜索友好辅助结构，针对性改善检索缺陷；跨LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S、动态DynaMem-Bench对七个记忆系统开展分层失败归因分析

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions with LLM-based assistants require memory systems that preserve and update user states, preferences, and interaction histories. Existing evaluations report end-to-end QA accuracy and cannot determine whether errors arise from encoding, retrieval, or generation. We introduce EvalMem, an operation-level diagnostic framework with three parallel Examiners. For each query, the Encoding Examiner checks whether the target fact is stored, the Retrieval Examiner assesses whether the native retriever returns usable evidence, and the Generation Examiner tests whether the model can answer from oracle evidence. Their outputs form fine-grained multi-label defect codes. To improve store-level diagnosis, we adapt agentic RAG with a recall-first strategy that searches using both the query and source evidence, increasing recall of present evidence on LoCoMo from 70.2% to 95.6%. Evaluations of seven memory systems on LoCoMo, LongMemEval-S, and dynamic DynaMem-Bench identify retrieval as the most frequently attributed failure layer; in default LoCoMo, retrieval defects reach 22.1%, compared with 7.7% for encoding and 6.5% for generation. Guided by this diagnosis, MemWiki, a search-friendly auxiliary structure built from each system's memory export, improves mean accuracy by 2.5 and 2.3 percentage points on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, respectively.

</details>


### [88] [Assessing Adversarial Robustness of Latent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22228)
*Chen, Shaolong,Li, Ang,Li, Mingjie,Wang, Yisen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了潜空间推理模型（LRM）在文本和多模态设定下的对抗鲁棒性，覆盖 8 个模型和 6 个基准。发现 LRM 通常比显式 CoT 更不鲁棒，白盒攻击下退化严重，且不同模态呈现不同失败模式。研究强调在设计隐式推理系统时需同时考虑效率与鲁棒性，并开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间推理模型（LRM）在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性如何，以及与显式思维链（CoT）基线相比的优劣。

Method: 论文评估潜空间推理模型（LRM），其核心思想是将中间推理步骤压缩为少量连续潜向量，而非显式生成长思维链（CoT）文本。输入为文本或多模态数据，模型通过自回归或非自回归方式生成潜向量序列，再解码出最终答案。评估涵盖对抗扰动（白盒与黑盒）下的鲁棒性，对比显式 CoT 基线。分析表明文本潜状态动态脆弱，多模态潜状态对扰动不敏感且对预测影响有限。未披露具体模型架构、训练目标或推理细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次系统评估潜空间推理模型在文本与多模态设定下的对抗鲁棒性，覆盖 8 个模型、6 个基准。；发现 LRM 普遍比显式 CoT 基线更不鲁棒，白盒攻击下退化尤为严重。；揭示跨模态的失败模式差异：文本潜状态动态脆弱、对特定输入模式高度敏感；多模态潜状态对输入扰动基本不变且对最终预测影响有限。；开源代码以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories for complex reasoning, but autoregressive generation brings substantial memory and inference costs. Latent reasoning models (LRMs) offer a more efficient alternative by compressing intermediate reasoning into a small number of continuous latent vectors. Despite their efficiency, however, the adversarial robustness of LRMs remains largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate the robustness of latent reasoning across textual and multimodal settings, covering eight models and six benchmarks. We find that, across our evaluated settings, LRMs are generally less robust than explicit CoT baselines under adversarial perturbations, with particularly severe degradation under white-box attacks. Further analysis reveals distinct failure modes across modalities: textual latent states exhibit brittle dynamics and high sensitivity to specific input patterns, while latent states in multimodal models can remain largely invariant to input perturbations and have limited influence on final predictions. These findings expose robustness limitations of current latent reasoning approaches and highlight the need to jointly consider efficiency and robustness when designing implicit reasoning systems. We have open-sourced our code to facilitate reproduction of our research https://github.com/PKU-ML/latent-reasoning-model-assessment.

</details>


### [89] [On Mitigation of Subliminal Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22215)
*Yanagisawa, Atsushi,Gho, Brendan,Narender, Rajendran Ramesh Babu Manoj,Zhu, Kevin,Panwar, Madhur,Mari, Antonio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究知识蒸馏中的潜隐学习现象，发现其训练动态非单调，并提出退火KL正则微调方法liminal training，在1.5B–8B模型上有效抑制不期望特质传递，同时保留任务性能，并揭示早期正则比晚期更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏中，即使训练数据语义上与目标行为无关，学生模型也会通过潜隐学习（subliminal learning）获得教师模型的不期望行为特质；现有工作未充分研究其训练动态与缓解手段。

Method: 论文研究基于开放权重语言模型（1.5B–8B，Qwen/Gemma/Llama家族）的微调场景。输入为学生模型微调数据集，其中数据在语义上与目标行为特质无关（数字序列、链式思考、法语响应风格等）。核心方法liminal training在标准微调损失（如交叉熵）基础上，于训练前期对模型相对基座模型的KL散度施加正则化，正则权重随训练退火逐渐减小，以约束早期参数漂移，防止潜隐特质被习得。训练过程中持续监控特质相关概率以观察非单调动态。推理阶段模型直接生成，未引入额外模块。方法通过调节KL正则的时机与强度，在任务学习与特质抑制之间权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "subliminal trait acquisition", "value": "substantially reduced", "setting": "Qwen/Gemma/Llama 1.5B-8B模型，数字序列及链式思考设置"}, {"metric": "GSM8K improvement retained", "value": "much of the improvement retained", "setting": "法语响应风格实验中，liminal training抑制语言迁移同时保留大部分GSM8K提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 追踪微调全过程中特质相关概率，首次揭示潜隐学习具有高度非单调性：出现瞬时峰值、反转及特质特异性的迁移失败。；提出liminal training：一种退火KL正则化微调方法，在早期约束模型相对于基座模型的漂移，从而缓解潜隐特质获取。；系统对比了liminal training与paraphrasing、layer freezing等缓解策略，显示前者显著更优。；发现KL正则化的时机至关重要：早期正则比晚期正则更有效；并扫描正则强度，揭示任务学习与特质抑制之间的经验性权衡。；在法语响应风格实验和GSM8K任务上验证方法的泛化性：抑制语言迁移同时保留大部分任务提升。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation can transmit unintended behavioral traits from a teacher model to a student through training data that appear semantically unrelated to those traits, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. Although recent work has established this effect, its training dynamics and mitigation remain underexplored. We study subliminal learning in open-weight language models ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, covering the Qwen, Gemma, and Llama families in number-sequence and chain-of-thought settings. Rather than evaluating only final models, we track trait-related probabilities throughout fine-tuning and find that subliminal acquisition can be highly non-monotonic, with transient spikes, reversals, and trait-specific failures of transfer. We then introduce liminal training, an annealed KL-regularized fine-tuning method that constrains early drift from the base model. Across our experiments, liminal training substantially reduces subliminal trait acquisition while largely preserving task gains, outperforming paraphrasing and layer freezing as mitigation strategies. The effect also extends beyond animal preferences: in a French-language response-style experiment, liminal training suppresses language transfer while retaining much of the GSM8K improvement. Finally, we show that KL timing matters: early regularization is more effective than late regularization, and sweeping the regularization strength reveals an empirical trade-off between task learning and trait suppression.

</details>


### [90] [The Bairong System for MLC-SLM 2026: Dynamic Question-Aware Evidence Routing for Multilingual Conversational Speech Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22214)
*Huang, Shangkun,Hu, Junchao,Shen, Huan,Wang, Guoji,Wang, Yingao,Li, Shaosai,Zou, Wei,Chen, Yunzhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bairong团队在MLC-SLM 2026挑战赛中的系统，通过动态问题感知证据路由，融合说话人分离-ASR转录与声学线索，在多语言对话语音问答中实现了优于全转录基线和音频中心系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时多语言对话语音问答中，如何平衡长程文本语义与稀疏的声学及说话人敏感线索，以动态选择最相关的证据类型和上下文范围来提升答案预测准确性。

Method: 系统包含一个说话人分离-ASR前端，生成带有说话人属性的转录文本。动态证据路由器根据输入的问题和答案选项，推断所需的证据类型和上下文范围，然后从四种证据源中选择：完整转录上下文、局部音频-文本融合、说话人关联证据、紧凑全局声学样本。所选证据被构造为问题特定的输入，送入答案预测模块。转录本骨干设计确保话语上下文始终可用，音频仅在提供互补证据时被激活。训练目标为答案预测的准确性，在MLC-SLM 2026挑战赛的任务1和任务2上进行评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "tcpMER", "value": "25.70%", "setting": "开发集，任务1"}, {"metric": "tcpMER", "value": "18.44%", "setting": "评估集，任务1"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "94.84%", "setting": "开发集，任务2，最终系统"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "1.68个百分点", "setting": "任务2，相比全转录基线"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "2.77个百分点", "setting": "任务2，相比最佳以音频为中心的诊断系统"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 动态证据路由器：根据问题和选项推断所需证据类型和上下文范围，在完整转录上下文、局部音频-文本融合、说话人关联证据和紧凑全局声学样本之间动态选择。；转录本骨干设计：保持话语上下文可用，仅在音频提供互补证据时激活音频模态。

Abstract: Long multilingual conversational spoken question answering requires systems to balance long-range transcript semantics with sparse acoustic and speaker-sensitive cues. We present the Bairong system for the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge, where a diarization-ASR front-end produces speaker-attributed transcripts and a dynamic evidence router constructs question-specific inputs for answer prediction. Instead of applying a fixed transcript-only or audio-only policy, the router infers the required evidence type and context scope from the question and answer options, and selects among full transcript context, local audio-text fusion, speaker-linked evidence, and compact global acoustic samples. This transcript-backbone design keeps discourse context available while activating audio only when it provides complementary evidence. Our Task 1 system achieves 25.70% and 18.44% tcpMER on the development and evaluation sets. For Task 2, the final system obtains 94.84% devel?opment accuracy, outperforming the full-transcript baseline by 1.68 points and the best audio-centric diagnostic system by 2.77 points. These results support dynamic question-aware routing as an effective evidence allocation strategy for conversational spoken QA.

</details>


### [91] [A Channel-Boosted Multi-Agent System with Iterative Consultation for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22212)
*Zainab, Aleesha,Khan, Asifullah,Khalid, Muhammad Ahmed,Khan, Faheem Ullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Channel-Boosted MAS（CB-MAS），实例化为IC-MAS（迭代咨询多智能体系统），用于长文档敏感度分类。通过Channel Critic Agent学习文档自适应信任权重进行门控通道提升，并让成对Consultation Agents迭代交换信念状态以调和首尾证据，在固定窗口编码下实现计算量不随文档长度增加。在Strategic 16K语料上达90.72%准确率、91.23% F1、92.01%敏感召回、90.46%敏感精确率，比固定轮次基线节省约54%平均计算量，并优于BERT单编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基线受固定输入长度截断限制，会丢弃保留窗口之外的证据，而敏感电缆往往最长；需在避免长上下文计算成本的前提下，调和长文档首尾证据并提升敏感度分类的性能。

Method: 系统以两个首窗口编码器为输入通道，分别处理长文档保留窗口内的开头与结尾片段（论文强调Transformer基线因固定长度截断丢弃窗口外证据，尤其长敏感电缆）。核心模块按处理顺序为：(1) Channel Critic Agent：学习文档自适应的信任权重，对两个首窗口编码器的输出进行Gated Channel Boosting，即由critic动态决定两通道的融合门控；(2) Max-Pool融合：在门控通道提升中采用最大池化进行表示融合；(3) 成对Consultation Agents：以迭代方式交换信念状态，在紧凑表示空间中调和来自文档开头与结尾的证据，并通过Blackboard Adaptive Consultation机制自适应协调，计算量不随文档长度增长；(4) 推理阶段：无论文档长度，仅对固定窗口进行编码与调和，保持计算恒定。训练目标围绕敏感度分类任务，通过critic控制的通道门控与迭代咨询的信念状态交换联合优化分类性能。消融显示critic控制的Channel Boosting贡献主要准确率增益，consultation在不导致精确率崩溃的情况下恢复召回。系统还包含LIME/SHAP可解释性与多智能体评估作为辅助分析。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "90.72%", "setting": "Critic-Controlled Gated Channel Boosting with Max-Pool fusion and Blackboard Adaptive Consultation，对比单编码器BERT基线89.14%（5折交叉验证，Strategic 16K语料）"}, {"metric": "F1-score", "value": "91.23%", "setting": "同上，对比BERT基线89.33%"}, {"metric": "sensitive recall", "value": "92.01%", "setting": "同上设置"}, {"metric": "sensitive precision", "value": "90.46%", "setting": "同上设置"}, {"metric": "average computation reduction", "value": "约54%", "setting": "相对固定轮次基线"}, {"metric": "statistical significance", "value": "McNemar's test p < 0.000001; paired t-test", "setting": "相对单编码器基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Channel Critic Agent学习文档自适应信任权重，控制两个首窗口编码器之间的Gated Channel Boosting。；成对Consultation Agents迭代交换信念状态，调和长文档开头与结尾的证据。；IC-MAS通过紧凑表示空间中调和固定窗口，使计算量不随文档长度增加而增加。；Critic-Controlled Gated Channel Boosting结合Max-Pool融合与Blackboard Adaptive Consultation。；提供LIME/SHAP可解释性、多智能体评估及局限性诚实说明。

Abstract: Organizations in critical national infrastructure sectors must assess heterogeneous documents for sensitivity before routing or storage. Manual assessment is slow, inconsistent, and unscalable. Extending our prior leakage-controlled benchmark, BERT established the top single-encoder baseline (89.14% accuracy, 89.33% F1-score under 5-fold cross-validation on the Strategic 16K corpus). However, transformer baselines suffer from a structural limitation: fixed input length truncation discards evidence beyond the retained window-precisely where sensitive cables tend to be longest. We present Channel-Boosted MAS (CB-MAS) and instantiate it as IC-MAS (Iterative Consultation Multi-Agent System) to solve this without long-context computational costs. A Channel Critic Agent learns document-adaptive trust weights governing Gated Channel Boosting between two first-window encoders, while paired Consultation Agents iteratively exchange belief states to reconcile evidence from the beginning and end of long documents. IC-MAS holds computation constant regardless of document length by reconciling fixed windows in a compact representation space. Ablation studies show critic-controlled Channel Boosting provides the bulk of accuracy gains, while consultation recovers recall without precision collapse. Critic-Controlled Gated Channel Boosting with Max-Pool fusion and Blackboard Adaptive Consultation achieves 90.72% accuracy, 91.23% F1-score, 92.01% sensitive recall, and 90.46% sensitive precision, using about 54% less average computation than a fixed-round baseline. Gains over the single-encoder baseline are statistically significant (McNemar's test, p less than 0.000001; paired t-test). We include LIME/SHAP explainability, multi-agent evaluation, and an honest accounting of limitations.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Raw Context Transfer: Representation-based Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22162)
*Peng, Can,Liu, Yu,Yang, Yingyu,Le, Anjie,Liu, Yuyuan,Yang, Qianye,Noble, J. Alison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FedRepRAG 提出一种去中心化检索增强生成框架：原始文档保留在各客户端，跨客户端检索仅交换紧凑的潜在表示，并通过协同训练的 projector 将检索嵌入转换为冻结 LLM/VLM 可用的表示 token。在去中心化 VQA 和 QA 基准上，FedRepRAG 优于直接推理和本地检索基线，同时显著降低检索上下文长度和推理计算开销，并分析了表示级泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 框架通常假设集中式检索语料库，在医疗等敏感领域中不适用；去中心化 RAG 主要采用提示式范式，交换原始可读检索内容，带来推理时计算开销和直接暴露检索信息的问题。

Method: FedRepRAG 是一个去中心化检索增强生成框架。输入为查询（如视觉问答中的图像-问题对或文本问答中的问题），原始文档保留在各自拥有的客户端，不进行跨客户端原始内容交换。检索阶段，各客户端基于本地文档计算检索嵌入，并仅将紧凑的潜在表示发送给查询方或聚合节点。跨客户端检索后，查询方获得来自不同客户端的检索表示。为将这些表示集成到生成器中，框架引入一个协同训练的 projector，将检索嵌入映射为生成器兼容的表示 token，并输入到冻结的 LLM/VLM 主干中，与查询表示一起参与生成。生成器冻结，仅训练 projector 及相关模块，以适配表示 token 的语义空间。训练目标为生成任务损失（如自回归语言建模损失），通过多客户端协作训练 projector。推理时，查询方从各客户端收集检索表示，经 projector 转换为表示 token 后，由冻结的 LLM/VLM 生成最终答案，无需传递原始上下文，从而降低检索上下文长度和推理计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "effectiveness", "value": "consistently outperforms direct inference and local retrieval baselines", "setting": "decentralized VQA and QA benchmarks"}, {"metric": "retrieval-context length", "value": "substantially reduced", "setting": "compared with raw-context transfer"}, {"metric": "inference-time computational overhead", "value": "substantially reduced", "setting": "compared with raw-context transfer"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FedRepRAG 去中心化 RAG 框架，保留原始文档在所属客户端，跨客户端检索时只交换紧凑的潜在表示；引入协同训练的 projector，将检索嵌入转换为生成器兼容的表示 token，以集成到冻结的 LLM/VLM 主干中；在去中心化 VQA 和 QA 基准上验证，相比原始上下文传递显著降低检索上下文长度和推理时计算开销，同时保持或提升效果；分析查询相关检索表示的重要性，并刻画表示交换带来的残余表示级泄漏

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) by grounding generation in external knowledge. However, most existing RAG frameworks assume a centralized retrieval corpus, which is often impractical in sensitive domains such as healthcare, where data are inherently distributed and raw content cannot be directly shared across institutions. Recent efforts on decentralized RAG primarily follow prompt-based paradigms that exchange raw, human-readable retrieved content, leading to substantial inference-time computational overhead and direct exposure of retrieved information. To address these limitations, we propose Representation-based Federated RAG (FedRepRAG), a decentralized RAG framework that keeps raw documents at their owning clients and exchanges only compact latent representations during cross-client retrieval. To integrate retrieved knowledge, we introduce a collaboratively trained projector that converts retrieval embeddings into generator-compatible representation tokens for a frozen LLM/VLM backbone. Experiments across decentralized visual question answering (VQA) and question answering (QA) benchmarks show that FedRepRAG consistently outperforms direct inference and local retrieval baselines while substantially reducing retrieval-context length and inference-time computational overhead compared with raw-context transfer. Further analyses confirm the importance of query-relevant retrieved representations and characterize the residual representation-level leakage associated with representation exchange. Overall, FedRepRAG provides an effective and efficient framework for federated RAG without transferring raw retrieved content.

</details>


### [93] [Is Imagination Derived from Hallucination? A Cross-Taxonomy Evaluation of Imagination and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22152)
*Tang, Zixuan,Li, Hongzong,Zhuang, Shuxin,Wu, Dapeng,Liang, Zi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Whiteboard，首个面向LLM想象力的认知机制驱动评测基准，通过将七种想象力子类型与十种幻觉子类型交叉，在同一生成上联合评分，并显式检查输出偏离是否被提示支持。在80项锚点集上评估79个LLM，用13,280个人工判断验证，发现想象力与幻觉存在反直觉的负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有创造力基准只衡量输出偏离常见答案的程度，不检查偏离是否被提示允许；幻觉评测则在不同生成上独立进行，导致想象力与幻觉是否源于同一生成机制的假设从未被直接检验。

Method: Whiteboard基准的架构包含以下模块：首先，从经典认知范式中适配出七种机制驱动的想象力子类型，并将它们与十种支持边界幻觉子类型交叉，形成联合评分空间。其次，对于每个生成，系统先执行显式的支持检查，判断输出偏离是否被提示内容所许可；只有通过支持检查的想象力得分才被计入，否则予以门控。然后，通过一个可审计的原子矩阵确定性计算想象力和幻觉两个轴上的分数，主路径不依赖LLM评判，避免引入评判模型偏差。完整题库包含1,660条提示，其中80项锚点集用于跨模型比较。在锚点集上评估79个SOTA LLM，并收集13,280个人工判断来验证基准的合理性。最后，分析想象力与幻觉子类型之间的耦合关系，发现多数耦合为负，并验证每个锚点项独立复现负耦合。推理时，被测LLM根据提示生成输出，然后由基准的确定性评分流程处理，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Whiteboard，首个基于认知机制范式的LLM想象力评测基准，包含7种想象力子类型与10种支持边界幻觉子类型交叉在同一生成上联合评分；每个想象力分数都通过显式支持检查进行门控，并通过可审计的原子矩阵确定性计算两轴，主路径不使用LLM评判；构建包含1,660条提示的题库，并在共享80项锚点集上评估79个SOTA LLM，用13,280个人工判断验证仪器；发现幻觉与想象力之间存在反直觉的负相关：大多数子类型耦合为负，且每个锚点项单独复现该负耦合

Abstract: Imagination performs as a high-level function of large language models (LLMs) which determines the potential of how an LLM creates unseen or creative content. While existing works have built a rich family of creativity benchmarks for this ability, they only measure how far an output departs from common answers and never check whether the departure is licensed by the prompt. Moreover, hallucination, the closest neighbor of imagination, is always measured in a separate pipeline on different generations, so the influential claim that imagination and hallucination stem from the same generative mechanism has never been directly testable. In this paper, we propose Whiteboard, the first LLM imagination evaluation benchmark. Its design follows the authoritative cognitive instruments developed to measure human imagination: seven mechanism-grounded imagination subtypes are adapted from classic paradigms, then crossed with ten support-boundary hallucination subtypes and scored jointly on the same generation. Different from previous creativity or hallucination benchmarks, Whiteboard gates every imagination score with an explicit support check and computes both axes deterministically through an auditable atom matrix, with no LLM judge on the primary path. The full Whiteboard item bank contains 1,660 prompts; on its shared 80-item anchor set, we evaluate 79 state-of-the-art LLMs and validate the instrument against 13,280 human judgments. Additionally, we further explore whether imagination derives from the same generative tendency as hallucination and what key factors shape it. Our analysis indicates a counterintuitive correlation between hallucination and imagination: Most of the subtype couplings are negative, every one of the anchor items reproduces the negative coupling on its own.

</details>


### [94] [Does the Truthfulness Signal Survive Code-Mixing? Probing Hidden States for Hallucination Detection in Hinglish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22138)
*Singh, Tanveer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探究 LLM 隐藏状态幻觉探针在 Hinglish 代码混合输入下的迁移能力，构建 5,674 条多语言 QA 基准并生成标注 17,022 条响应，在三个 7-8B 模型上验证跨语言迁移 AUROC 0.88-0.99，发现 Hindi 训练的探针迁移到 Hinglish 更可靠，并观察到 Hindi/Hinglish 幻觉显著多于英语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Hinglish（印地语-英语代码混合）输入下，基于 LLM 隐藏状态训练的幻觉检测探针能否从干净语言迁移过来，以及幻觉信号在代码混合条件下是否退化。

Method: 输入为 Hindi/English/Hinglish 问答对，通过三个 7-8B 开源 LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）生成响应并标注忠实性。从每个模型的每一层提取两个 token 位置的隐藏状态作为特征表示。使用这些隐藏状态训练线性分类器和 MLP 探针，执行分布内幻觉检测以及跨语言迁移（例如从 Hindi 训练迁移到 Hinglish）。训练目标为二分类（忠实 vs 幻觉）。推理时，将输入序列喂入 LLM，提取指定层和 token 位置的隐藏状态，经探针分类得到幻觉判断。该架构旨在绕过生成文本表面形式，直接从内部激活判断模型答案是否忠实于输入，并检验代码混合输入下该信号是否保持。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "transfer AUROC", "value": "0.88-0.99", "setting": "Hinglish 与跨语言迁移到 Hinglish 的幻觉检测探针"}, {"metric": "AUROC gap relative to in-distribution", "value": "mostly under 0.05", "setting": "迁移检测 vs 分布内检测"}, {"metric": "hallucination rate difference", "value": "substantially more on Hindi and Hinglish than on English for matched facts", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-8B 在匹配事实上的表现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建 5,674 条 Hindi/English/Hinglish 问答基准，并生成标注 17,022 条模型响应；在三个 7-8B 开源 LLM 上逐层提取两个 token 位置的隐藏状态，训练线性与 MLP 探针进行分布内检测和跨语言迁移；实证发现幻觉信号在代码混合下仍保持较高 AUROC（0.88-0.99），且 Hindi 训练的探针比 English 训练的探针更可靠地迁移到 Hinglish；匹配事实下，三个模型在 Hindi/Hinglish 上的幻觉显著多于英语

Abstract: Hidden-state hallucination probing - training a linear classifier on an LLM's internal activations to detect whether a generated answer is faithful to the input - is an active area of 2026 research, with recent work reporting 0.90-1.00 AUROC across several benchmarks and languages. However, none of this work has tested probes on code-mixed input, despite the fact that a huge population of chatbot users write in Hindi-English code-mixed text ("Hinglish"). We address this gap directly: does a hallucination probe trained on clean-language hidden states transfer to Hinglish, or does the signal degrade under code-mixing? We construct a 5,674-item Hindi/English/Hinglish QA benchmark, generate and label 17,022 model responses across three open-weight 7-8B LLMs (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-8B), extract per-layer hidden states at two token positions, and train linear and MLP probes for in-distribution detection and cross-lingual transfer. We find that the hallucination signal survives code-mixing well: transfer AUROC ranges from 0.88 to 0.99, with gaps of mostly under 0.05 AUROC relative to in-distribution performance, and that Hindi-trained probes transfer to Hinglish more reliably than English-trained probes. As an independent, practically motivated finding, all three models hallucinate substantially more on Hindi and Hinglish than on English for matched facts. We release our code and synthetic Hinglish QA dataset to support further work on code-mixed hallucination detection.

</details>


### [95] [Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22131)
*Xu, Sicheng,Shi, Hao,Zhang, Wei,Pang, Haoran,Ming, Zhenyu,Wu, Hao,Huang, Zhongyi,Yao, Xin,Zhang, Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出相关性感知的结构化剪枝方法，用二元二次规划建模剪枝单元间依赖，配合贪心算法与梯度驱动的逐层稀疏分配，在主流LLM上取得有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法独立评估剪枝单元重要性，隐含剪枝误差可加的假设，忽略了模型权重非正交性和单元激活相关性导致的依赖关系，可能引起性能退化。

Method: 该方法针对LLM结构化剪枝，将剪枝单元（如通道、注意力头）的选择问题建模为基数约束的二元二次规划，目标函数中引入单元间的相关性项以显式建模重构误差的跨单元依赖。由于BQP是NP难问题，设计基于依赖感知边际成本的贪心交互算法，迭代选择边际成本最小的单元进行剪枝。同时，利用梯度信息自适应地为不同层分配稀疏度，以平衡整体压缩率与精度。推理时，修剪后的模型具备硬件友好的结构化稀疏性，可直接用于加速推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将剪枝目标形式化为基数约束的二元二次规划（BQP），显式建模跨单元依赖的重构误差；基于依赖感知边际成本的贪心交互算法求解BQP；基于梯度的自适应逐层稀疏度分配策略

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for reducing the substantial inference costs of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining hardware efficiency. Many existing methods assess the importance of prunable units (e.g., channels or heads) in isolation, implicitly assuming that pruning errors are additive. This independence assumption is often invalidated by the non-orthogonality of model weights and strong correlations between unit activations, potentially leading to performance degradation. To address this, we propose a Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning method. We formulate the pruning objective as a cardinality-constrained binary quadratic program that explicitly models cross-unit dependencies in the reconstruction error. Since this binary quadratic program is NP-hard and difficult to solve exactly, we develop a greedy interaction algorithm based on dependency-aware marginal costs to optimize unit selection. Furthermore, we incorporate a gradient-based strategy to achieve adaptive layer-wise sparsity allocation across the entire model. Extensive experiments on mainstream LLMs demonstrate that incorporating correlation information yields competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to representative structured pruning baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Type-Driven Tokenization for Brahmic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22125)
*Kapila, Sai Hemanth,Bagavathy, Rakshika*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文针对 LLM 分词器在婆罗米系文字（如天城文、泰卢固文等）上产生非法 token 的问题，形式化其正字法为部分半群，并在 Agda 中验证，推导出 fixToken 函数，提供 SentencePiece 补丁和 Rust 预分词库，消除观察到的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分词器（如 SentencePiece）在应用于婆罗米系文字时会产生违反正字法约束的无效 token，导致文本畸形。根本原因在于这些文字的正字法构成部分半群（而非英文字母文字的完全半群），即并非任意两个有效 token 的拼接都是有效的。

Method: 输入为婆罗米系文字字符串。首先将有效 Brahmic token 建模为一个转移系统中的链（chain），其中状态对应正字法边界条件，转移对应合法的字符序列。通过形式化验证（Agda），证明任何候选 token 必须满足链的约束。基于此推导出 fixToken 函数：给定任意候选 token，若其违反正字法边界，则通过扩展（而非简单截断）使其成为有效 token。该函数被集成到 SentencePiece 的分词流程中作为补丁，同时也实现为独立的 Rust 预分词器库，在分词前对输入文本进行处理，确保输出的 token 序列符合婆罗米系文字的正字法规则。训练目标不直接涉及模型训练，而是保证分词正确性。推理时，预分词器接收原始文本，经过状态转移和边界检查，输出修正后的有效 token 序列，供下游 LLM 使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "非法 token 错误率", "value": "消除所有观察到的错误", "setting": "对多种 Indic 脚本的 SentencePiece 分词测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将婆罗米系文字正字法形式化为部分半群，并在 Agda 中形式化验证，与英文字母文字的完全半群形成对比。；将有效 Brahmic token 建模为转移系统中的链，并推导出可证明正确的 fixToken 函数，能扩展任何候选 token 以遵守正字法边界。；将形式化推导转化为 SentencePiece 的实用补丁以及独立的基于 Rust 的预分词器库，消除观察到的错误。

Abstract: Standard tokenizers used in large language models produce malformed text when applied to Brahmic scripts. They are a family of abugidas, writing systems whose consonants carry an inherent vowel that dependent marks can modify. They include Devanagari, Telugu, Tamil, Kannada, and others. The underlying issue is that these tokenizers violate orthographic constraints that do not arise in alphabetic scripts like English. We observe that while English orthography forms a \emph{semigroup} (any two valid tokens can be freely concatenated), Brahmic orthography forms a \emph{partial semigroup}: not every concatenation yields a valid string. We formalise this distinction in Agda, model valid Brahmic tokens as chains in a transition system, and derive a provably correct \texttt{fixToken} function that extends any candidate token to respect orthographic boundaries. We then show how this formal derivation translates into a practical patch for SentencePiece as well as a standalone Rust-based pre-tokenizer library, eliminating the observed errors across Indic scripts.

</details>


### [97] [Balancing Reasoning and Hardware Constraints in RAG Pipelines for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22124)
*Havrylov, Illya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UNLP 2026多领域文档理解共享任务的参赛系统。在严格的9小时离线执行限制下，系统采用BM25、BGE-M3和Cross-Encoder重排序的混合RAG流水线，并使用4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上通过llama.cpp进行推理，优先保证流程稳定性而非多步推理，最终私有测试集得分0.8095，在15支活跃队伍中排名第10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在9小时离线Kaggle执行限制下，OCR成为时间瓶颈，剩余约2小时需完成500题的LLM推理，需在极有限算力下保证RAG流水线稳定完成并取得较好准确率。

Method: 系统采用混合检索增强生成流水线处理乌克兰语PDF文档。输入为多领域乌克兰语PDF，首先进行OCR，但OCR为顺序单线程执行，耗时5-7小时，成为主要瓶颈。检索阶段结合BM25稀疏检索与BGE-M3稠密检索，对候选文档进行召回，随后使用Cross-Encoder对候选进行重排序以提升相关性。生成阶段使用4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B模型，通过llama.cpp在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上执行推理，处理最终的问答生成。训练目标未披露，推理路径为：OCR→混合检索（BM25+BGE-M3）→Cross-Encoder重排序→量化LLM生成答案。该架构旨在严格的时间预算内平衡推理质量与硬件约束。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 混合检索RAG架构：BM25稀疏检索 + BGE-M3稠密检索 + Cross-Encoder重排序；采用4-bit量化LapaLLM 12B与llama.cpp在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上部署，避免参数密集型推理模型超时；以流水线稳定性优先于多步推理的工程取舍策略

Abstract: This paper describes the system submitted to the UNLP 2026 Shared Task on Multi-Domain Document Understanding. The challenge required extracting precise answers, document IDs, and page numbers from a diverse corpus of Ukrainian PDF documents within a strict 9-hour offline Kaggle execution limit. During evaluation on the hidden private test set, optical character recognition (OCR) of scanned documents emerged as a severe bottleneck, consuming 5-7 hours of the total time budget due to sequential single-threaded execution. This overhead strictly limited the remaining time for Large Language Model (LLM) inference to approximately two hours for 500 questions. To guarantee pipeline completion without timeouts, we developed a resource-efficient Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing BM25, BGE-M3, and Cross-Encoder reranking. Rather than deploying parameter-heavy reasoning models (e.g., DeepSeek R1) which consistently timed out, we utilized a 4-bit quantized LapaLLM 12B model via llama.cpp on dual NVIDIA T4 GPUs. Prioritizing pipeline stability over multi-step reasoning, our system achieved a Private Score of 0.8095, placing 10th out of 15 active teams.

</details>


### [98] [An Empirical Cost Attribution of Context-Compression Gateways in Multi-Turn Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22114)
*Chen, Luzhuo,Shi, Jiayu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对生产级上下文压缩网关进行成本归因，发现工具模式过滤是唯一稳定正向的节约杠杆（线性增长），内容压缩累计节约呈二次增长但远超固定节约，且单次压缩基准与多轮智能体成本正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮编程智能体中，压缩文件读取等上下文压缩手段是否真正节省 token 成本，以及不同压缩杠杆（工具模式过滤、内容压缩、历史摘要）的实际节约效果与交互关系。

Method: 该生产系统在编程智能体（Claude Code、Codex）与前沿 LLM（Claude Sonnet、GPT-5）之间部署了一个上下文压缩网关（Paritok）。网关对每次请求和响应进行拦截与处理，包含三个独立可配置的杠杆模块：1) 工具模式过滤：每轮移除固定的工具模式块（约 21K-57K tokens），该节约随轮次线性增长；2) 内容压缩：对文件读取和工具输出进行压缩，每次压缩节约约 2% 的缓存定价前缀，但由于压缩后的读取内容保留在历史中并在后续每轮重新发送，累计节约随轮次呈二次增长；3) 历史摘要：对历史对话进行摘要。网关采用非破坏性设计，原始字节保留在网关侧，智能体可在需要时按需召回（recall），每次召回仅重新发送刚被压缩掉的单个片段，因此召回成本固定且有界，不会导致乘法式膨胀。模型方面，网关使用 Paritok-4B（4B 参数）进行压缩，该模型在 SWE-bench 单次压缩基准上实现 25.7% 压缩率下保留 86.5% 质量。论文通过受控 A/B 运行在生产环境中测量各杠杆的独立节约效果，并对比其随轮次变化的累计成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "每轮 token 节约", "value": "约 21K-57K tokens", "setting": "工具模式过滤，典型轮次，生产 A/B 测量"}, {"metric": "缓存定价前缀每轮节约", "value": "约 2%", "setting": "内容压缩，生产 A/B 测量"}, {"metric": "累计 token 节约", "value": "约 3350*N^2 tokens", "setting": "内容压缩，随轮次 N 累计，生产测量"}, {"metric": "SWE-bench 质量保留率", "value": "86.5%", "setting": "25.7% 压缩率，Paritok-4B 单次压缩基准（离线基准，非在线成本结果）"}]}

Conclusion: 将智能体会话的 token 账单分解为三个独立杠杆：工具模式过滤、内容压缩、历史摘要，并分别量化其节约率。；发现内容压缩的累计节约呈二次增长（约 3350*N^2 tokens），而工具模式过滤节约随轮次线性增长。；设计非破坏性压缩网关，允许按需拉回原始字节，使召回成本有界且固定，而非乘法式膨胀。；揭示单次压缩基准（如 25.7% 压缩率下保留 86.5% SWE-bench 质量）与多轮智能体成本正交，不能作为成本节约论据。

Abstract: Context compression is widely proposed as a way to cut the token bill of LLM coding agents, and public benchmarks report that aggressive compression preserves task-solving quality. These two facts do not imply the third one commonly assumed: that compressing file reads saves money in a real multi-turn agent. We instrument a production compression gateway (Paritok) between coding agents (Claude Code, Codex) and frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet, GPT-5), and decompose the token bill of real sessions into three independent levers: tool-schema filtering, content compression of file reads and tool output, and history summarization. Measured in isolation under controlled A/B runs, the three save at fundamentally different rates. Tool-schema filtering removes a fixed block every turn, roughly 21K-57K tokens on a typical turn; it is linear in the turn count N and the only unambiguously and reproducibly positive lever. Content compression saves only about 2% of the cache-priced prefix per turn, but compressed reads accumulate in history and are re-sent on every later turn, so its cumulative saving grows quadratically, about 3350*N^2 tokens (measured), overtaking the fixed tool-filter saving within roughly 6 turns until the context window caps it. A non-destructive gateway lets the agent pull original bytes back on demand; each recall re-sends exactly the one segment just compressed away, so its cost is fixed and bounded rather than a multiplicative blowup, and heavy recall spends the accumulated saving back one segment at a time. Finally, a strong single-shot compression benchmark - 86.5% of SWE-bench quality retained at a 25.7% compression rate, achieved by the model this gateway deploys (Paritok-4B, reported separately) - is orthogonal to multi-turn agent cost and must not be cited as a cost-saving argument. We distill the results into an actionable recipe for where token-saving effort pays off.

</details>


### [99] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Zhou, Huapeng,Wang, Huayu,Wang, Xinyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeSpark 提出一种校准的、负载自适应的草稿树构建方法，用于半自回归推测解码。通过从草稿器 Markov 头读取父条件分布校准边接受概率，基于路径生存概率进行最佳优先扩展和自适应停止，并在服务负载变化时动态调整树大小。实验表明，相比相同草稿器的调优链式方法，每轮接受更多草稿 token（15-25%），单请求解码速度提升 8-14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中草稿树构建与自适应控制问题：现有方法按位置边际排序候选，忽略父节点条件，导致宽树引入错误排序节点；固定大小的树无法适应每轮解码和负载变化。

Method: TreeSpark针对半自回归草稿器（单次前向产生整个草稿块）的推测解码。核心架构：草稿器已有Markov头，作者从中低成本读取父条件分布 p(child|parent)，并将其校准为边接受概率估计。基于该概率，维护路径生存概率作为核心度量：每轮解码中，从当前根开始，按生存概率进行最佳优先扩展生成树节点；当生存概率低于阈值或达到停止准则时停止扩展；兄弟节点采样时无放回采样，并在递归拒绝中使用匹配残差，保证最终输出分布与目标模型一致（无损）。服务阶段，根据当前负载动态调整树大小：负载升高时，树退化为链式结构，降低验证计算开销。推理流程：单次草稿器前向产出块token，同时获得边接受概率，构建最优树；目标模型并行验证树中多条候选路径；接受最长通过验证路径的前缀；根据每轮实际接受情况和负载反馈更新下一轮树大小。训练目标未涉及新训练，属于推理时优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "每轮接受草稿token数", "value": "15-25% more", "setting": "相同草稿器，与调优链式方法对比"}, {"metric": "单请求墙钟解码速度", "value": "8-14% faster", "setting": "相同草稿器，与调优链式方法对比"}, {"metric": "自适应树对比匹配固定预算", "value": "所有温度下均有提升", "setting": "温度变化实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从草稿器已有的Markov头读取父条件分布，以可忽略成本校准为边接受概率估计；基于路径生存概率的最佳优先扩展、每轮停止和负载自适应服务策略；无放回采样兄弟节点并匹配残差，在递归拒绝中保持任意温度下的无损解码

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [100] [Summarize, Judge, Refine: Decoupled Content Understanding and Policy Learning for Multimodal Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22094)
*Ahmed, Zeeshan,Qin, Yang,Huang, Hanqing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SJR 用“多模态摘要 + 纯文本政策判定”双模型解耦内容审核中的理解与策略，配合 GRPO 协同训练和文本空间增强，在误导广告检测上相对零样本 CoT 提升 23.6% F1，且仅用合成正类数据即可接近全量模型，展示政策冷启动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态审核把多模态理解与具体政策分类耦合，政策变动需全流水线重训，且多模态原始数据难以有效增强、违规标签稀缺，导致新政策上线与冷启动困难。

Method: SJR 采用双模型解耦架构。输入端为多模态内容（论文场景为广告）。Content Model（多模态）读取原始多模态输入，输出结构化自然语言摘要，摘要充当内容理解与政策判断之间的可读接口。Policy Model（纯文本）以该摘要和自然语言政策定义为输入，输出是否违规的分类。训练通过迭代协同训练闭环：Policy Model 在文本摘要空间进行策略分类，其反馈用于 GRPO 精炼 Content Model，使其生成更政策相关的摘要；同时利用文本空间增强生成对抗摘要变体，因为原始多模态数据难以做语义增强。推理时只需 Content Model 生成摘要、Policy Model 按政策分类，政策变化仅替换 Policy Model 或其政策定义，无需重训多模态理解部分；最终决策可由人类阅读摘要获得解释。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Non-misleading F1", "value": "+23.6% relative", "setting": "误导广告检测，对比零样本 chain-of-thought 基线"}, {"metric": "Violating F1 gap", "value": "within 0.2% relative", "setting": "零真实违规样本、仅合成正类数据变体 vs 全量数据模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用自然语言接口解耦内容理解与策略判断：多模态 Content Model 只产出结构化文本摘要，纯文本 Policy Model 依据政策定义分类，实现政策可替换与决策可解释。；迭代协同训练：用 GRPO 基于 Policy Model 反馈精炼 Content Model，使其生成政策相关摘要。；文本空间对抗增强：在摘要文本空间生成对抗变体，绕过原始多模态数据难以增强的限制，支持少样本政策冷启动。；零真实违规样本冷启动：仅用合成正类数据训练的变体在违规 F1 上接近全量数据模型。

Abstract: Content moderation systems traditionally entangle multimodal understanding with policy-specific classification, requiring full pipeline retraining for every policy change and suffering from label scarcity since multimedia cannot be meaningfully augmented. We propose Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR), a two-model architecture that decouples these concerns via a natural language interface: a multimodal Content Model produces structured text summaries, and a text-only Policy Model classifies them against policy definitions. An iterative co-training loop refines the Content Model via GRPO to produce policy-relevant summaries, while text-space augmentation generates adversarial summary variants---an augmentation pathway impossible on raw multimedia---enabling few-shot policy bootstrap. Every decision is grounded in a human-readable summary, providing interpretability as a structural byproduct. On misleading advertisement detection, SJR achieves +23.6\% relative non-misleading F1 over a zero-shot chain-of-thought baseline, outperforming end-to-end SFT, STaR/RFT, and RLFT. Notably, a variant trained on zero real violating examples---with all positive-class data synthetically generated---matches the full-data model within 0.2\% relative on violating F1, demonstrating that new policies can launch without any real violation data.

</details>


### [101] [Memory That Looks Forward: A Zero-Inference Prospective Term for Personal Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22091)
*Groff, Jonathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零推理成本的前瞻性记忆检索项，通过显式承诺账本对关联记忆进行显著性增强，并与嵌入相似度乘法融合。在合成的预期记忆任务集上，可将困难层的recall@5从0.000提升至0.955，且无误增强。但评估集为作者自建，需等待TriggerBench正式数据进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统记忆检索是回溯式的，无法处理用户承诺的未来事项（前瞻性记忆），导致相关记忆在需要时无法被检索出来。

Method: 该架构包含一个显式承诺账本，条目为带日期或触发条件的记录；记忆条目与账本中触发的条目通过链接关联，获得显著性增强因子。检索时，将增强因子与基于嵌入的相似度得分进行乘法融合，从而在保持嵌入相关性主导的同时，提升与承诺相关的记忆的排序位置。整个检索过程不涉及额外的模型推理，增强因子在查询前预先计算。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall@5 (hard stratum)", "value": "0.000 → 0.955", "setting": "默认混合权重，在合成的预期记忆任务集（48个盲写对话，175个任务）上"}, {"metric": "recall@5 (hard stratum, floor variant)", "value": "1.000", "setting": "使用floor变体，相同数据集"}, {"metric": "false boosts", "value": "0", "setting": "在53个已解决的承诺任务上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出显式承诺账本，条目按日期或触发条件存储，无需查询时推理。；将承诺链接产生的显著性增强乘以嵌入相似度，保证相关性主权。；零查询时推理成本的前瞻项。

Abstract: Retrieval over a personal memory store is retrospective: it surfaces what resembles the query, and it is blind to what the user has committed to do. We describe a prospective term for memory retrieval that costs no inference at query time. Commitments are held in an explicit ledger as dated or trigger-conditioned entries; memory items linked to a firing entry receive a salience boost, blended multiplicatively into embedding-based retrieval so that relevance remains sovereign. On a synthetic prospective-memory task set modeled on TriggerBench's published structure (48 blind-authored dialogues, 175 tasks), the term raised recall@5 on the hard stratum from 0.000 to 0.955 at the default blend weight and to 1.000 under a floor variant, with zero false boosts across 53 resolved-commitment tasks. Blind authorship also produced a scope finding: only 17-29% of naturally phrased commitment-trigger pairs defeat embedding similarity, so the term matters on a real minority of cases and must do no harm on the rest, which it does not. We position precomputed commitment linkage as the always-on floor of a layered design whose expansion layer is query-time prospection. Results are preliminary: the evaluation set is author-constructed, and evaluation on TriggerBench proper is committed follow-up work once its data is released.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [RRSI: Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24972)
*Xia, Peng,Han, Rujun,Wang, Zifeng,Chen, Yanfei,Zhang, Yufan,Lee, Yoonho,Huang, Chengsong,Yu, Han,CuiZhu, Zhongying,Ming, Yifei,Yao, Huaxiu,Gokturk, Burak,Pfister, Tomas,Lee, Chen-Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRSI 提出带正则化的 Agent Harness 递归自我改进框架，通过约束提议与选择过程抑制对训练任务的过拟合，在八个基准上获得分布内最高 14.1 分、分布外最高 4.7 分提升，并减少 30% 策略 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Harness 的递归自我改进容易过拟合训练任务，导致分布内增益在分布外基准上大幅衰减或消失。

Method: RRSI 在冻结的骨干模型外围对 Agent Harness（包括提示、控制流、工具、记忆与上下文管理）进行递归自我改进。流程为：提议器迭代生成组件级编辑候选，编辑受时间退火预算约束（限制单候选捆绑的编辑数量），并依据进化历史鼓励探索未探索轨迹；选择器包含 critic 与 pruner，critic 先筛除基准特定提议，pruner 再移除幅度过小、代价过高或不再有用的改动。经过约束后的候选在进化过程中被保留或淘汰，最终产出一个可复用的 harness。该方法在八个编码、Agent 工作区与工程设计任务基准上验证，分布内最高提升 14.1 分，五个分布外基准最高提升 4.7 分，同时策略 token 消耗减少 30%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "进化基准得分", "value": "最高提升 14.1 分", "setting": "与未正则化进化对比（进化所用 split）"}, {"metric": "分布外基准得分", "value": "最高提升 4.7 分", "setting": "五个分布外基准对比"}, {"metric": "Policy Token 消耗", "value": "降低 30%", "setting": "与未正则化进化产生的 harness 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将正则化思想引入 Harness 自我改进，约束候选提议与选择过程。；提议器采用时间退火预算限制单个候选可捆绑的编辑数量，并基于进化历史鼓励探索未探索轨迹。；选择器配备 critic 与 pruner：critic 过滤基准特定提议，pruner 移除过小、过于昂贵或不再有用的改动。；整体机制偏向可复用 Agent 机制而非基准特定或噪声改动。

Abstract: An LLM agent's capability is largely magnified by its harness, namely the prompts, control flow, tooling, memory, and context management surrounding the frozen backbone model. Recent methods increasingly automate this process by iteratively proposing and selecting component-wise edits of an agent harness, practically establishing a form of recursive self-improvement (RSI) at the agent-system level. However, such recursive evolution may overfit by memorizing the training tasks, showing large in-distribution gains that shrink or even vanish on out-of-distribution benchmarks. We introduce Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses (RRSI), which incorporates the principles of regularizations into harness self-improvement by constraining the evolution candidate proposal and selection. The proposer operates with a temporally annealed budget, limiting how many edits a candidate can bundle, and it encourages unexplored trajectories based on evolution history. The selector is equipped with a critic and a pruner: the critic screens benchmark-specific proposals, while the pruner, removes changes that are too small, too expensive, or no longer useful. Together these constraints favor reusable agent mechanisms over benchmark-specific ones or even noises. Across eight benchmarks spanning coding, agentic workspace and engineering design tasks, RRSI gains up to 14.1 points on the split it evolves against and up to 4.7 points on the five out-of-distribution benchmarks, while producing a harness that runs on 30% fewer policy tokens than the unregularized evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/google-research/rrsi and project page is https://regularized-rsi.com/.

</details>


### [103] [Rare Event Estimation via Iterative Unalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24969)
*Yang, Hanming,Mittal, Daksh,Dong, Jing,Namkoong, Hongseok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出迭代未对齐方法，通过可微分权重扰动进行重要性采样，高效估计语言模型智能体轨迹中极稀有事件概率，在120M和2.6B模型上实现800倍计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计语言模型智能体随机输出轨迹中极稀有事件（低至10^-9）的发生概率。

Method: 该方法将提议分布参数化为可微分语言模型，通过扰动原模型权重实现。具体流程：给定原模型和稀有事件定义，首先初始化提议模型权重（可能从原模型微调或扰动）；然后对每个稀有事件族，使用梯度上升优化目标函数，该函数包含一个可微的事件放大代理项（通过可微采样或松弛技术实现）和一个自适应正则化项，正则化项根据估计器方差动态调整，平衡探索与稳定性。优化过程中，提议模型生成轨迹，计算重要性权重，并更新权重参数以最大化事件发生概率同时控制方差。最终，使用优化后的提议模型进行重要性采样，估计稀有事件概率，并计算估计器的相对标准误。推理阶段，提议模型可用于高效采样稀有事件轨迹。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "计算加权效率提升", "value": "800倍以上", "setting": "概率低于10^-7的事件，对比朴素蒙特卡洛，模型规模120M和2.6B"}, {"metric": "相对标准误", "value": "<10%", "setting": "参考概率计算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过扰动原模型权重构造重要性采样提议分布，并使其可微分以进行梯度搜索；提出结合事件放大可微代理和自适应正则化的目标函数，动态平衡放大与估计器稳定性

Abstract: As agents are deployed with increased autonomy, even extremely rare events along their stochastic output trajectories can occur and prove catastrophic. Safe deployment therefore does not depend on whether these events can occur, but on how often they might. We study the problem of estimating the probability of rare events that arise from stochastic variation in the agent's own actions. Estimating this type of risk requires searching over the combinatorially vast space of trajectories. Naive Monte Carlo is computationally prohibitive in this regime, and constructing effective importance sampling (IS) proposals requires coordinated changes to a context-dependent chain of conditional distributions. We develop a new IS method that perturbs the original model's weights to construct the proposal. The proposal is itself a differentiably parameterized language model, enabling gradient-based search over weight space. We formulate an objective that combines a differentiable surrogate for event amplification and an adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically balances amplification against estimator stability. We evaluate our approach on $\sim$120M and $\sim$2.6B models across three event families spanning 300+ rare events as rare as $10^{-9}$, with reference probabilities computed with $<10\%$ relative standard error. In our most verifiable settings, we observe that our IS estimator achieves over $800\times$ compute-weighted efficiency gains over naive Monte Carlo for events with probabilities lower than $10^{-7}$. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/namkoong-lab/iterative-unalignment.

</details>


### [104] [iSDFT: Information-Proximal Self-Distillation for Continual Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24646)
*Khamis, Ahmed Khaled,Ji, Xiaotong,Jaber, Hassan,Tutunov, Rasul,Zimmer, Matthieu,Wang, Jun,Bou-Ammar, Haitham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iSDFT 提出一种信息近端自蒸馏微调方法，在每个 token 上以教师信息预算为约束、选择最接近学生的闭式指数目标分布，并锚定冻结基础策略以控制漂移。在四个 LLM 主干和两个任务上，多数设置优于 SDFT，并在保留基准上取得更紧的保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy 自蒸馏微调（SDFT）总是向完整的演示条件教师蒸馏，无法在每个预测状态控制应传递多少演示信息，导致对教师影响的控制不足，难以在习得新技能的同时保持原有能力。

Method: iSDFT 在每个 token 预测状态上，将教师模型视为受信息预算约束的信息源，而非固定向完整教师蒸馏。学生模型在生成轨迹上逐 token 决策：给定当前学生分布和教师分布，寻找一个目标分布，使其在满足教师信息量约束（如与教师在某种距离度量下的信息差异不超过预算）的前提下，尽可能接近当前学生分布，从而得到闭式指数形式的目标分布，其倾斜程度由局部信息约束决定。为避免多步累积产生漂移，iSDFT 进一步将学生锚定到其冻结的基础策略，形成稳定性约束。训练目标综合了每个 token 的信息约束蒸馏损失和锚定正则项，通过优化该目标更新学生模型。推理时直接使用微调后的学生模型，无额外计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "模型-任务设置改进数", "value": "8 个设置中 7 个优于 vanilla SDFT，剩余 1 个持平", "setting": "四个异构 LLM 主干、两个专业化任务"}, {"metric": "保留评估比例（与基础模型差距在 0.5 分以内）", "value": "73%", "setting": "原始 SDFT 基准套件，对比最强基线 52%"}, {"metric": "平均提升", "value": "最大平均提升", "setting": "额外十个数学、编码和竞赛数学基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将教师视为有预算的信息源，在每个 token 上选择在满足规定教师信息约束下最接近当前学生分布的分布，得到具有局部确定倾斜的闭式指数目标。；为控制累积漂移，将学生锚定到其冻结的基础策略。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation fine-tuning (SDFT) learns new skills from demonstrations while reducing forgetting, but it always distils toward the full demonstration-conditioned teacher. This fixes teacher influence at the full-teacher endpoint, providing no control over how much demonstration information should be transferred at each prediction state. We introduce Information-Proximal SDFT (iSDFT), which instead treats the teacher as a budgeted source of information. At each token, iSDFT selects the distribution closest to the current student that satisfies a prescribed teacher-information constraint, yielding a closed-form exponential target with a locally determined tilt. To control cumulative drift, we further anchor the student to its frozen base policy. Across four heterogeneous LLM backbones and two specialisation tasks, iSDFT improves vanilla SDFT in 7 of 8 model-task settings and matches it in the remaining one. It also provides tighter retention on the original SDFT benchmark suite, with 73% of evaluations remaining within 0.5 points of the base model versus 52% for the strongest baseline, while achieving the largest mean improvement on all ten additional mathematics, coding, and competition-mathematics benchmarks. These results show that controlling how much and when teacher information is introduced improves specialisation while preserving broader capability.

</details>


### [105] [ARM: Attention with Routed-Memory for Learnable Sparse Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24417)
*Zeng, Qiuhao,Huang, Jerry,Lu, Peng,Fang, Ruiyi,Xu, Gezheng,Jing, Zihao,Cui, Yufei,Ling, Charles,Niu, Gang,Wang, Boyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARM提出可微的固定大小分层路由记忆替代传统KV缓存，用Gumbel-Softmax选择槽位、sigmoid门控软更新，并由策略网络动态控制记忆访问量，在长上下文推理中兼顾质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长上下文推理中受限于KV缓存增长：选择性token驱逐/剪枝虽可缓解，但会丢弃关键信息，导致质量损失。

Method: ARM替换传统KV缓存：维护固定数量的记忆槽位，并以分层路由器组织。推理时，对每个查询/写入，路由器通过Gumbel-Softmax在槽位间进行可微选择，实现稀疏访问；被选中的槽位通过sigmoid门控更新，将新信息与已存信息软性融合，避免硬驱逐导致的信息丢失。此外训练一个策略网络，根据输入自适应决定使用的记忆量：简单上下文少量访问，复杂推理更多访问。该结构整体可端到端训练，长上下文场景下保持固定内存预算并降低生成延迟。论文称在常识与长上下文推理基准上优于固定KV缓存方法，但未披露具体数据集、基线细节与消融数值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出完全可微、固定大小的分层路由记忆系统ARM，作为新的KV缓存结构；通过Gumbel-Softmax学习选择记忆槽位，并用sigmoid门控软更新新旧信息，避免硬驱逐；训练策略网络在推理时动态选择记忆访问量，以适配简单上下文与需深度推理的输入

Abstract: Despite advances in long-context inference, large language models (LLMs) remain fundamentally limited by the key-value (KV) caching mechanisms that are necessary for stable computation. Techniques such as selective token eviction and pruning have vastly mitigated these issues, but often discard core information to manage the growing cache. In this paper, we propose Attention with Routed Memory (ARM) a novel KV caching structure that introduces a fully differentiable, fixed-size memory system organized as a hierarchical router. Via a Gumbel-Softmax, ARM learns to select memory slots and perform sigmoid-gated updates that softly combine new and stored information, avoiding hard eviction and reducing information loss. By further training a policy to dynamically select varying amounts of memory at inference, ARM adapts its accesses for both simple contexts and inputs that require deeper reasoning, enabling more scalable and effective retrieval on both short- and long-contexts. Experimental results on standard commonsense and long-context reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ARM achieves superior performance and efficiency compared to fixed KV-caching approaches, while remaining efficient and scalable in terms of both memory and generation latency.

</details>


### [106] [Artificial Structure Function Search: Preserving Artificial Functional Connectivity for Structured Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24401)
*Illeperuma, Mindula,Pina, Rafael,Herath, Charuka,Gabayre, Sharmarke A.,De Silva, Varuna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASF-S 是一种新型结构化剪枝框架，通过受神经科学启发的 PGI 准则和 AFC 约束选择剪枝候选，在 70% 参数减少下无需重训练即可恢复基线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法依赖不透明的启发式或权重准则，无法反映网络中的结构依赖关系，导致剪枝后模型精度下降或需要昂贵的重训练。

Method: ASF-S 框架包含两个核心组件：1) Principle Gradient Importance (PGI) 剪枝准则：基于梯度信息评估神经元或通道的重要性，灵感来自大脑结构-功能关系；2) 人工功能连接 (AFC)：确保剪枝后的结构保持输出层的拓扑组织。整体流程：首先计算每个候选结构的 PGI 分数，然后根据 AFC 约束选择剪枝候选，形成剪枝后的子网络。剪枝后的模型无需对剪枝层进行重训练即可恢复基线精度。论文未披露具体的网络架构、训练目标或推理流程细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "参数减少", "value": "70%", "setting": "结构化剪枝后模型变体"}, {"metric": "精度恢复", "value": "恢复基线精度", "setting": "无需重训练剪枝层"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 受大脑结构-功能关系启发的 Principle Gradient Importance (PGI) 剪枝候选选择准则；定义人工功能连接 (AFC) 以确保剪枝结构尊重输出层的拓扑组织；提出结构化剪枝框架 ASF-S，可在无需重训练剪枝层的情况下恢复基线精度并实现 70% 参数减少

Abstract: Structured pruning is a model compression technique that is used to reduce the computational cost of deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. Popular methods of pruning rely on opaque heuristics or weight-based criteria that give no indication as to the structural dependencies in the network. To address these limitations we present Artificial Structure Function Search (ASF-S): a novel structured pruning framework. ASF-S utilizes Principle Gradient Importance (PGI): a novel prune-candidate selection criteria that is inspired by structure-function relationships in the brain. By ensuring the pruned structure of the model respects topographical organization of the output layer, we define Artificial Functional Connectivity (AFC) for artificial neural networks. AFC provides evidence to demonstrate that accurate smaller networks can be found using careful prune candidate selection criteria. We present results for PGI as a selection criterion and for ASF-S as a pruning framework against recent benchmarks, demonstrating that our method yields model variants with 70\% parameter reduction, that can recover baseline accuracy without re-training the pruned layers.

</details>


### [107] [Information-Time Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24380)
*Zeng, Yongcheng,Cui, Xinyu,Song, Yan,Liu, Guoqing,Xin, Hongsheng,Zhang, Kaike,Deng, Cheng,Zhan, Kun,Ying, Jian,Zhao, Jian,Zhang, Haifeng,Wang, Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoPPO 针对 RLVR 中 token 级时间参数化导致折扣失效的问题，提出以信息密度重新参数化 MDP 时间，并据此设计状态相关的自适应裁剪阈值，在 Qwen3 上于五个数学推理基准取得一致提升且对折扣设置更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法按 token 逐个生成来参数化 MDP 的时间推进，但自回归轨迹上的信息流高度不均匀，导致折扣因子在长序列推理中失效：要么有效视界收缩不足，要么终端监督被过度衰减。

Method: InfoPPO 在 RLVR 框架下改造 PPO 的时间轴与策略更新机制。输入为 LLM 自回归生成轨迹（prompt、逐步 token 与最终奖励）。核心模块一：信息时间重参数化——将 MDP 的时间步从 token 计数改为状态级信息密度，使得折扣因子作用在信息量变化而非固定 token 间隔上，从而在长序列中保留有效视界收缩同时避免终端奖励过早衰减。核心模块二：状态相关自适应裁剪——基于信息密度动态调整每个 token 位置的 PPO 裁剪阈值，信息密度高的位置允许更大幅度的策略更新，低密度位置保持更严格近端约束。训练目标为在信息时间 MDP 下的策略优化，结合裁剪后的重要性采样比与折扣回报。理论部分推导信息时间 MDP 下的性能差与策略改进下界，并将状态信息密度与局部策略移动关联。推理/部署路径与标准 RLVR 训练的采样-更新循环一致，仅策略更新规则不同；未披露具体推理硬件或延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学推理准确率", "value": "在五个竞赛式数学推理基准上相对竞争基线取得一致提升，且在 token-time PPO 退化的非平凡折扣设置下保持稳定准确率与响应长度（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "Qwen3 模型，五个挑战性竞赛风格数学推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用信息密度而非原始 token 数重新参数化 MDP 时间推进，使时间信用传播与策略更新共享状态相关结构；在该信息时间下恢复非平凡折扣的有效性，既保留有效视界收缩又避免长 token 序列对终端监督的过度衰减；通过自适应裁剪实现状态相关的更新约束：依据每个 token 位置对应状态的信息密度调整裁剪阈值；将性能差与策略改进分析扩展到信息时间 MDP，推导出由信息密度调控策略变化时的策略改进下界；建立状态级信息密度与局部策略移动的联系，为自适应更新机制提供理论依据

Abstract: RLVR has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. However, existing methods typically parameterize temporal progression in the Markov Decision Process by token-by-token generation, despite the highly non-uniform information flow along autoregressive trajectories. In this paper, we propose InfoPPO, which reparameterizes temporal progression using information density rather than raw token count. This reparameterization induces a common state-dependent structure for both temporal credit propagation and policy updates. InfoPPO restores the effectiveness of non-trivial discounting in long-horizon reasoning, retaining effective-horizon contraction while avoiding excessive attenuation of terminal supervision over long token sequences. Moreover, the information-time policy-improvement analysis naturally leads to a state-dependent update constraint, which we implement through adaptive clipping. By adapting the clipping threshold at each token position to the information density of its corresponding state, this mechanism enables more targeted policy updates while preserving proximal control. Theoretically, we extend performance-difference and policy-improvement analyses to the information-time MDP, deriving a policy-improvement lower bound when policy changes are regulated by information density. We further connect the general information-time analysis to practical LLM policy optimization by relating state-wise information density to local policy movement, while also providing theoretical grounding for the adaptive update mechanism. Experiments on Qwen3 models demonstrate consistent gains over competitive baselines across five challenging competition-style mathematical reasoning benchmarks. InfoPPO also maintains stable accuracy and response length across non-trivial discount settings under which token-time PPO deteriorates.

</details>


### [108] [The Undetected Damage of Quantization on Retrieval and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24322)
*Zhou, Luca,Zirilli, Alessandro,Solombrino, Daniele,DessÃ¬, Roberto,RodolÃ , Emanuele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示量化对检索top-1结果的隐性损害（14%-46%变化），提出基于top-1与top-2分数差的无标签诊断指标，并给出检索场景的混合精度分配和分类场景的低gap路由修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化后模型分类精度不变但检索top-1结果发生14%-46%变化，聚合排序指标无法揭示这一损害，需要一种无需标签的机制判断量化答案是否可信并分配额外精度。

Method: 论文研究量化模型在检索任务中的行为。输入为查询-文档对，模型输出查询-文档分数。量化后，模型对每个输入产生一组分数，取top-1和top-2分数的差值作为gap。理论分析表明：仅当gap大于两倍最大舍入误差时，top-1结果在量化前后保持一致。分类任务中，分数为logits，损失函数推动正确类远离其他类，使得gap通常较大；检索任务中，分数为query-document相似度，没有任何机制分离top-1和top-2，因此gap通常较小，量化更容易改变top-1结果。该gap可无标签测量，用于部署前预测模型风险（gap小的模型容易在量化下失效）和部署时逐输入判断量化答案是否可信。基于此，检索任务中提出混合精度方案：计算各层量化对gap的移动程度，对移动最大的层分配额外比特宽度，从而以一半成本恢复最高四分之三的额外比特收益；分类任务中则采用路由策略，将低gap输入路由到全精度计算，其余输入使用量化模型，以极小成本恢复大部分精度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "top-1 retrieval result change", "value": "14-46%", "setting": "量化模型保留分类精度时"}, {"metric": "extra bit benefit recovery", "value": "最高四分之三", "setting": "检索任务，对分数差移动最大的层增加比特宽度，成本为额外比特的一半"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明仅当top-1与top-2分数差超过两倍最大舍入误差时，top-1结果才保证在量化后幸存；提出无需标签的分数差度量，可在部署前预测模型是否会在量化下失效，部署时逐输入判断量化答案是否与全精度一致；针对检索任务，提出对量化移动分数差最大的层分配额外比特宽度的混合精度方法，以一半成本恢复最高四分之三的额外比特收益；针对分类任务，提出将少量低分数差输入路由到全精度，以极少成本恢复大部分精度

Abstract: We show that a quantized model that keeps its classification accuracy still changes $14$ to $46\%$ of its top-1 retrieval results, and that aggregate ranking metrics reveal only part of this damage. We tie this failure to the gap between the two highest scores and use that gap to decide when a quantized answer can be trusted and where additional precision should be spent. We show that the top-1 result is guaranteed to survive quantization only when this gap exceeds twice the largest rounding error. In classification, scores are the logits, and the loss function pushes the correct class away from other classes, encouraging this gap. In retrieval, scores are query-document scores, and nothing separates the top-1 item from the second. This gap can be measured without labels. Before deployment, it predicts which models will break under quantization, and at deployment time it tells, per input, whether the quantized answer still matches the full-precision answer. Most classification inputs have a gap wide enough to trust the quantized answer, but few retrieval queries do. That gap motivates a different fix in each task. In retrieval, spending extra bit-width on the layers whose quantization moves the gap most recovers up to three-quarters of an extra bit's benefit for half its cost. In classification, routing the few low-gap inputs to full precision recovers most of the lost accuracy at a fraction of the cost.

</details>


### [109] [TTSE: A Two-Track Online Self-Evolution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24289)
*Pei, Ruimin,Wu, Yongkang,Zheng, Shangyi,Zhang, Yaqing,Li, Deyang,Tao, Jianjun,Zhang, Xinyu,Zhang, Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTSE是一个面向LLM智能体的双轨在线自演化框架，把知识拆成需持续验证的环境事实（FACT）与任务条件化流程（TIP），并从决策论给出超额风险的分解与界。实验涵盖GDPevo消融、ALFWorld/ScienceWorld、SOPBench五大领域三次重复，以及PinchBench端到端三次运行，报告稳定优于基线并与既有技能自演化方法兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法把环境知识当作外部固定输入，而非智能体自身持续演化的一部分；强化学习方法通常只适配固定任务分布或单一环境，难以在连续交互环境中同步演化环境表示与任务条件化执行能力。

Method: TTSE是一个双轨在线自演化框架，将智能体需持续更新的知识显式拆分为两条轨道：FACT轨道承载环境事实，其可靠性并非一次性写入而是通过持续积累的交互证据不断验证；TIP轨道承载任务条件化的实现流程，即在不同任务条件下如何执行的知识。框架从决策论出发，把智能体的超额风险分解为环境表示悔值（因环境事实识别不准确导致的损失）与条件化执行悔值（因未按条件匹配执行导致的损失），并刻画环境条件化策略严格优于条件无关策略的条件，同时用FACT识别误差与跨条件失配代价给出下游风险的界。在线运行中，两条轨道随交互持续演化，FACT依据交互证据被验证与修正，TIP依据任务条件被组织与更新；推理阶段框架通过基于检索的注入方式将演化得到的知识接入通用智能体框架，使智能体在具体任务（如PinchBench端到端任务）执行时调用相应的FACT与TIP。实验路径包括：在GDPevo上进行双轨与单轨的消融，在ALFWorld与ScienceWorld上评测任务适应能力，与Bayesian-Agent算法结合后在SOPBench五大领域进行三次独立重复验证双轨相对单轨的优势，并在PinchBench上以检索式注入集成到通用智能体框架，三次独立运行对比基线。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件、时延或吞吐等资源信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "双轨与单轨消融对比优势", "value": "未给出具体数值，仅报告双轨演化在GDPevo上具有优势", "setting": "GDPevo消融实验"}, {"metric": "任务适应性能", "value": "未给出具体数值，报告优于基线", "setting": "ALFWorld与ScienceWorld经典智能体任务基准"}, {"metric": "SOPBench五大领域聚合分数", "value": "未给出具体数值，报告三次独立重复中显著提升", "setting": "与Bayesian-Agent算法结合的单轨消融对比，三次独立重复"}, {"metric": "PinchBench端到端任务表现", "value": "未给出具体数值，报告三次独立运行中稳定优于基线", "setting": "真实端到端任务基准PinchBench，检索式注入集成到通用智能体框架"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出双轨在线自演化框架TTSE，将演化知识显式拆分为FACT（环境事实，其可靠性通过交互证据持续验证）与TIP（任务条件化实现流程）两条独立轨道；从决策论角度将智能体的超额风险分解为环境表示悔值和条件化执行悔值，刻画环境条件化策略严格优于条件无关策略的条件，并用FACT识别误差与跨条件失配代价界定下游风险；在通用智能体框架中通过基于检索的注入方式集成TTSE，验证与既有技能自演化方法的广泛兼容性

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents are applied in continuously interactive environments, driving the evolution of their own capabilities becomes a core problem for achieving long-term autonomy. Currently, environmental knowledge is typically treated as an external fixed input rather than as part of the agent's ongoing evolution. Reinforcement learning methods usually optimize policies through environmental interaction but tend to adapt only to fixed task distributions or single environments. This paper proposes TTSE (Two-Track Self-Evolution), a dual-track online self-evolution framework that separates evolving knowledge into FACT (environmental facts, whose reliability is continuously verified through interaction evidence) and TIP (task-conditioned implementation procedures). From a decision-theoretic perspective, we decompose the agent's excess risk into environment-representation regret and conditional-execution regret, characterize the conditions under which environment-conditioned policies strictly outperform condition-agnostic policies, and bound the downstream risk in terms of FACT identification error and cross-condition mismatch cost. In practice, TTSE's ablation experiments on GDPevo validate the advantage of dual-track evolution. On the classic agent task benchmarks ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, TTSE further demonstrates superior task adaptation. Moreover, TTSE is broadly compatible with existing skill self-evolution methods; combined with the Bayesian-Agent algorithm, a single-track ablation validates the dual-track advantage, substantially improving the aggregate score across the five major domains of SOPBench over three independent repetitions. Finally, on the real end-to-end task benchmark PinchBench, TTSE is integrated into a general agent framework via retrieval-based injection and stably outperforms the baseline across three independent runs.

</details>


### [110] [RLVR$^{2}$: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23457)
*Li, Hao,Zhang, Zhengkun,Hu, Gangqiang,Zhang, Zhen,Gao, Yude,Dai, Dai,Liu, Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR² 提出一种基于可验证排序的评分细则强化学习范式，通过组内序数比较避免异构评分尺度的校准问题，并支持辅助属性调整。在3个模型规模和16个基准测试上优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维度评分细则下的 RLVR 中，如何将多个标准分数聚合成标量奖励，现有线性组合方法假设评分差异可比且可补偿，但同质标准下不可靠。

Method: RLVR² 将每个标准的评分细则分数转换为组内序数结果（配对比较），构建比较矩阵。通过偏好聚合方法（如 Bradley-Terry 模型）从比较矩阵中恢复潜在效用，然后将这些效用合并为单一训练信号用于策略优化。该方法支持在不扩展评分细则的情况下，将辅助属性纳入估计，从而保持原始目标。推理时直接使用训练好的策略模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出可验证的排序范式：将评分细则分数转化为标准内的组内序数结果，避免异构评分尺度的校准。；从比较矩阵中恢复潜在效用，并合并为单一训练信号。；支持目标保持的属性调整：与观察排序相关但非训练目标的辅助属性可进入估计，而无需扩展评分细则或直接奖励。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is expanding from tasks with well-defined correctness signals, such as mathematics and code, toward multifaceted quality requirements specified by multi-dimensional rubrics. Since policy optimization consumes one scalar per rollout, rubric-based pipelines must map multiple criterion scores into a scalar reward. This aggregation is often treated as score scaling, but it implicitly determines how quality dimensions trade off during training. The prevailing practice, normalizing each criterion and taking a linear combination, assumes that cardinal score differences are comparable across criteria and that gains on one criterion compensate for failures on another; both assumptions are unreliable when criteria are semantically heterogeneous. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking (RLVR$^2$), a verifiable ranking paradigm for rubric-based RLVR. For each criterion, RLVR$^2$ converts rubric scores into criterion-specific within-group ordinal outcomes, recovers a latent utility from the resulting comparison matrix, and merges these utilities into one training signal. By retaining only within-group ordering and discarding raw score magnitudes, RLVR$^2$ avoids calibrating heterogeneous rubric scales. It further supports objective-preserving attribute adjustment: auxiliary attributes that correlate with observed rankings but are not training objectives can enter the estimation without expanding the rubric or rewarding them directly. Across three model scales and 16 benchmarks, RLVR$^2$ consistently outperforms representative rubric-based baselines, achieving the best overall performance on most benchmarks at every scale. Analysis shows it controls systematic effects tied to reasoning efficiency and response formatting while preserving the quality objective.

</details>


### [111] [Why Ghost Outputs Teach: A Kernel-Based Understanding of Subliminal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23260)
*Li, Zhe,Ying, Bicheng,Dong, Chaosheng,Yang, Haibo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从学习动力学角度对 Subliminal Learning 现象给出机械化解释，提出 chained cross-task kernel 框架，统一解释了 ghost-output 监督有效性的三个核心谜题，并通过实验验证理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Subliminal Learning 现象中，学生模型仅通过匹配教师模型看似无关的辅助输出（ghost outputs）即可获得下游任务能力，但其优化机制不清晰。

Method: 论文未披露具体模型架构细节，但基于标准的学生-教师训练设置。学生模型在共享初始化下，接收教师模型生成的 ghost outputs（看似无关的辅助输出）进行监督训练。通过共享主干表示的链式跨任务核，将 ghost-output 监督的变化传播到下游任务预测。理论分析表明，在共享初始化下，跨任务迁移算子呈半正定结构，使得优化过程隐式对齐教师的任务目标；ghost output 的维度构成秩瓶颈，限制可迁移的任务相关特征数量；高熵输入作为宽带探针最大化核重叠，促进迁移。实验在典型 ghost-output 设置上验证了三个理论预测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 chained cross-task kernel 将 ghost-output 监督与任务预测变化通过共享主干表示联系起来；证明共享初始化下转移算子具有严格半正定（PSD）结构，保证 ghost-output 优化对齐学生与教师真实任务目标；揭示 ghost-output 维度作为显式秩瓶颈控制任务相关特征迁移；解释高熵合成输入作为宽带探针最大化跨任务核重叠，从而优于结构化数据

Abstract: Subliminal Learning (SL) is a recently identified phenomenon in which a student model acquires downstream task capabilities by matching seemingly unrelated auxiliary outputs from a teacher, despite never observing task labels, task-specific outputs, or the original training data. While recent studies have identified where subliminal signals may reside, the optimization mechanism underlying this phenomenon remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide a mechanistic understanding of SL through the lens of learning dynamics. Specifically, we derive a chained cross-task kernel that explicitly links ghost-output supervision to changes in task predictions through shared backbone representations. Our unified analytical framework provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for three central empirical puzzles in SL: (i) under shared initialization, the transfer operator forms a strictly Positive Semi-Definite (PSD) structure, guaranteeing that ghost-output optimization aligns the student with the teacher's true task objective without explicit label exposure; (ii) the ghost-output dimensionality acts as an explicit rank bottleneck governing the transfer of task-relevant features; and (iii) synthetic, high-entropy inputs function as broadband probes that maximize cross-task kernel overlap, explaining why random noise consistently outperforms structured data for subliminal transfer. Experiments on the canonical ghost-output setting validate all three theoretical predictions, providing the first learning-dynamics-based theoretical explanation of how ghost-output supervision gives rise to subliminal learning.

</details>


### [112] [Merge++: Universal Merge Refinement Through Data-Free Checkpoint Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22886)
*Pola, Aditya,Balasubramanian, Vineeth N.*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Merge++ 提出了一种无需数据的模型合并细化方法，通过反转专家检查点合成任务图像并蒸馏专家知识，显著提升合并模型性能，平均提升2-8个点，最高达25.9点，适用于多种合并算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据模型合并方法仅在权重空间进行算术操作，无法利用通过前向评估才能获得的行为信号来提升合并模型的多任务性能。

Method: Merge++ 作为一种后处理细化阶段，首先接收多个微调专家检查点，通过反转（inversion）过程合成任务代表性图像。这些合成图像用于蒸馏专家知识：将每个专家的预测行为（基于合成图像的前向输出）作为软目标，指导合并模型更新。合并模型先由任意权重空间合并算法（如任务算术、谱方法）生成初始权重，然后通过蒸馏损失进行微调，从而融入行为信号。整个过程无需额外数据，仅依赖检查点本身。最终得到一个细化的多任务合并模型，适用于下游推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均性能提升", "value": "+2 至 +8 点", "setting": "多种合并算法（任务算术、谱方法等）"}, {"metric": "单配置最大提升", "value": "+25.9", "setting": "特定合并配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Merge++，一种无数据的后处理细化方法，通过反转专家检查点合成任务代表性图像。；利用合成的图像将专家知识蒸馏到合并模型中，无需额外数据。；方法通用，可应用于多种合并算法（如任务算术、谱方法等），作为独立的细化阶段。

Abstract: Model merging consolidates fine-tuned experts into one multi-task model without retraining. All existing data-free methods approach this problem entirely in weight space. Restricted to arithmetic on parameters, these methods never observe how each expert behaves, a signal that only emerges through forward evaluation. Accessing this behavioral signal requires inputs to evaluate on, which the data-free setting prohibits. We propose Merge++, a post-hoc method that addresses this by inverting the expert checkpoints to synthesize task-representative images, then distilling expert knowledge into the merged model using those images. Merge++ requires no additional data beyond the checkpoints themselves. It applies universally across merging algorithms and operates as a complementary refinement stage independent of the underlying weight-space method. The method consistently improves merging algorithms ranging from simple task arithmetic to state-of-the-art spectral methods, with average gains of +2 to +8 points and up to +25.9 on individual configurations.

</details>


### [113] [Resist, Update, Reject: Preference Optimization Installs a Prior-Dependent Reliability Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22359)
*Yang, Sen,Yeung, Yuen-Hei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明常用偏好优化目标无法识别来源可靠性，提出阈值基准并通过平衡覆盖的偏好优化安装先验依赖的可靠性开关，在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B上验证，决策准确率最高0.86，且可泛化到未见可靠性与标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化目标无法区分来源可靠性，导致模型在抵抗操纵、可靠更新与不可靠拒绝之间形成固定-轻信前沿；需通过数据设计使可靠性可识别，并验证偏好优化能否安装基于先验的可靠性开关。

Method: 论文基于标准LLM（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Llama-3.1-8B）进行偏好优化。输入为包含源可靠性声明r的对话或断言模板；模型需输出是否翻转先验答案。训练使用平衡覆盖的偏好数据，覆盖不同可靠性值r，优化DPO或IPO目标。该目标使模型学会将r与先验强度p比较，当r>p时翻转。推理时模型根据输入中声明的r和内部先验进行决策。控制实验改变配对方式、损失函数或训练目标（监督模仿）以定位原因。评估在阈值基准上进行，测量决策准确率、翻转曲线单调性及泛化性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "决策准确率", "value": "0.84", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，三个随机种子，阈值基准测试"}, {"metric": "翻转曲线单调性 (Spearman)", "value": "0.56", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "决策准确率", "value": "0.80", "setting": "不匹配变体，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "决策准确率", "value": "0.86", "setting": "IPO优化器，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "决策准确率", "value": "0.50", "setting": "监督模仿基线，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "决策准确率", "value": "0.84", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B迁移，确认性测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明常用反谄媚偏好优化目标对来源可靠性不可识别，任何标量混合仅产生单一顺从度调节。；提出阈值基准测试：源以宣称可靠性r断言相反答案，正确动作是当r超过模型先验强度p时翻转。；通过平衡覆盖的偏好优化安装先验依赖的可靠性开关，并证明该开关可泛化到未见可靠性与不同标记。；控制实验分离原因：不匹配变体同样安装开关，IPO同样有效，而监督模仿无效，表明原因在于带可靠性标签覆盖的偏好优化而非配对、损失或模仿。

Abstract: An aligned model asked to hold its answer against a manipulative source must still update on a reliable one and reject an unreliable one: resistance, reliable-update, and unreliable-source rejection are one three-way contract, not three independent behaviors. We show the objective most anti-sycophancy work optimizes is non-identifying with respect to source reliability: because no preference label depends on whether a source is actually reliable, any scalar mixture of the arms traces a single deference dial, and no point separates two same-template testimonies differing only in stated reliability. This fixation$\leftrightarrow$gullibility frontier is a property of the objective, not any model. We make reliability identifiable through data: a threshold benchmark where a source asserts the opposite answer while stating its reliability $r$, and the correct action is to flip iff $r$ exceeds the model's prior strength $p$. Preference optimization over balanced coverage installs a prior-dependent reliability switch: across three seeds on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct the threshold $r^\star$ rises monotonically with the prior, decision accuracy reaches $0.84$ with a monotone flip curve (Spearman $0.56$), and the policy generalizes to unseen reliability values and a held-out notation, following stated reliability over role prestige. Three controls localize the cause: an unmatched variant installs the switch equally ($0.80$), a second preference optimizer (IPO) installs it just as well ($0.86$), whereas supervised imitation does not ($0.50$), so the cause is preference optimization over reliability-labeled coverage, not pairing, loss, or imitation. A confirmatory battery replicates the switch on a fresh test draw, bounds it honestly (it keys on reliability stated in the testimony, not a separately audited record), and transfers it to Llama-3.1-8B. The frontier is empirical, not a theorem.

</details>


### [114] [RS-Claw-Evolution: Environment-Feedback-Driven Evolution for Lightweight Remote Sensing Agents in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22258)
*Ouyang, Kai,Hou, Dongyang,Liu, Liangtian,Wang, Zeyuan,Li, Ziyu,Liu, Chengfu,Tang, Zichao,Cui, Xuezhi,Ouyang, Shengwu,Yang, Wentao,Yu, Hanwen,Li, Haifeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RS-Claw-Evolution 提出面向轻量级遥感智能体的环境反馈驱动三阶段演化框架：交互演化用可执行代码维持长时程状态并压缩上下文，经验演化用失败感知轨迹与错误轮次掩码学习恢复经验，决策演化用多维环境奖励强化学习并做轮次级优势保护。基于 Qwen3-4B 的智能体在 Earth-Bench 自主规划模式达 65.9%，超越未训练 Qwen3-32B（43.8%）和 DeepSeek-V3.1（60.8%），接近 GPT-5（71.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级LLM驱动的遥感智能体在多步交互任务中面临长时程状态丢失、环境反馈利用效率低以及稀疏优化信号问题，难以完成长时程地理空间分析任务。

Method: RS-Claw-Evolution 是一个三阶段渐进式训练框架，用于构建轻量级遥感智能体。输入为多步交互式遥感分析任务，智能体需在长时程中调用工具并规划。第一阶段交互演化：智能体通过可执行代码控制观察（observation）、维护中间状态，并压缩上下文冗余，使轻量模型能在有限上下文内保持长时程状态。第二阶段经验演化：通过失败感知的轨迹生成策略，从包含失败与恢复的轨迹中挖掘信息，同时采用错误轮次掩码，在训练时屏蔽导致失败的动作，从而学习恢复行为而不模仿错误动作。第三阶段决策演化：采用强化学习，奖励由多维环境反馈构成，并引入轮次级优势保护机制，改善长序列中信用分配，优化工具调用与决策行为。推理时，训练后的轻量智能体执行代码控制观察、维护状态并调用工具完成遥感任务。论文未披露具体网络结构、训练数据规模、硬件资源及推理延迟等细节。
