Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Autonomous Planning 准确率", "value": "65.9%", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-based agent on Earth-Bench"}, {"metric": "Autonomous Planning 准确率", "value": "43.8%", "setting": "untrained Qwen3-32B baseline on Earth-Bench"}, {"metric": "Autonomous Planning 准确率", "value": "60.8%", "setting": "DeepSeek-V3.1 on Earth-Bench"}, {"metric": "Autonomous Planning 准确率", "value": "71.6%", "setting": "GPT-5 on Earth-Bench"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 交互演化：使用可执行代码控制观察、维护中间状态、减少上下文冗余；经验演化：失败感知轨迹生成结合错误轮次掩码，在不模仿错误动作的前提下从失败恢复经验中学习；决策演化：利用多维度环境奖励和轮次级优势保护进行强化学习，优化工具使用行为并改进长序列信用分配

Abstract: Large language model-driven remote sensing (RS) agents offer a promising approach to automating geospatial analysis. However, lightweight RS agents based on compact language models struggle with multi-step interactive tasks due to loss of long-horizon states, inefficient environmental feedback utilization, and sparse optimization signals. We propose RS-Claw-Evolution, an environment-feedback-driven framework that progressively improves lightweight agents through three stages. Interaction evolution uses executable code to control observations, maintain intermediate states, and reduce context redundancy. Experience evolution combines failure-aware trajectory generation with error-turn masking to learn from informative failure-recovery experiences without imitating faulty actions. Decision evolution uses reinforcement learning with multi-dimensional environment rewards and turn-level advantage protection to optimize tool-use behaviors and improve credit assignment in long sequences. On Earth-Bench, the optimized Qwen3-4B-based agent achieves 65.9% accuracy in Autonomous Planning mode, outperforming the untrained Qwen3-32B baseline (43.8%) and DeepSeek-V3.1 (60.8%), while approaching GPT-5 (71.6%). These results demonstrate that learning from environmental feedback can improve lightweight agents and narrow their performance gap with larger models in long-horizon RS tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution for Automated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22257)
*Zhao, Haoran,Du, Wei,Yang, Dingwen,Huang, Jixuan,Shang, Junlin,Fang, Lingyong,Guo, Ya,Gui, Tao,Zhang, Qi,Huang, Xuanjing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一个两阶段框架，旨在使自动化LLM微调搜索具有累积性。第一阶段通过多智能体流水线和MCTS探索训练策略，并由并行蒸馏Agent提取任务特定记录和带置信度的跨任务洞察，形成结构化经验库。第二阶段从经验库检索并选择相关经验来引导新任务的训练。在九个未见任务上的实验表明，累积经验将平均相对改进从3.2%提升至15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化微调系统是无状态的：每次搜索结束后会丢弃发现的策略、数据集洞察和超参数发现，导致每个新任务都必须从冷启动重复昂贵的搜索。

Method: SAGE是一个两阶段框架。第一阶段：多智能体流水线执行基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的探索，以发现有效的训练策略。一个并行的蒸馏Agent从探索过程中提取两类信息：任务特定的探索记录和带置信度评分的跨任务洞察；这些信息共同构成一个结构化的经验库。第二阶段：对于新任务，SAGE从经验库中检索相关经验，并选择适用于当前任务的经验来引导训练。检索和选择机制的具体实现细节未在摘要中披露。整个框架的目标是使自动化微调搜索具有累积性，避免每个新任务都从冷启动开始搜索。训练目标或优化方法未在摘要中明确说明。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均相对改进（相对于基线）", "value": "从3.2%提升至15.6%", "setting": "九个未见任务，涵盖单类别和跨类别设置，单轮执行"}, {"metric": "相对于无经验库的相同流水线的改进", "value": "12.4个百分点", "setting": "九个未见任务，涵盖单类别和跨类别设置，单轮执行"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段框架SAGE，使自动化微调搜索具有累积性：第一阶段通过多智能体流水线执行基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的探索，并行蒸馏Agent提取任务特定的探索记录与带置信度评分的跨任务洞察，共同构成结构化经验库；第二阶段从经验库检索相关经验并选择适用于新任务的部分来引导训练。；引入并行Distillation Agent从MCTS探索记录中提取任务特定经验与跨任务洞察，并为跨任务洞察赋予置信度评分。；将经验库作为可检索、可复用的结构化资源，在第二阶段通过检索与选择机制实现对新任务的引导执行。

Abstract: Producing task-specific large language models requires discovering effective training strategies through experimentation. Automated fine-tuning systems have made this experimentation feasible with far less manual effort. However, these systems are stateless: each search discards its discovered strategies, dataset insights, and hyperparameter findings once it ends. Every new task must then repeat this costly search from a cold start. To address this, we propose Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution (SAGE), a two-stage framework that makes automated fine-tuning search cumulative. In the first stage, a multi-agent pipeline performs Monte Carlo Tree Search-based exploration. A parallel Distillation Agent extracts task-specific exploration records and confidence-scored cross-task insights, which together constitute a structured experience repository. In the second stage, SAGE retrieves relevant experience from this repository and selects what applies to guide training on the new task. We evaluate SAGE on nine unseen tasks spanning both single- and cross-category settings. In single-round execution, SAGE's accumulated experience raises the average relative improvement over baseline from 3.2% to 15.6%, a 12.4-percentage-point gain over the same pipeline without it. These results show that persistent strategy experience provides effective guidance for automated fine-tuning on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Teacher Should Think Ahead: Adaptive Continuations for Reliable On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22254)
*Zhou, Jingang,Zhou, Yuyi,Guo, Haiyang,Wang, Xukai,Feng, Shuai,Gao, Sirui,Xu, Jian,Guo, Qingpei,Zhang, Xu-Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 on-policy distillation 中教师监督不可靠的问题，论文识别教师不确定性收缩现象，理论分析续写长度的方差-偏差权衡，提出自适应选择教师续写视界的 AC-OPD 方法，在数学推理和代码生成上持续优于标准 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation 中，教师模型在学生生成的不完整或低质量前缀上提供的 token 级监督不可靠，存在教师不确定性收缩（TUC）现象，导致监督信号偏差。

Method: AC-OPD 建立在标准 on-policy distillation（OPD）框架上。学生模型沿自身轨迹生成 token 序列，形成 rollout 前缀；教师模型在特定状态（informative states）处对学生前缀进行续写（continuation），从而补充教师自身的生成路径。方法的核心是自适应选择教师续写的有效监督视界（horizon），即决定从哪个 token 位置开始以及持续多长。该设计基于对教师分支梯度的方差-偏差分解：随着续写长度增加，教师预测不确定性下降（TUC 现象），监督梯度方差减小；但同时师生路径分歧增大，引入偏差。因此存在一个有限的最优续写长度。AC-OPD 在训练时动态估计并选取该最优视界，在信息量高的状态处施加教师续写监督。训练目标仍为 token 级蒸馏损失，但仅在自适应选择的续写段上计算，以平衡方差与偏差。推理路径与学生模型常规生成一致，不增加额外推理开销。论文提及在数学推理和代码生成任务上跨模型规模验证，并通过控制续写与匹配预算分析验证设计有效性，但未披露具体模型规模、数据量、硬件或延迟等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "math reasoning & code generation 性能提升", "value": "持续优于 standard OPD", "setting": "多个模型规模，数学推理与代码生成任务；具体数值未在摘要中给出"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并形式化 Teacher Uncertainty Contraction（TUC）现象：教师从学生前缀继续生成时预测不确定性系统性降低。；通过教师分支梯度的方差-偏差分解，理论刻画续写长度对监督方差与师生路径分歧偏差的权衡，证明存在有限最优续写长度。；提出 AC-OPD，在学生 rollout 的信息状态处附加教师续写并自适应选择有效监督视界。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach for transferring knowledge between language models, where a student receives dense token-level supervision along its own generated trajectories. However, teacher supervision can be unreliable when conditioned on incomplete or low-quality student prefixes. We identify Teacher Uncertainty Contraction (TUC), a systematic phenomenon whereby the teacher's predictive uncertainty decreases as it continues from a student-generated prefix. We theoretically characterize this trade-off through a variance-bias decomposition of teacher-branch gradients, showing that uncertainty contraction reduces variance while teacher-student path divergence increases bias, thereby favoring a finite continuation. Guided by this insight, we propose Adaptive-Continuations On-Policy Distillation (AC-OPD), which augments informative states along student rollouts with teacher continuations and adaptively selects their effective supervision horizons. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation across model scales demonstrate that AC-OPD consistently improves over standard OPD. Controlled-continuations and matched-budget analyses further validate the adaptive-continuations design, highlighting adaptive teacher continuations as an effective principle for reliable on-policy distillation.The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [117] [CAMFT: Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22253)
*Zhou, Jingang,Guo, Haiyang,Ma, Yuan,Zhu, Han,Zhang, Xu-Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMFT 提出一种冲突感知的可合并微调方法，通过在微调阶段引导任务仅更新低冲突稀疏坐标，使多个任务模型更易合并为单一 LLM，实验表明其优于标准微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法主要对独立微调后的模型进行事后处理，忽略了训练阶段对跨任务兼容性的影响；事后解决参数冲突是次优的。

Method: CAMFT 在标准微调流程中引入冲突感知机制：训练每个任务时，不直接进行稠密参数更新，而是根据跨任务参数冲突度量筛选出冲突较低的稀疏坐标，仅在这些坐标上进行更新。这样得到的任务特定更新既稀疏（训练高效），又与其他任务的更新在参数空间上重叠少，从而在下游模型合并（如权重平均或任务算术）时减少参数冲突。具体冲突度量方式、稀疏选择策略、可合并性目标函数以及合并算法在摘要中未披露，无法进一步推断。整体流程为：多任务分别微调 → 冲突感知稀疏更新 → 合并为单一模型，目标是在多任务合并场景下优于标准微调基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将可合并性视为微调过程中塑造的属性，而非仅在微调后解决的问题。；提出冲突感知的可合并微调（CAMFT），引导每个任务仅更新跨任务冲突较低的稀疏坐标。；产生训练高效且对下游模型合并更兼容的任务更新。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a promising paradigm for integrating multiple task-specific capabilities into a single large language model. However, existing methods predominantly focus on post-hoc processing of independently fine-tuned models, overlooking how the training phase itself impacts cross-task compatibility. Resolving parameter conflicts after fine-tuning is inherently sub-optimal. To address this, we propose CAMFT, a Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning method that makes task adaptation both efficient and mergeaware. CAMFT treats mergeability as a property shaped during fine-tuning, rather than only a problem to be solved after fine-tuning. By guiding each task to update sparse coordinates with lower cross-task conflict, CAMFT produces task updates that are efficient to train and more compatible for downstream model merging. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAMFT outperforms standard finetuning baselines in multi-task merging scenarios. Codes are available at https://github.com/gyanchow/CAMFT-LLM.

</details>


### [118] [CHART: A Harness-Rotation Curriculum for Harness-Robust Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22247)
*Zhang, Xinlu,Lin, Ying-Chun,Zhang, Zhihan,Fetahu, Besnik,Chen, Xi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHART 针对搜索智能体在 harness 更新后行为失效的问题，提出轮换课程式 harness 训练：周期性毕业已学会并行搜索的 harness 并替换为仍可学习的 harness，使 GRPO 的并行-串行奖励差保持活跃。相比静态 harness 增强（最多学会一半 harness），CHART 学会全部 harness，在 held-out harness 上 89% 回合并行化（静态池最多 5%），迁移到新 QA 任务与新搜索环境 pass@1 提升 5.6pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定 harness 下训练得到的智能体行为与训练 harness 表面形式纠缠，harness 更新后行为失效（回退到串行搜索）；简单增加 harness 数量无法解决，因为 GRPO 的并行-串行奖励差在 harness 池小的时候过早饱和、池大时又被稀释。

Method: 方法以搜索智能体为对象，训练目标是让其在多种 harness 下稳定执行“并行搜索”策略。输入为问题以及由 harness（system prompt 等表面形式）包装的交互上下文；模型在该 harness 下生成搜索动作轨迹（并行或串行）。训练采用 GRPO，对同一问题分别采样并行与串行 rollout，用两者奖励差作为学习信号，使并行行为获得正优势。静态 harness 增强的缺陷在于：小 harness 池会较早饱和奖励差，大池则把每个 harness 的信号稀释，导致没有 harness 能巩固该行为。CHART 引入周期性评估：在每个评估点判断哪些 harness 上期望行为已学会，将其“毕业”移出当前训练池，并替换为仍可学习的 harness，使训练池始终维持可产生有效奖励差的样本，从而在整个训练过程中持续提供对比信号。推理/部署时，模型面对新 harness（包括未见过的 harness）应能泛化触发并行搜索；实验在 held-out harness 上测得 89% 的回合并行化，并进一步迁移到新 QA 任务与搜索环境，同时与 meta-harness search 结合时收益更大。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在 harness 池上学会并行搜索的比例", "value": "静态增强最多成功一半 harness；CHART 在所有 harness 上学会", "setting": "从同一 harness 池出发，训练搜索智能体执行并行搜索"}, {"metric": "held-out harness 上并行化回合占比", "value": "CHART 89%，静态池最多 5%", "setting": "未见过的 held-out harness 评估"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "+5.6pp（相对最佳静态池）", "setting": "迁移到新 QA 任务与搜索环境"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 CHART（Curriculum HArness Rotation Training）：轮换课程式 harness 训练，周期性评估并“毕业”已掌握目标行为的 harness，替换为仍可学习的 harness，使奖励差在训练全程保持活跃；揭示并刻画“harness-local”行为：固定 harness 训练导致并行搜索行为过拟合于 harness 表面形式；证明简单 harness 增强（静态多 harness 池）无法解决该问题，并给出奖励差饱和/稀释的机制解释；验证行为可迁移到 held-out harness、新 QA 任务与新搜索环境，且 CHART 训练的智能体在 meta-harness search 下受益更大

Abstract: Search agents are usually trained under a single harness. But once an agent is deployed in a real application, its harness is frequently updated (e.g., a rewritten system prompt) to fit production needs. This exposes a fragility of post-trained agents: because a learned behavior is entangled with its training harness, even a harness update that leaves the task unchanged can fail to elicit the behavior. We train a search agent to perform parallel search, a popular strategy for improving both search efficiency and performance. We find that training under a fixed harness makes the behavior harness-local, overfit to that harness's surface form: when the harness changes, the model falls back to serial search. An intuitive fix is harness augmentation, but simply training on more harnesses does not resolve the problem. GRPO learns from the reward gap between parallel and serial rollouts of the same question: a small harness pool saturates that gap early, while a large pool dilutes the per-harness signal too thinly for any harness to consolidate. We therefore propose Curriculum HArness Rotation Training (CHART), a rotating curriculum that lets a search agent gradually consolidate parallel search across harnesses. At each periodic evaluation, CHART "graduates" the harnesses whose expected behavior is learned and replaces them with still-learnable ones, keeping the reward gap alive throughout training. Starting from the same harness pool, CHART makes the model learn parallel search on all harnesses, whereas static augmentation succeeds on at most half of them. The behavior also carries to held-out harnesses: CHART parallelizes on 89% of held-out turns, against at most 5% for the static pools. It further transfers to a new QA task and search environment, improving pass@1 by 5.6pp over the best static pool. Finally, CHART-trained agents benefit more from meta-harness search than baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Not All Ranks Are Equal: Budget-Aware LoRA Merging Across Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22237)
*Amballa, Avinash,Saidutta, Yashas Malur,Li, Wenbo,Valkov, Lazar,Chappidi, Srinivas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出LoRA合并中均匀秩分配的局限性，提出Net Utility无数据度量，通过SVD分解和全局选择最优奇异方向实现非均匀秩分配。在视觉和语言任务上平均提升2.1%和2.2%，适用于多种合并方法和合并空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA合并方法假设每一层需要相同的秩预算，且任务间均分秩预算。这种均匀预算假设是合并后与每任务LoRA性能差距的主要来源，但秩选择是NP难问题。

Method: 该方法首先对每个任务的LoRA进行奇异值分解（SVD），得到奇异方向和奇异值。然后，对于每个奇异方向，计算其任务效用（该方向对任务性能的贡献）和与其他任务方向的干扰（与其他任务方向的冲突程度）。接着，将这些分数全局池化，在总方向数约束下选择净效用最高的方向。最后，选中的方向用于合并，可应用于五种不同的合并方法和三种合并空间。合并后的模型用于多任务推理，无需任务特定的权重交换。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "+2.1%", "setting": "视觉任务，平均超过无秩分配 counterparts"}, {"metric": "性能提升", "value": "+2.2%", "setting": "语言任务，平均超过无秩分配 counterparts"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Net Utility：一种无数据度量，通过对每个任务LoRA进行SVD分解，为每个奇异方向打上任务效用和与其他任务方向的干扰分数。；全局池化这些分数，在总方向数约束下选择净效用最高的奇异方向，实现非均匀的秩分配。；将Net Utility应用于五种合并方法和三种合并空间，在视觉和语言任务上验证。

Abstract: Merging low-rank adapters (LoRAs) promises to eliminate the overhead of swapping task-specific weights at inference time. However, existing merging methods assume every layer needs the same rank budget. Further, some methods assume that rank budget needs to be split equally among the tasks too. We show this uniform-budget assumption is a major source of the performance gap between merged and per-task LoRAs. However, rank selection is an NP hard problem. To this end, we introduce Net Utility, a data free metric that first decomposes every task LoRA by its Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) and scores each of those singular directions by its task utility and its interference with other tasks directions. Next, we globally pool these scores to select singular directions with the highest values with a constraint on the total number of directions selected. The proposed Net Utility metric is applied on top of five different merging methods across three different merging spaces. The merging is done over two sets of tasks, vision and language tasks. Net utility based rank allocation outperforms its counterparts without that allocation. On average, over vision tasks it achieves +2.1% improvement in performance, and +2.2% improvement over the language tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Toollery: Scaling LLM Agents to Thousands of Skills and Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22218)
*Tian, Xiangxi,Guan, Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Toollery 提出无训练的候选压缩框架，通过文档侧查询扩展构建检索索引，将大规模技能/工具库压缩为紧凑 top-k 候选集后再由 LLM 决策。在 SkillRouter、BFCL-V4 和智能座舱数据集上改善召回，并在固定 top-10 预算下提升端到端选择或保持 comparable AST Accuracy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 LLM 智能体面对数百到数万技能、工具与 API 时，全库提示成本高、速度慢且选择可靠性下降。

Method: Toollery 是无训练候选压缩框架。输入为技能/工具规格与真实用户请求。流程：1）文档侧查询扩展：对每个技能/工具规格生成用户意图查询；2）基于这些生成查询构建检索索引；3）真实用户请求到达时，检索索引将其映射到紧凑的 top-k 候选集；4）由 LLM 在压缩后的候选集上做最终选择决策。该方法将高层技能与原子工具统一视为可选能力，因此可同时用于技能库与工具注册表。评估覆盖约 79K 能力的 SkillRouter 基准、BFCL-V4 的 440+ 原子工具，以及 3,396 条专有 smart-cockpit 请求与 220+ 工具。目标是通过前置检索压缩候选规模，降低 prompt token 和延迟，并减少长候选列表带来的干扰，同时保持或提升选择质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall", "value": "优于普通规格检索，具体数值未在摘要中给出", "setting": "SkillRouter、BFCL-V4、cockpit 数据集上的检索召回"}, {"metric": "end-to-end selection", "value": "在固定 top-10 预算下提升，具体数值未给出", "setting": "cockpit 数据集"}, {"metric": "AST Accuracy", "value": "可比，具体数值未给出", "setting": "BFCL-V4，top-10 预算"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "end-to-end selection", "value": "固定 top-10 预算下提升，具体数值未在摘要中给出", "setting": "3,396 条专有 smart-cockpit 请求、220+ 工具"}]}

Conclusion: 无训练候选压缩框架 Toollery；从技能/工具规格生成用户意图查询的文档侧查询扩展；构建将真实用户请求映射到紧凑候选集的检索索引；统一处理高层技能与原子工具两类可选能力

Abstract: As LLM agents are exposed to hundreds to tens of thousands of skills, tools, and API functions, full-library prompting becomes costly, slow, and less reliable: each added candidate increases prompt tokens and latency, while longer candidate lists introduce more distractors for LLM selection. We present \textbf{Toollery}, a training-free candidate-compression framework for scalable LLM skill/tool selection. Following established document-side query expansion, Toollery generates user-intent queries from each skill/tool specification and builds a retrieval index that maps real user requests to compact candidate sets before final LLM decision-making. By treating high-level skills and atomic tools as selectable capabilities, Toollery can be applied to both skill libraries and tool registries. We evaluate Toollery on the roughly 79K-capability SkillRouter benchmark, BFCL-V4 with over 440 atomic tools, and 3,396 proprietary smart-cockpit requests over 220 tools. Across these settings, Toollery keeps online selection bounded to a compact top-$k$ candidate set and improves recall over ordinary specification retrieval. At a fixed top-10 budget, Toollery improves end-to-end selection on the cockpit dataset, and maintains comparable AST Accuracy on BFCL-V4. These results support Toollery as a practical candidate-compression framework for large and evolving agent capability libraries, while showing that quality and cost gains depend on workload coverage and provider caching.

</details>


### [121] [Improving Parameter Utilization by Sharing Neural Experts Across Layers in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22199)
*Jiao, Dian,Duan, Jiaxin,Zhao, Shuai,Leng, Jiabing,Zhang, Yiran,Huang, Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CS-MoE通过让各Transformer层共享一个全局专家池，减少层间参数冗余，实现token级弹性计算，在仅激活55%参数时获得比同规模dense模型更低的困惑度，并在固定FLOPs下接近更耗算力的MoE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大语言模型存在层间参数冗余，不同深度重复学习相似的功能变换，导致参数利用效率低。

Method: CS-MoE是一种Transformer架构，在每个Transformer块中同时包含层独立专家和一个全局共享专家池。对于每个输入token，模型通过门控机制决定激活哪些专家（包括层独立专家和来自共享池的专家），从而控制token级的参数激活量和计算量（FLOPs）。共享专家池是跨所有层并发访问的，因此不同层可以复用同一组专家参数，减少层间冗余。训练目标为标准语言建模损失（困惑度）。推理时模型可根据计算预算弹性调整激活专家数量，实现计算成本与模型容量之间的Pareto前沿。具体门控实现、专家数量等细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "perplexity", "value": "低于同等规模dense Transformer", "setting": "激活参数仅55%"}, {"metric": "FLOPs效率", "value": "在固定FLOPs预算下性能接近消耗更多FLOPs的MoE变体", "setting": "扩展共享专家池"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出CS-MoE架构，在每个Transformer层中并行使用层独立专家和全局共享专家池。；全局专家共享机制允许token级弹性控制参数激活与计算消耗（FLOPs）。；在固定FLOPs预算下，通过扩大共享专家池提升性能，并建立计算成本与模型容量之间的Pareto前沿。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models often suffer from inter-layer parameter redundancy, where functional transformations are redundantly learned across network depths. We propose CS-MoE, a novel Transformer architecture featuring cross-layer expert sharing to address this inefficiency. Deviating from the widely used Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture that terminates each Transformer block with layer-isolated experts, CS-MoE combines layer-independent experts with concurrent access to a centralized, globally shared expert pool. This \textit{Global Experts Sharing} mechanism enables elastic control over token-level parameter activation and computational consumption (FLOPs). Experiments demonstrate that CS-MoE achieves lower perplexity than equal-scale dense Transformers while activating only 55\% of parameters. Furthermore, its performance scales monotonically with an increased number of activated experts and approaches MoE counterparts that consume more FLOPs by expanding the shared pool with a fixed FLOPs budget. CS-MoE also establishes a flexible Pareto frontier between computational cost and model capacity, offering an efficient alternative for computation-constrained environments.

</details>


### [122] [EvoRank: LLM-Guided Evolution of Multi-Objective Learning-to-Rank Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22196)
*Patel, Rayhan,Patel, Shabaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoRank提出LLM引导的进化循环自动构建多目标学习排序管线，在Expedia数据集上超越Optuna调优的LambdaMART，并发现头部门控可预测搜索有效性，附带审计工具与失败模式目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何自动发现完整的学习排序管线（特征、模型、损失、集成）以在多目标电商搜索中平衡相关性、转化和收入，并预测进化搜索是否值得投入计算资源（头部门控）。

Method: EvoRank是一个LLM引导的进化循环，用于自动发现完整的学习排序管线。输入为电商搜索查询-文档对及多目标（相关性、转化、收入）的标注数据。系统通过进化算法在搜索空间（特征、模型、损失、集成）中迭代，LLM用于指导搜索（具体机制未在摘要中详述）。每个候选管线在验证集（选择折）上评估，选择得分高的作为下一代。最终管线在留出集上评估。论文还引入了转移审计：在留出数据上重新评估选择折上的胜出者，发现收益可能是适应度噪声。头部门控用于提前预测搜索空间相对于适应度噪声是否足够大，从而决定是否投入LLM成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在Expedia ICDM 2013数据集上的排序性能", "value": "三个独立运行在60k留出查询上均优于Optuna调优的LambdaMART，优势在全数据规模下保持，位于原始竞赛前6%", "setting": "Expedia ICDM 2013数据集，多目标（相关性、转化、收入），60k留出查询"}, {"metric": "每个运行的LLM成本", "value": "约10美元", "setting": "每个运行在50次迭代内收敛"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LLM引导的进化循环自动搜索完整LTR管线（特征、模型、损失、集成）；发现搜索空间头部门控（headroom gate）：在花费LLM成本前预测进化循环是否有效；转移审计工具与失败模式目录，识别选择折上的收益可能是适应度噪声

Abstract: We present EvoRank, an open autonomous ranking engineer: an LLM-guided evolutionary loop that discovers complete Learning-to-Rank pipelines (features, models, losses, ensembles) for multi-objective e-commerce search. On the Expedia ICDM 2013 dataset, with relevance, conversion, and revenue as competing objectives, three independent runs each converge within 50 iterations (about ten dollars) on interpretable pipelines that beat an Optuna-tuned LambdaMART on 60k held-out queries, an advantage that persists at full data scale and places in the top 6 percent of the original competition. A first campaign, evolving only training objectives, builds the central design rule: it appeared to work on its selection fold (the small dataset it uses to pick winners) while a transfer audit, re-scoring winners on held-out data, showed the gains were almost entirely fitness noise (the randomness of its own scoring), and neither seeded domain knowledge nor richer diagnostic feedback changed what transferred. The deciding quantity is measurable in advance: search-space headroom relative to fitness noise. We package this as a headroom gate that predicts, before any LLM spend, whether the loop will pay off, and we release the system, the auditing tools, and a catalog of failure modes with their guardrails, so teams can apply the procedure to their own ranking stacks.

</details>


### [123] [A Pinch of SFT, A Dash of RL: When Reinforcement Learning Helps Long-Horizon Advertising Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22194)
*Kolekar, Aakash,Genc, Sahika,Sisman, Bunyamin,Shariat, Shahriar,Kachroo, Shree Vandana,Saha, Avishek,Wu, Qianli,Singer, Ari,Dumoulin, Benoit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向广告分析智能体的SFT与RL混合训练研究，提出基于教师支持和奖励余量的前瞻性路由策略，在GPT-OSS 120B上实现7/8技能提升，最大增益+11.27点，泄漏率大幅降低，并节省43%的RL计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在长程广告分析智能体中合理分配SFT与RL，避免RL对已校准技能的扰动，同时最大化奖励可观测行为的学习效率。

Method: 系统处理企业广告分析的长程工具调用任务，涉及检索、推理、API调用和代码执行。训练分为SFT和RL两个阶段：SFT用于校准工具语法和教师支持行为，RL用于探索奖励可观测行为。论文提出基于检查点轨迹的诊断方法，将训练过程划分为模仿、提升、发现三种机制，并利用教师支持度与奖励可观测余量将特征路由到仅SFT、SFT后RL、增加RL分配或进一步环境开发。推理时智能体按业务需求调用工具链，并通过定向RL减少泄漏，同时保持可操作性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "非披露技能提升", "value": "+11.27 points (95% CI [+9.72, +12.82])", "setting": "GPT-OSS 120B，定向SFT+RL vs 前沿Control，在线广告主技能"}, {"metric": "七技能平均提升", "value": "+3.57 vs +1.62", "setting": "匹配的均匀训练 vs 定向RL对比，共享奖励与优化"}, {"metric": "标准泄漏率", "value": "11.8% → 2.9%", "setting": "定向RL vs SFT，SME审计"}, {"metric": "对抗泄漏率", "value": "22.9% → 6.8%", "setting": "定向RL vs SFT，SME审计"}, {"metric": "可操作性", "value": "86.2% → 85.7%", "setting": "定向RL vs SFT，SME审计"}]}

Conclusion: 发现并利用检查点轨迹将训练划分为模仿、提升、发现三种机制，并基于此进行前瞻性路由。；提出基于教师支持度和奖励可观测余量的特征路由策略：仅SFT、SFT后RL、增加RL分配、或进一步环境开发。；诊断方法在后续18个特征实验中的15个成功预测轨迹。；在GPT-OSS 120B上，定向SFT+RL相比前沿Control在7/8广告主技能上产生正向点估计，5个增益的95%置信区间排除零。；定向RL相比SFT显著降低标准泄漏（11.8%→2.9%）和对抗泄漏（22.9%→6.8%），同时保持可操作性（86.2%→85.7%）。；匹配比较中，定向RL将七技能平均提升从+1.62提升至+3.57，同时减少43%的增量RL计算。

Abstract: Enterprise analytics agents solve long-horizon tool-use problems over distributed business data, requiring retrieval, reasoning, API calls, code execution, and adaptation to intermediate observations. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) calibrates tool syntax and teacher-supported behavior, whereas reinforcement learning (RL) can explore reward-supported behaviors beyond demonstrations; applied uniformly, however, RL can perturb already-calibrated skills. We study how to balance SFT and RL under production-mirroring beta APIs. We observe that, in our controlled experiment, checkpoint trajectories retrospectively separated into three regimes: Imitation, where SFT captured reliable teacher behavior; Lift, where both stages helped; and Discovery, where useful reward-observable behavior lay outside reliable teacher support. We leverage this prospectively, using teacher support and reward-observable headroom to route features to SFT only, SFT then RL, increased RL allocation, or further environment development. Across 18 subsequent feature-specific experiments, the diagnostic predicted 15/18 observed trajectories. On GPT-OSS 120B, targeted SFT then RL produced positive point estimates on 7/8 advertiser skills relative to a frontier Control; five positive gains had paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero, while one skill had a confidence-supported regression. The largest gain was non-disclosure (+11.27 points; 95% CI [+9.72, +12.82]). A separate SME audit surfaced that targeted RL reduces standard leakage from 11.8% to 2.9% and adversarial leakage from 22.9% to 6.8% relative to SFT while preserving actionability (86.2% to 85.7%). In a matched uniform-versus-targeted comparison with shared rewards and optimization, targeted RL improved the seven-skill mean delta from +1.62 to +3.57 while using 43% less incremental RL compute.

</details>


### [124] [Multiple latent orderings better predict language model preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22170)
*Chawla, Aviral,Thompson, William H. W.,Young, Jean-Gabriel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出混合Bradley-Terry模型，将LLM偏好不一致性解释为多个潜在一致排序的聚合，实验表明混合模型优于单效用模型，揭示了偏好的多元性，对推荐系统偏好建模有借鉴意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何更准确地建模语言模型观察到的偏好不一致性（如非传递性），而非简单地将其视为噪声。

Method: 输入：重复的成对比较数据（用户或模型对项目对选择的偏好）。处理流程：1）通过统计检验证明单一潜在排序无法解释观察到的非传递性偏好；2）构建噪声增强的混合Bradley-Terry模型（MBT），假设每个观察到的偏好来源于K个潜在排序中的一个，每个潜在排序对应一个Bradley-Terry效用函数，并加入噪声项；3）使用期望最大化或变分推断方法从重复成对比较中推断混合组件参数（每个组件的效用权重和混合比例）；4）输出多个潜在排序组件，用于分析模型偏好的异质性和一致性。训练目标：最大化观察到的成对比较数据的似然。推理：给定新的成对比较，可以计算每个组件的后验概率或预测偏好。该架构旨在解耦观察偏好中混合的多个一致排序，从而更好地预测语言模型偏好。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解释结构性不一致的能力（如拟合优度）", "value": "混合模型优于单效用模型", "setting": "在7个语言模型和4个任务上的重复成对比较数据"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次证明观察到的偏好不一致无法由单一潜在排序在任意单调链接函数下解释，需要用多个潜在排序的混合来解释。；提出了噪声增强的混合Bradley-Terry模型（MBT），从重复成对比较中推断多个潜在偏好组件。；在7个模型和4个任务上验证了混合排序模型往往比单效用模型更好地解释结构性不一致，并揭示了聚合偏好掩盖偏好异质性。；通过Moral Machine案例研究表明，在聚合排序上不一致的模型可以共享潜在偏好组件，说明LLM反映多元偏好。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed in settings where they are asked to make value judgments and choices. These observed choices often exhibit intransitivity: A model may prefer item $A$ to $B$ and $B$ to $C$, while also preferring $C$ to $A$. Existing work that models LLM preferences treats such inconsistencies as sampling noise around a single latent ordering. We instead propose that intransitivity reflects the aggregation of multiple latent, internally consistent orderings. We first show that observed inconsistencies cannot be explained by a single ordering under any monotone link function. We then introduce a noise-augmented mixture Bradley-Terry (MBT) model that infers latent preference components from repeated pairwise comparisons. Across seven models and four tasks, a mixture of orderings often explains structural inconsistencies better than single-utility models. We find that aggregate preferences often hide underlying preference heterogeneity. A case study on Moral Machine dilemmas shows that models which disagree on aggregate orderings can still share latent components. Together, these results suggest that LLMs reflect plural preferences. Alignment and evaluation pipelines that treat LLM preferences as a single function, therefore, risk averaging over coherent orderings that different users may endorse differently.

</details>


### [125] [Global Ranks Survive, Selected Heads Shift: BOS-Sink Topology under 4-bit Weight-Only Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23585)
*Chen, Kuanlin,Kuo, Chen-Wei,Ou, Cheng-En*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文量化分析了4-bit权重量化对BOS-sink注意力头拓扑的影响，提出STC指标，在多个小模型上发现全局排名高保持但局部头和跨域迁移不稳定，量化和部署需重新验证，并在Jetson Orin NX上验证了边缘可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在4-bit NF4权重量化后，预量化阶段发现的BOS-sink重要注意力头映射（用于边缘部署的稀疏化/优化）是否仍然可靠，即全局排名、top-k集合和层间sink质量分布在量化前后是否保持一致。

Method: 论文针对4-bit NF4 weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ)场景，研究BOS-sink注意力头拓扑在量化前后的变化。输入为文本序列（长度4096 tokens），通过Transformer模型（Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B）产生注意力权重。核心模块是Sink Topology Consistency (STC)度量：全局排名保持性（Spearman ρ_s）衡量bf16与4-bit模型之间头重要性排序的整体一致性；top-k Jaccard重叠和成员保留率衡量离散重要头集合的稳定性；层间sink质量偏移分析则检测局部层的变化。实验流程为：先在bf16模型上通过校准数据（C4或LongBench）识别sink头并生成映射，然后对模型进行4-bit NF4权重量化，再在量化模型上重新测量注意力头统计量，比较两者的一致性。训练目标为最小化量化误差（NF4格式权重仅量化，无梯度更新）。推理时在Jetson Orin NX上运行量化模型，评估设备端测量可行性。关键发现包括：全局排名高保持但top-k集合重叠仅0.619-0.793；终端Qwen层漂移达模型均值的6.2-7.9倍；跨域校准迁移比同域退化更严重；选定层更新不足以达到全图稳定性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "全局排名相关性 ρ_s", "value": "≥0.980 at 4,096 tokens", "setting": "bf16到4-bit PTQ，Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B"}, {"metric": "top-k Jaccard重叠", "value": "0.619-0.793", "setting": "4-bit PTQ，bf16与4-bit所有权重对比，3个模型"}, {"metric": "成员保留率", "value": "76.5%-88.5%", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "终端Qwen层漂移", "value": "模型均值的6.2-7.9倍", "setting": "层间sink质量偏移分析"}, {"metric": "4-bit重校准达到90%稳定性平台所需最小样本数", "value": "n=8（Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B），n=32（Llama-3.2-1B）", "setting": "匹配域校准，非阈值效应"}, {"metric": "跨域重叠退化", "value": "C4到LongBench的跨域重叠比同域精度对比退化更多（Qwen模型），Llama-3.2-1B无此现象", "setting": "校准集从C4迁移到LongBench"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Sink Topology Consistency (STC)指标，分离全局排名保持、top-k集合重叠和层间sink质量漂移三个维度。；区分逐输入敏感性与校准映射迁移，揭示全局排名高保持性下的局部失效。；发现跨域（C4到LongBench）校准迁移比同域精度对比退化更严重，且不同模型族表现不同（Qwen终端层漂移显著，Llama漂移均匀）。；在Jetson Orin NX上验证了16样本工作负载的实时性，给出边缘部署可行性数据。

Abstract: Sink-aware deployment may identify important first-token attention heads before a model is quantized, then reuse that map at the edge. We test when this shortcut is safe for 4-bit NF4 weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ). Our Sink Topology Consistency (STC) metrics separate global rank preservation, top-$k$ set overlap, and layerwise sink-mass shift, and distinguish per-input sensitivity from calibration-map transfer. Across Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama-3.2-1B, global bf16-to-4-bit ranks remain high at 4,096 tokens ($\rho_s \geq 0.980$), yet top-$k$ Jaccard overlap is only 0.619-0.793, corresponding to 76.5-88.5% membership retention. The global statistic also masks local failures: terminal Qwen layers shift by 6.2-7.9x their model means, whereas Llama-3.2-1B shows low, nearly uniform drift. Under a C4-to-LongBench shift, cross-domain overlap degrades more than the within-domain precision comparison for both Qwen models, but not for Llama-3.2-1B. Matched-domain 4-bit recalibration reaches 90% of a split-half stability plateau at the smallest tested $n=8$ for both Qwen models and $n=32$ for Llama-3.2-1B, though not as a sharp threshold; for the two Qwen models, updating only selected layers does not reach the full-map stability criterion. On Jetson Orin NX, the 16-sample workload takes seconds for the two models with valid on-device sink measurements. The practical message is precise: global rankings often transfer, but discrete head sets, layer-local policies, and cross-domain calibration should be revalidated after quantization.

</details>


### [126] [Efficient Mixture-of-Experts with Speculative Decoding via Expert Coactivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22471)
*Nishu, Kumari,Kim, Han-Byul,Chilkunda, Santosh,Horton, Maxwell,Kundu, Arnav,Samragh, Mohammad,Hannah, Lauren,Sekhavat, Mohammad,Bhendawade, Nikhil,Ciosici, Manuel,Mirzadeh, Iman,Vahid, Keivan Alizadeh,Harrison, David,Belousova, Irina,Farajtabar, Mehrdad,Cho, Minsik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究MoE与投机解码结合时路由器设计对专家共激活和推理速度的影响，提出增强共激活的训练方案，实现21%吞吐量提升且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型与投机解码结合时，验证token数量增加导致更多专家从DRAM传输到NPU，内存传输成本成为瓶颈，限制了推理加速效果。

Method: 论文针对MoE模型与投机解码(SD)结合的推理流程：SD并行验证一组token，MoE路由为每个token选择专家。作者分析路由器设计对专家共激活的影响，发现高共激活度可减少DRAM到NPU的专家传输量，从而缓解验证token增多带来的内存瓶颈。提出的训练方案结合全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失以及自回归专家选择机制，以增强专家共激活。推理时，增强的共激活使得更多token可共享已加载的专家，提高吞吐量。具体模型结构、训练目标和推理流程的详细描述未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "吞吐量", "value": "21%", "setting": "与MoE基线相比，保持相当准确率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现路由器的高专家共激活程度可显著降低MoE+SD的运行时开销；系统评估多种路由器设计选择对专家共激活和运行时的影响；提出结合全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失和自回归专家选择机制的训练方案以增强专家共激活

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly deployed alongside Speculative Decoding (SD) to accelerate inference, but combining the two is challenging. SD improves the inference speed of dense models by verifying groups of tokens in parallel. However, the inference speedup for SD with MoEs depends heavily on the number of tokens being verified. Using more verification tokens results in more experts being transferred from DRAM to the Neural Processing Unit (NPU), which increases the memory transfer cost. This negatively impacts model runtime, as memory transfer is typically the bottleneck in inference. In this work, we investigate the impact of MoE router design during training on the speed of MoEs with SD. We find that routers with high degrees of expert coactivation result in much faster runtimes, mitigating the impact of using more verification tokens. Motivated by this observation, we assess the impact of various router design choices on expert coactivation and runtime using billion-parameter transformer models. We find that combining a global load-balancing loss, shared experts, a consistency loss, and an autoregressive expert selection mechanism during training results in significantly stronger expert coactivation. This increased coactivation translates into higher overall runtime throughput: our exploration yields a model that improves throughput by 21% over MoE baselines, while maintaining on-par accuracy with the baseline MoE.

</details>


### [127] [PAGE: Partition-Aware Gated KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22157)
*Kumar, Pankaj,Mishra, Subhankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAGE 提出一种无需训练的 KV-Cache 淘汰门控机制，利用 prefill 注意力中早期与晚期层注意力头一致性下降幅度预测是否应执行淘汰，从而在长上下文任务中避免淘汰导致的准确率崩溃，同时保留淘汰带来的压缩收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SnapKV 类 KV-Cache 淘汰方法在容量受限输入上可能将准确率降至 0，而部分输入保留完整缓存反而更好；需要判断每个输入是否应该执行淘汰，而非仅决定保留哪些 token。

Method: PAGE 是一个无需训练的推理包装器。输入为长上下文输入，首先进行 prefill 前向传播。在 prefill 过程中，计算一个无标签指标：观察早期层与晚期层之间，注意力头在“读取哪些 token”上的一致程度下降幅度（drop）。该指标在解码开始前即可获得。若下降幅度大，则调用任意 SnapKV 风格的 KV-Cache 淘汰器进行缓存压缩；若下降幅度小，则保留完整 KV-Cache 以避免淘汰带来的风险。推理时按原模型解码流程进行，因此 PAGE 可视为一个门控决策模块，不改变模型参数或训练流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "容量受限输入上的 harm rate", "value": "0.75 降至 0.026", "setting": "未知基准与模型配置，论文未在摘要中披露"}, {"metric": "多键检索准确率（Mistral-7B plain SnapKV 基线随预算缩小）", "value": "99% 降至 0%", "setting": "Mistral-7B，多键检索任务，plain SnapKV"}, {"metric": "多键检索准确率（PAGE）", "value": "保持 89%", "setting": "Mistral-7B，多键检索任务，预算缩小"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现一个无需标签的指标：prefill 注意力中早期层与晚期层之间注意力头对读取 token 的一致性下降幅度，可在解码前预测该输入属于哪一类。；提出 PAGE（Partition-Aware Gated Eviction）包装器：当下降幅度大时运行任意 SnapKV 风格淘汰器，当下降幅度小时保留完整缓存。；无需训练、标签或微调，可即插即用，且在其他场景下保持基础淘汰器行为不变（16 格中 8 格不变）。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction can do more than compress. In long-context LLMs, keeping only some cached tokens sometimes matches or exceeds full-cache accuracy, because many redundant prefill tokens otherwise dilute attention away from the tokens that carry the answer. This benefit is not uniform, and evicting the wrong tokens can drop accuracy to zero on tasks that require precise retrieval, so the useful question is not only which tokens to keep but also whether to evict this input at all. We show that one label-free number computed from the prefill attention, the drop between early and late layers in how much attention heads agree on which tokens to read, predicts per input, before any decoding, which of the two cases an input falls under. We build this into PAGE (Partition-Aware Gated Eviction), a wrapper that runs any SnapKV-style evictor when the drop is large and keeps the full cache when it is small, with no training, labels, or fine-tuning. PAGE is a safety mechanism rather than a compressor, so we measure it by the failures it prevents. It cuts the harm rate on capacity-bound inputs from 0.75 to 0.026, and on multi-key retrieval with Mistral-7B plain SnapKV falls from 99\% to 0\% as the budget shrinks, while PAGE holds it at 89\%. Elsewhere, it passes the base evictor through unchanged, which is the intended behaviour and is what we observe in 8 of 16 cells. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PAGE-018239.

</details>


### [128] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Qi, Jianing,Tang, Hao,Zhu, Zhigang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM后训练（SFT和RL）在预训练权重的SVD框架下的几何分解，将权重更新分为对角、非对角和零空间三个成分。在数学评估套件上，移除对角成分通常保留大部分后训练增益，表明后训练增益主要来自重新配置和扩展预训练通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练（SFT和RL）如何改变LLM权重，以及这些改变中哪些几何成分携带了主要性能增益。

Method: 论文提出一种分析框架，在预训练权重矩阵的奇异值分解（SVD）框架下表达后训练导致的权重更新。具体地，对于每个预训练矩阵，其SVD为UΣV^T，后训练后的权重变化ΔW可投影到三个几何上正交的子空间：1）对角分量，对应于在原始奇异值方向上的缩放（即重新缩放奇异值）；2）非对角分量，对应于在原始奇异向量所张成的子空间内但非对角线上的旋转，改变了预训练输入和输出方向的耦合方式；3）零空间分量，对应于原矩阵零空间（或补空间）中的方向，即在原始非零SVD核心之外引入新的路由路径。作者对12条后训练链（包括SFT和RL）计算了这些分量，并通过在评估时移除特定分量（如将对角分量置零）来测量性能变化。在数学评估套件上的结果表明，移除对角分量通常能保留大部分后训练增益，这表明后训练效果主要来源于重新配置和扩展预训练通路（非对角和零空间分量），而不是大幅改变奇异值。该分析为理解后训练机制提供了新的几何视角，但论文未披露具体模型架构、训练目标或部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学评估套件性能保留", "value": "移除对角分量通常保留大多数后训练增益", "setting": "12条后训练链（SFT和RL）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在预训练矩阵的SVD框架下将权重更新分解为对角（重塑奇异值）、非对角（旋转输入输出方向耦合）和零空间（在原始SVD核心之外路由）三个几何上不同的组件；通过对12条后训练链（包含SFT和RL）进行此类分解并开展消融实验，发现移除对角成分通常能保留大部分后训练增益，表明后训练增益主要来自重新配置和扩展预训练通路而非大幅改变奇异值

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [129] [LoRA-generating hypernetworks for efficient on-device LLM generative personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24979)
*Augenstein, Sean,Ding, Li,Lee, Jihwan,Rush, Keith,Zhmoginov, Andrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于超网络的端侧LLM个性化方法，通过生成用户特定的LoRA权重，实现高效个性化的同时避免ICL的延迟和PEFT的设备端计算负担。方法在多个个性化数据集上进行了验证，专注于长文本生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备有限计算资源下，如何高效地对端侧LLM进行个性化，同时避免上下文学习（ICL）的延迟增加和参数高效微调（PEFT）的设备端计算不可行问题。

Method: 该方法训练一个超网络，输入用户上下文令牌，输出一个低秩适应（LoRA）权重矩阵。超网络本身部分复用目标LLM的权重，从而减少额外存储。在部署阶段，训练好的公共工件（超网络和目标LLM）被部署到用户设备。每个用户使用超网络，仅通过前向传播（无需反向传播）在设备上合成个性化的LoRA。然后，该LoRA被应用于目标LLM，修改其权重以实现个性化。整个过程中，目标LLM的权重保持不变，仅通过LoRA进行适配。这种方法结合了ICL和PEFT的优点：与ICL类似，设备端计算可行；与PEFT类似，通过权重修改避免输入序列延长带来的延迟增加。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 训练一个超网络将用户上下文令牌映射到适合该用户的LoRA权重；端侧仅通过前向传播合成个性化LoRA，避免反向传播；超网络部分复用目标LLM的权重，减少额外存储和计算

Abstract: On-device large language models (`LLMs'), e.g. running on mobile phones, are ripe for improvement via personalization. The limited compute resources of mobile devices impose limits on model scale and thus model quality, making any realizable quality gains highly impactful. At the same time, their personal nature (i.e., the close coupling to a particular user) means that a given on-device LLM tends to be used in similar, predictable patterns over the course of time. This paper presents a novel method for personalizing on-device LLMs. It trains a hypernetwork to map a user's context tokens to a low-rank adaptation (`LoRA') well-suited to that user. Once the trained common artifacts are deployed to users' devices, each user uses the hypernetwork to synthesize (entirely on device) a personalized LoRA. This approach blends the benefits while avoiding the drawbacks of two existing approaches to LLM customization: in-context learning (`ICL') and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (`PEFT'). Like ICL (and unlike PEFT), the on-device phase of our approach is computationally feasible, requiring only forward passes through neural networks. Like PEFT (and unlike ICL), our approach modifies the `target' base LLM via weights (the LoRA), avoiding negative consequences (e.g. increased latency) associated with extending the input sequence. Our approach is particularly well-suited to the mobile device regime. Apart from the on-device compute and latency benefits mentioned, it also requires minimal additional storage, as internally its architecture partly leverages the same LLM weights as belong to the target LLM to be personalized. We demonstrate the benefits of LoRA-generating hypernetworks on several representative personalization datasets, comparing against baselines like ICL and PEFT. Of note, our personalization experiments focus on more challenging and less studied long-form text generation tasks.

</details>


### [130] [Complex KDA: Understanding and Enhancing the Expressivity of Kimi Delta Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24797)
*Siems, Julien,Grazzi, Riccardo,PÃ¶ppel, Korbinian,Singh, Jaisidh,Zela, Arber,Carstensen, Timur,Jitsev, Jenia,Hutter, Frank,Cevher, Volkan,Orvieto, Antonio,Klein, Aaron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CKDA通过扩展KDA的参数量范围，用单个delta规则加通道门反射实现2D旋转，理论证明达到DeltaProduct_2的表达力，并在状态跟踪和语言建模上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RNN基于delta规则的更新用低秩校正限制了表达力，已有工作通过组合两个delta规则转移来建模2D旋转，但会增加更新秩和计算成本；如何在不增加成本的情况下提升KDA的状态跟踪表达能力。

Method: CKDA基于Kimi Delta Attention (KDA)，一种线性RNN架构，其循环更新采用delta规则：状态转移矩阵为对角加秩一，并保持非扩张性以保证稳定性。原KDA的门控和delta规则系数β有特定范围限制，导致只能实现有限表达力。CKDA通过扩展参数范围（门控在[-1,1]，β在[0,2]）使得单次delta规则变换结合通道门提供的反射可以合成2D旋转，从而在不增加秩的情况下达到与组合两个delta规则转移（DeltaProduct_2）相同的表达能力。理论证明每个正交对角加秩一矩阵恰好是CKDA转移矩阵，且单层CKDA可跟踪SO(3)的有限子群。训练目标为标准语言建模（下一个token预测），推理时以循环方式计算，保持线性复杂度。具体实现细节、层数、隐藏维度等未在摘要中公开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长度外推性能", "value": "最强（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "S_3, S_4, 周期性音频续写任务"}, {"metric": "语言建模性能", "value": "优于Transformer和其他线性RNN，与KDA基线相似", "setting": "语言建模基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明KDA可以通过其通道门提供的反射与单个delta规则变换组合来实现2D旋转；扩展KDA参数范围：门控允许[-1,1]且delta规则系数β允许[0,2]，形成Complex KDA (CKDA)；表征CKDA表达力，证明每个正交对角加秩一矩阵恰好是CKDA转移矩阵，达到DeltaProduct_2的状态跟踪表达力；单层CKDA可跟踪SO(3)的任意有限子群，许多状态跟踪任务比对角加秩一线性RNN少用一层；在语言建模中CKDA优于Transformer和其他线性RNN，与KDA基线相当，并展示有前景的缩放行为

Abstract: Linear RNNs based on the delta-rule enable efficient sequence modeling, but their linear updates with a low-rank correction constrain their expressivity. Prior work has shown that composing two delta-rule transitions in a single recurrent update can model a 2D rotation, but this increases the rank and the cost of the updates compared to a single transition. We show that Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) can realize 2D rotations by combining a single delta-rule transformation with a second reflection supplied by its channel-wise gate. This requires extending the parameter ranges of KDA by combining two existing range extensions: allowing gates in $[-1,1]$ and the delta-rule coefficient $\beta$ in $[0,2]$. We call the resulting model Complex KDA (CKDA). It preserves KDA's stability and efficiency, with transitions that remain diagonal-plus-rank-one and non-expansive, while reaching the state-tracking expressivity of DeltaProduct$_2$. We characterize the expressivity of CKDA and prove that every orthogonal diagonal-plus-rank-one matrix is exactly a CKDA transition matrix. A single CKDA layer can track every finite group isomorphic to a subgroup of $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and many state-tracking results use one fewer layer for CKDA compared to other diagonal-plus-rank-one Linear RNNs. Empirically, combining both extensions yields the strongest length extrapolation among tested KDA range settings on $S_3$, $S_4$, and periodic audio continuation. In language modeling, CKDA outperforms Transformers and other linear RNNs, obtains similar results to a KDA baseline, and shows promising scaling behavior. Our code is open source at https://github.com/OpenEuroLLM/ComplexKDA and our models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/openeurollm/complexkda.

</details>


### [131] [RAILS: Retrieval-Augmented Incremental LLM Clustering at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24464)
*Oliya, Armin,Sawczuk, Aleksandra,BiaÅobrzeski, RadosÅaw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAILS将LLM聚类改造为检索增强的增量标签池循环，通过文档批处理与有界并发实现生产规模吞吐，在六个公开基准上将Accuracy从51.2%提升至59.3%、NMI从67.2%提升至74.8%、ARI从45.4%提升至54.7%，并已在SaaS工单主题发现生产流水线中替代HDBSCAN阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以LLM作为生产规模的聚类器存在两个瓶颈：提示无法容纳完整标签空间，且逐文档串行处理无法满足真实工作负载所需吞吐。

Method: RAILS 将聚类过程转化为对不断增长的标签池的迭代循环。输入为待聚类的文档流；每个文档先通过检索模块从当前标签池中召回候选标签，使提示长度不随标签总数线性增长。模型在提示中选择已有标签或将文档归入新标签，新标签随后加入标签池，形成有状态增量更新。为提升吞吐，文档按批次并行处理，并通过有界并发控制同时进行的LLM调用数量，避免过载。整体流程以LLM作为聚类器，检索模块负责在有限提示预算下提供候选标签空间，批处理与并发负责规模化推理，标签池状态支持新文档到来时的持续演化，而无需全量重聚类。论文未披露训练目标、模型规模、硬件、延迟或成本等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Accuracy", "value": "51.2% -> 59.3%", "setting": "六个公开基准平均，对比最强先前LLM聚类方法"}, {"metric": "NMI", "value": "67.2% -> 74.8%", "setting": "六个公开基准平均，对比最强先前LLM聚类方法"}, {"metric": "ARI", "value": "45.4% -> 54.7%", "setting": "六个公开基准平均，对比最强先前LLM聚类方法"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "聚类质量", "value": "高于传统HDBSCAN阶段", "setting": "SaaS工单主题发现生产流水线，定性描述未给具体数值"}]}

Conclusion: 将聚类建模为对不断增长的标签池的简单循环，使用检索增强方式为每个文档检索候选标签；通过文档批处理与有界并发实现可扩展吞吐；支持有状态增量操作，新数据无需全量重聚类；提示驱动的透明可控聚类行为，替代传统HDBSCAN阶段

Abstract: Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as the clusterer at production scale is hard: prompts cannot hold the entire label space, and per-document serial processing does not deliver the throughput real workloads require. We present RAILS, a retrieval-augmented incremental LLM clusterer that turns clustering into a simple loop over a growing label pool and scales through document batching with bounded concurrency. On six public benchmarks RAILS exceeds the strongest prior LLM-clustering method on average, lifting accuracy from 51.2% to 59.3%, NMI from 67.2% to 74.8%, and ARI from 45.4% to 54.7%. We further report production-deployment evidence from a SaaS ticket-topic-discovery pipeline, where RAILS has replaced a traditional HDBSCAN stage with higher clustering quality, transparent prompt-driven control, and stateful incremental operation.

</details>


### [132] [Prescriptive SVD-Inspired Attention via Spectral Energy Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24370)
*Arampatzakis, Vasileios,Sevetlidis, Vasileios,Pavlidis, George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SVDA 注意力机制，通过在注意力得分中注入可学习对角谱，使潜在方向可解释，并在此基础上进行谱能量保留裁剪，在图像分类任务上以 2.6-4.3% 参数和 2.8-5.4% MACs 的减少实现了几乎无损的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不显著损失精度的前提下，使 Transformer 注意力机制内部的潜在方向可解释、可操作，并实现对注意力得分通路的确定性修改（如剪枝）。

Method: SVDA 在标准 Transformer 自注意力机制基础上，将 query-key 点积得分替换为引入可学习对角谱的交互形式：对查询和键的投影后，应用一个可学习的对角矩阵（谱）来调制注意力得分，使得注意力矩阵的潜在方向（通过 SVD 分解理解的左右奇异向量方向）变得显式且可检查。通过谱熵、有效秩、稀疏性、对齐度和扰动响应等指标，可以诊断哪些潜在方向是结构重要的。论文提出了诊断-干预-验证框架：先诊断注意力得分的谱结构，然后基于诊断结果实施干预——本论文中评估的干预为谱能量保留，即按预设能量保留率 ρ（如 0.90）裁剪掉低能量方向，从而减少注意力得分通路的维度，进而降低参数和计算量。最后验证裁剪后模型在多个图像分类数据集上的精度变化。训练目标与标准分类训练一致，没有引入额外的损失项；推理时使用裁剪后的注意力通路，实现参数和 MACs 的减少。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "参数减少", "value": "2.6-4.3%", "setting": "SVDA ρ=0.90 在 FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Food-101 上的剪枝后模型"}, {"metric": "估计 MACs 减少", "value": "2.8-5.4%", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "得分方向移除比例", "value": "24.5-53.7%", "setting": "ρ=0.90 处方"}, {"metric": "精度变化（配对均值）", "value": "-0.03 到 +0.05 百分点", "setting": "三个随机种子，SVDA 裁剪后模型 vs 基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 SVDA（SVD-Inspired Attention），在 query-key 得分交互中引入可学习的对角谱，使潜在注意力方向可被谱熵、有效秩、稀疏性等指标检验。；建立诊断-干预-验证框架，对注意力得分通路实施谱能量保留（裁剪），并量化其对参数、MACs 和精度的影响。；在多个图像分类数据集上验证 ρ=0.90 的裁剪处方：移除 24.5-53.7% 的得分方向，参数减少 2.6-4.3%，估计 MACs 减少 2.8-5.4%，配对平均精度变化为 -0.03 到 +0.05 个百分点。

Abstract: Self-attention is central to modern Transformer architectures, but its dense dot-product formulation makes it difficult to identify which internal directions are structurally important and which can be modified without disrupting the model. SVD-Inspired Attention (SVDA) addresses part of this problem by introducing a learned diagonal spectrum into the query-key score interaction, making latent attention directions explicitly inspectable through indicators such as spectral entropy, effective rank, sparsity, alignment, selectivity, and perturbation response. This paper examines the transition from diagnostic interpretation to operational intervention. A diagnosis--intervention--verification framework is proposed, and one intervention is evaluated: spectral energy retention in the attention-score pathway. Across FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Food-101, the $\rho=0.90$ prescription removes 24.5--53.7\% of score directions, reduces parameters by 2.6--4.3\%, and reduces estimated MACs by 2.8--5.4\%. The paired mean accuracy change of the dimension-reduced model ranges from $-0.03$ to $+0.05$ percentage points over three seeds. These results support SVDA as an intrinsically interpretable attention mechanism whose learned spectrum exposes an operational coordinate system for deterministic and verifiable modification of attention-score formation.

</details>


### [133] [H-Spec: Parallel Speculative Decoding Without a Drafter-Side KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24197)
*Jiang, Weifan,Chitty-Venkata, Krishna Teja,Flynn, Megan,Meyerson, Reed,Qi, Zhenting,Wu, Tianyu,Kurtic, Eldar,Yu, Minlan,Marques, Alexandre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-Spec提出一种无需drafter侧KV缓存的混合Mamba-注意力并行草稿架构，通过原地复用目标KV并仅在最末位置注入目标隐藏状态，在保持块并行草稿质量的同时消除草稿缓存开销，在三个目标模型上实现了5–13%的接受长度提升和5–12%的解码延迟加速，并发场景下吞吐更高、KV占用更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散草稿模型在推测解码中需要为每个请求维护独立的drafter侧KV缓存，导致随并发数增长的内存开销和KV写入开销；而直接原地复用目标模型的KV又无法在块内维持草稿质量。如何在无drafter侧KV缓存的条件下保持高质量并行草稿，并降低并发服务时的显存占用与延迟。

Method: H-Spec包含一个基于块扩散的并行草稿器，采用混合Mamba-注意力结构。输入端，草稿器通过两个互补来源获取目标模型上下文：(1) 直接原地复用目标模型的键值缓存（KV）作为注意力模块的KV；(2) 仅在每个块的最后一个输入位置，将目标模型的隐藏状态投影后注入Mamba模块，作为其初始化状态。注意力模块负责在草稿过程中利用原地复用的目标KV进行并行令牌预测，而Mamba模块则通过并行扫描提供递归形式的上下文整合，两者协同保证块内草稿质量。由于不需要为草稿器单独维护KV缓存，每请求的草稿缓存内存和KV写入开销被消除。推理流程：给定目标模型前向传播产生的隐藏状态和KV，草稿器并行预测多个未来令牌，然后将候选令牌提交给目标模型进行验证，验证通过则输出，否则回退。该设计在并发服务时能降低KV缓存利用率并提升吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均接受长度", "value": "5.0–13.3%", "setting": "相比最佳基线，跨三个目标模型和多样任务"}, {"metric": "batch-size-1 inter-token latency speedup", "value": "5.3–12.6%", "setting": "相比最佳基线，跨三个目标模型和多样任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 混合目标上下文注入：直接原地复用目标KV，并仅在最后一个输入位置注入目标隐藏状态，从而避免维护独立的drafter侧KV缓存。；H-Spec混合Mamba-注意力并行草稿器：Mamba模块由投影后的末token目标隐藏状态初始化，注意力模块原地复用目标KV。；利用Mamba的并行扫描在保持块并行草稿的同时支持循环形式，实现并行推测解码。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates large language model inference by having a lightweight draft model predict future tokens for verification by the target model. Recent block diffusion drafters further reduce drafting latency by predicting multiple tokens in parallel. However, existing block drafters project target hidden states at every input position into a separate drafter-side KV cache, incurring per-request memory and KV-write overhead that grow with concurrency; directly reusing target KVs in place removes this cache but fails to sustain draft quality throughout the block. We propose a hybrid target-context injection method that complements direct target KV reuse with target hidden states only at the last input position, requiring no separate drafter-side KV cache. Building on this design, we propose H-Spec, a hybrid Mamba-attention parallel drafter that consumes the two target-context sources through complementary modules. Mamba modules are initialized with projected last-token target hidden states, while attention modules reuse target KVs in place. Despite its recurrent formulation, Mamba's parallel scan allows H-Spec to preserve block-parallel drafting. Across three target models and diverse tasks, H-Spec improves over the best baseline by 5.0--13.3% in mean accepted length and 5.3--12.6% in batch-size-1 inter-token latency speedup. Under concurrent serving, H-Spec consistently achieves higher throughput while maintaining lower KV cache utilization than baselines across evaluated concurrency levels.

</details>


### [134] [Acceptance-Aware Draft Model Training for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24150)
*Xia, Tianhua,Ganesan, Mugilan,Feng, Yifei,Wang, Haiyu,Egger, Maximilian,Zhang, Sai Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出接受长度感知的草稿模型训练损失（EAL和WTV），直接优化推测解码的期望接受长度，并可结合GRPO强化学习。实验表明在多种目标/草稿模型、任务和解码设置下，接受长度优于传统KL训练，且不同损失适配不同解码模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿模型训练方法主要优化交叉熵或KL散度等分布匹配代理目标，并未直接优化推测解码的接受长度，且贪婪解码与采样解码的接受机制不同，导致加速效果次优。

Method: 论文针对推测解码框架：草稿模型生成多个候选token，目标模型单次前向验证。草稿模型训练阶段，输入为上下文token序列，输出为草稿分布。核心修改在训练目标：对于贪婪验证，定义期望接受长度（EAL）损失，直接最大化推测窗口内被接受的token期望数；对于采样解码，定义窗口总变分（WTV）损失，优化温度缩放后的草稿分布与目标分布在窗口内的重叠，并考虑token间顺序接受依赖。两种损失均可与GRPO强化学习阶段结合，以模拟接受长度为奖励进行优化。推理时，草稿模型按标准推测解码流程生成候选，目标模型并行验证，接受长度提升直接转化为加速。论文未披露具体模型架构、训练数据、硬件或延迟数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出接受长度感知训练损失，直接优化推测窗口内的期望接受token数；针对贪婪验证推导期望接受长度（EAL）损失，显式最大化期望接受长度；针对采样解码引入窗口总变分（WTV）损失，优化温度缩放后草稿与目标分布的重叠并考虑顺序接受依赖；将上述目标与组相对强化学习（GRPO）阶段结合，以模拟接受长度作为奖励

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate multiple candidate tokens that are verified by the target model in a single forward pass. Its speedup is largely determined by the acceptance length, yet existing draft-model training methods mainly optimize cross-entropy or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence as proxies. These objectives encourage distribution matching but do not directly optimize acceptance length, and the acceptance mechanism also differs between greedy and sampling-based decoding. In this work, we propose acceptance-length-aware training losses that directly optimize the expected number of accepted tokens within a speculative window. For greedy verification, we derive an expected accepted length (EAL) loss that explicitly maximizes expected acceptance length. For sampling-based decoding, we introduce a window total variation (WTV) loss that optimizes the overlap between temperature-scaled draft and target distributions while accounting for sequential acceptance dependencies. Both objectives can be further combined with a group-relative reinforcement learning stage (GRPO) using simulated acceptance length as the reward. Experiments across different target and draft models, tasks, and decoding settings show that our losses consistently improve acceptance length over KL-based training. WTV provides particularly strong gains under sampling-based decoding, while EAL better matches greedy verification. These results show that directly optimizing the acceptance objective, with losses tailored to the decoding mode, is more effective than conventional distribution-matching objectives.

</details>


### [135] [Luck Is Not Skill: When Do Paired Rollouts Help Group-Relative RL of LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24144)
*Sakib, Nazmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体群体相对强化学习中的配对rollout方法，理论分析表明配对能减少奖励对比方差但不一定降低梯度方差，并在2B工具使用智能体上进行了三种子实验，工具故障下成功率提升5.1个百分点但未达预注册学习曲线标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体相对强化学习（如GRPO）中，独立环境噪声会模糊同一prompt下rollout的比较，配对rollout能否减少梯度方差并提升学习效果尚不明确。

Method: 论文提出配对rollout方法用于群体相对强化学习。输入是同一prompt下的多个rollout轨迹；核心模块是事件键控的噪声调度：在每个组内，所有rollout共享同一个事件键的噪声序列，但每个rollout的噪声实现不同，从而保持每个rollout的边缘分布不变。配对消除了奖励对比方差中源于不同噪声调度的组间分量。训练目标为群体相对策略优化，使用奖励对比作为优势估计。作者在2B工具使用智能体上实例化，环境包含工具故障和评分翻转两种噪声。推理/服务路径未详细说明。理论部分推导了一侧评分噪声下方差降低的精确条件，并构造了奖励对比改善但梯度方差增加的反例。实验部分通过梯度探针测量多个检查点上的协方差迹，验证了方差缩减机制，但未观察到一致的学习速度提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "final noisy-test success", "value": "+5.1 percentage points on average (all three seed differences positive)", "setting": "tool faults, 2B tool-use agent, paired rollouts vs unpaired"}, {"metric": "validation-AUC difference", "value": "+0.003 (95% interval [-0.029, +0.033])", "setting": "grader flips, paired rollouts vs unpaired"}, {"metric": "mean-centered covariance traces reduction", "value": "21 to 30% for grader flips; 40 to 63% for tool faults", "setting": "gradient probe on eight distinct checkpoints from two fault-trained trajectories"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出配对rollout方法：组内共享事件键噪声调度，同时保持每个rollout的边缘分布不变。；理论分析：配对消除奖励对比方差中的组间调度分量，但不一定降低梯度方差；给出一侧评分噪声下方差降低的精确条件及反例。；在2B工具使用智能体上，针对工具故障和评分翻转噪声，进行三种子受控实验，并预注册了已完成的试点。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning compares rollouts of the same prompt, but independent environment noise can obscure these comparisons. We study paired rollouts, which share an event-keyed noise schedule within each group while preserving each rollout's marginal distribution. Pairing removes the between-schedule component of reward-contrast variance, but need not reduce gradient variance. For one-sided grader noise, we derive an exact condition for reduction and give a counterexample in which reward contrasts improve while gradient variance increases. A controlled study trains a 2B tool-use agent under tool faults and grader flips, with three seeds per design. The protocol was registered with a disclosed, previously completed pilot. Under tool faults, pairing improves final noisy-test success by +5.1 percentage points on average, with all three seed differences positive, but misses the registered learning-curve criterion. The criterion is also missed under grader flips: the validation-AUC difference is +0.003 (95% interval [-0.029, +0.033]). A gradient probe on eight distinct checkpoints from two fault-trained trajectories finds lower mean-centered covariance traces under both noise types: 21 to 30% for grader flips and 40 to 63% for tool faults. These finite-sample measurements support the variance mechanism without establishing a general learning-speed benefit. The results distinguish improving reward comparisons, reducing estimator variance, and improving learning.

</details>


### [136] [CLOOPD: Closing the Learner Loop in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24141)
*Zheng, Keye,Li, Hanyu,Cheng, Zhan,Gao, Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLOOPD提出闭环在线策略蒸馏框架，将教师信号获取与学生实现分离，通过冻结打分批次、重复actor pass与token预算控制，提升教师token利用效率；在8-H20上6组300步实验中宏准确率从15.41提升至17.78/19.36，并在第100步以更少教师token达到TOP-D第300步水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏中每个新批次需支付两次成本（学生生成轨迹、教师打分），但现有方法通常只用一个actor更新消费该信号，导致教师打分token的利用率低、学习不充分。

Method: CLOOPD将教师信号获取与学生端实现解耦。对学生生成的轨迹批次，教师进行打分并构造教师信号；框架在KL包络内选择一个自适应α路点，并冻结该已打分批次及其优势值。随后进行学生端actor更新：每次actor pass后重新前向学生、度量其对已冻结教师信号的实现程度，并按单独的token预算分配actor工作量。框架提供确定性的两遍与三遍策略、按token定价的CLOOPD-TPMR以及预算匹配对照，目标是更充分地利用每个教师打分token。训练目标仍基于蒸馏KL信号与优势加权更新，但通过闭环复用已打分批次提高信号利用率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro accuracy", "value": "15.41 -> 17.78", "setting": "6组300步运行，8-H20节点，与one-pass TOP-D anchor在可比教师token规模下"}, {"metric": "macro accuracy", "value": "15.41 -> 19.36", "setting": "同上，CLOOPD-Fixed3"}, {"metric": "macro accuracy at step 100", "value": "15.35", "setting": "CLOOPD-Fixed3，8-H20，300步训练中的第100步"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将教师信号获取与学生端实现分离的闭环框架CLOOPD；在KL包络内选择自适应α路点并冻结已打分批次及其优势；每次actor pass后重新前向学生、度量实现程度并按独立token预算分配actor工作量；确定性两遍/三遍策略、按token定价的CLOOPD-TPMR以及预算匹配对照

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) pays twice for each fresh batch: the student generates trajectories and a stronger teacher scores them. Existing methods improve which trajectories are scored and how the teacher signal is constructed, but usually consume it with one actor update. We introduce CLOOPD, a closed-loop framework separating teacher-signal acquisition from student-side realization. CLOOPD selects an adaptive $\alpha$ waypoint inside a KL envelope, freezes the scored batch and its advantages, re-forwards the student after each actor pass, measures realization, and allocates actor work under a separate token budget. The framework includes deterministic two- and three-pass policies, token-priced CLOOPD-TPMR, and a budget-matched control. Across six 300-step runs on an 8-H20 node, every CLOOPD policy improves the one-pass TOP-D anchor at comparable teacher-token scale: macro accuracy rises from 15.41 to 17.78 with CLOOPD-Fixed2 and 19.36 with CLOOPD-Fixed3. At step 100, CLOOPD-Fixed3 reaches 15.35, nearly matching TOP-D at step 300 while using 67.2% fewer teacher-scored tokens and 28.0% fewer GPU-hours. Earlier 8-A100 ablations show adaptive $\alpha$ eliminates observed trust-envelope violations; a third pass adds headroom. These results position CLOOPD as a framework for budgeting how fully students learn from teacher-scored tokens.

</details>


### [137] [Graph-to-Grid (G2G): Continuous-Coordinate Feature Painting for Soccer Pass Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24040)
*GÃ¼nay, Kaan,Gun, Orhun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graph-to-Grid (G2G) 方法，将足球球员的连续坐标特征通过双线性散点绘制到网格，并端到端训练每球员编码器。在 2022 世界杯 53,628 次传球上，相比仅使用栅格特征，选择似然提升约 1/4 nat，且消融显示原始特征绘制贡献了大部分增益。该方法对推荐系统中的连续特征建模与空间特征表示具有借鉴意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型将球员状态表示为每格计数的栅格，丢失了球员在格内的精确坐标信息；本文评估将连续坐标特征通过双线性散点法绘制到网格上，并端到端训练每球员编码器，能否提升传球表面预测性能。

Method: 输入为比赛状态：每个球员的连续坐标和9个原始特征（如位置、速度等），以及球的位置。处理流程为：1) 球员特征通过双线性散点按测量坐标绘制到网格上，形成连续坐标特征图；2) 网格特征输入核心网络（可为 SoccerMap 或 U-Net 类），其中可包含每球员编码器和消息传递模块；3) 输出为密集传球表面，每个网格单元对应传球到达概率、持球人选择该传球的似然以及该传球后的控球价值。训练目标为表面损失（具体损失函数未披露），该损失通过双线性散点反向传播至每球员编码器，实现端到端训练。推理时，将球员特征按坐标绘制后前向传播得到传球表面。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "选择似然 (selection likelihood)", "value": "约提升 1/4 nat", "setting": "2022世界杯 53,628 次传球，八折交叉验证，每臂调参5个种子，与仅栅格核心对比"}, {"metric": "原始特征绘制贡献", "value": "约四分之三的增益", "setting": "消融实验，无编码器绘制9个原始球员特征"}, {"metric": "编码器与消息传递增量", "value": "较小但可分辨的增量", "setting": "消融实验，绘制+学习编码器与消息传递 vs 仅绘制"}, {"metric": "跨提供商泛化", "value": "优势消失", "setting": "冻结模型在另一提供商数据上评估"}, {"metric": "跟踪误差鲁棒性", "value": "优势被压缩", "setting": "注入跟踪误差后评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Graph-to-Grid (G2G) 连续坐标特征绘制：将每个球员的特征按其实际坐标双线性散点至网格，替代离散分箱或学习式传输。；端到端训练：表面损失直接优化每球员编码器，而非先固定编码器再绘制。；大规模消融研究：预注册的13项研究定位提升来源（原始特征绘制贡献约四分之三增益，编码器与消息传递贡献较小）。；跨数据集与消融对比：在2022世界杯和德甲等数据上验证，并与 SoccerMap、U-Net 等基线比较。

Abstract: Dense pass surfaces give, for every pitch cell, whether a pass played there would arrive, whether the carrier would choose it, and what the possession would then be worth. The networks that draw them read the state as a raster of per-cell counts, losing where inside a cell each player stands. LiDAR detectors, bird's-eye-view perception and graph weather models move entity features onto a grid, binning each entity to a cell or learning the transfer. We evaluate the interpolated form: each player's features are scattered bilinearly onto the grid at the player's measured coordinates, so the surface loss trains the per-player encoder end to end. Those systems adopt an interface; this paper measures one. On 53,628 passes from the 2022 World Cup, painting improves selection likelihood over the same core fed rasters alone by about a quarter of a nat: in every match of an eight-fold cross-validation, with every arm tuned over five seeds, and after retraining on seven Bundesliga and 2. Bundesliga matches from another provider. Thirteen pre-specified studies locate the gain: painting the nine raw player features with no encoder carries three quarters of it, and the learned encoder and message passing add a smaller, resolved increment. Painting also helps the original SoccerMap and a canonical U-Net, whereas offset channels, a finer raster, an attention painter and a raster-free decoder do not. Frozen across the provider boundary the likelihood advantage is lost; injected tracking error compresses it. These results concern observed-endpoint prediction, not calibrated evaluation of hypothetical passes.

</details>


### [138] [Simpler Methods Work Better for L1 Penalized Logistic Models and Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23995)
*Raff, Edward,Holt, James*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对高维L1正则化线性模型的多种求解器进行实证比较，发现较老方法在生产环境中优于部分SOTA方法，并提出基于LBFGS次梯度的简单有效基线，强调其易于扩展和部署，适合工业级MLOps流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L1正则化线性模型求解器在大规模高维（d > 1,000,000）工业场景下速度慢、并行化效果差，难以在MLOps流水线中训练，且文献中一些所谓SOTA方法在实际通用场景中并不实用。

Method: 论文聚焦于L1正则化线性模型的优化求解器设计。输入为高维稀疏特征（维度d > 1,000,000），目标函数为线性模型的损失加上L1范数惩罚。研究中测试了多种来自文献的“SOTA”求解器，并比较其在大规模工业级语料上的训练速度、并行化能力和生产可用性。提出的新基线采用LBFGS优化次梯度，而非直接优化不可微的L1目标；通过少量调整（如次梯度处理、步长或迭代策略等，摘要未详述）使其在实践中高效。该方法虽缺乏理论收敛保证，但易于支持与扩展，适合MLOps流水线中的大规模训练。推理路径未在摘要中展开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统对比多种被文献称为SOTA的L1求解方法，发现较老方法在当前通用工业场景中更优；提出使用LBFGS在次梯度上的简单基线，并辅以少量调整，尽管文献因理论不收敛而否定该方法，但在实践中高效且易于支持与扩展；总结学术研究建议，避免过于自信的结论阻碍生产转化

Abstract: Linear models with an $L_1$-norm penalty remain state-of-the-art for high-dimensional ($d > 1,000,000$) tasks, offering a straightforward method for solving real-world industry problems. Despite their widespread use in industry and utility, many $L_1$ solvers are not effective for general use, are prohibitively slow, and are ineffective in parallelization. This makes them difficult to train in an MLOps pipeline on large industry-scale corpora. In this work, we test several proposed ``state-of-the-art'' solutions from the literature and find that older methods are currently far superior for general use. We also identify several recommendations for academics to perform research that avoids erroneously overconfident results, which can prevent the transition to production use. Equally surprising, we find that a new and simple baseline, using LBFGS on a sub-gradient, is highly effective with minor tweaks, despite being dismissed in the literature for theoretical non-convergence. In practice, we find it is an easier-to-support and easier-to-scale method for production use.

</details>


### [139] [Bilinear Optimization Divergence: Diagnosing Factor-Constrained LoRA Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23594)
*Wang, YongShun,Su, JianLin,Ma, Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从优化动力学角度分析LoRA持续学习中正交约束为何不足，提出BOD诊断框架，并据此设计SFOR与O-LoRA硬保护及WRP，在Qwen3-8B四任务实验中改善了反向迁移与遗忘指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LoRA持续学习中，正交性约束本身并不足以保证组合更新对历史特征的保护效果；保护效果取决于任务起始状态、参数化方式以及优化器实际位移，需要明确在何种架构条件下应保护什么量、如何施加约束。

Method: 论文提出BOD（Bilinear Optimization Divergence）作为以锚点为参考的有效更新响应诊断器，用于分析LoRA持续学习中正交性对组合更新的保护效果。有限步分析将情形分为两类：(1) 共享适配器：保护路由位移会通过变化的伴随因子留下学习锚点残差；(2) 零输出新块：可行的路由状态能够保护组合更新，同时两个当前因子保持可训练。基于该分析，针对共享适配器提出Semi-Frozen Orthogonal Routing (SFOR)，针对累积式O-LoRA提出当前块硬保护。Weight Residual Projection (WRP) 在优化器步之后强制施加所需的位移，以修正优化器实际位移与目标位移之间的偏差。受控双任务轨迹验证了预测的残差路径；在Qwen3-8B上的四任务实验刻画了不同约束带来的折衷。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "normalized historical response (shared family)", "value": "从19.12%降至0.005%", "setting": "受控双任务轨迹验证预测的残差路径"}, {"metric": "normalized historical response (cumulative family)", "value": "从7.72%降至0.002%", "setting": "受控双任务轨迹验证预测的残差路径"}, {"metric": "BWT (backward transfer)", "value": "从-2.47提升至-0.86", "setting": "Qwen3-8B四任务实验，SFOR，平均准确率(AA)几乎不变"}, {"metric": "three-order mean AA", "value": "从80.27%提升至81.30%", "setting": "Qwen3-8B四任务实验，O-LoRA硬保护"}, {"metric": "FM (forgetting measure)", "value": "从2.20降至0.43", "setting": "Qwen3-8B四任务实验，O-LoRA硬保护"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Bilinear Optimization Divergence (BOD)：一种以锚点为参考、在选定历史特征上诊断有效更新响应的诊断框架。；有限步分析区分共享适配器与零输出新块两种情形：共享适配器中保护路由位移会通过变化的伴随因子留下学习锚点残差；零输出新块中可行的路由状态可在两个当前因子均保持可训练的同时保护组合更新。；提出Semi-Frozen Orthogonal Routing (SFOR)用于共享适配器，以及对累积式O-LoRA的当前块硬保护。；提出Weight Residual Projection (WRP)，在优化器步之后强制施加所需位移。

Abstract: Orthogonality in a LoRA factor does not by itself specify what the composed update protects: the answer depends on the task-start state, the parameterization, and the realized optimizer displacement. We formalize this question through Bilinear Optimization Divergence (BOD), an anchor-relative diagnostic of effective-update response on selected historical features. The finite-step analysis distinguishes two cases. In a shared adapter, protecting the routing displacement leaves a learned-anchor residual through the changing companion factor. In a fresh zero-output block, a feasible routing state can protect the composed update while both current factors remain trainable. These conditions yield Semi-Frozen Orthogonal Routing (SFOR) for shared adapters and current-block hard protection for cumulative O-LoRA; Weight Residual Projection (WRP) enforces the required displacement after the optimizer step. Controlled two-task traces verify the predicted residual paths, reducing normalized historical response from 19.12% to 0.005% in the shared family and from 7.72% to 0.002% in the cumulative family. Four-task experiments on Qwen3-8B characterize the resulting trade-offs: SFOR improves backward transfer (BWT) from -2.47 to -0.86 with nearly unchanged average accuracy (AA), while O-LoRA hard protection improves three-order mean AA from 80.27% to 81.30% and forgetting measure (FM) from 2.20 to 0.43. Component controls also show that stricter feasibility need not improve final task performance. Together, the analysis and evidence provide an architecture-conditioned account of which constraint to enforce, how to enforce it, and how to interpret its empirical value.

</details>


### [140] [Optimal Multi-way Decision Trees for Stratified Sampling in Online Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23308)
*Takei, Tomoka,Ikeda, Shunnosuke,Takano, Yuichi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OMST框架，用最优多路决策树优化在线实验分层抽样，通过路径选择二进制优化最小化方差，在真实和模拟数据集上取得与现有方法相当或更优的方差缩减，且树结构浅层可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线对照实验中，如何在固定样本量下通过优化分层构造来最大化方差缩减（提升实验灵敏度）。

Method: OMST将分层构造建模为特征图上的路径选择问题。输入为特征和结果变量，首先通过监督最优分箱为数值特征生成与结果相关的候选分裂点，构建候选分裂的特征图。然后在特征图上选择一组路径，每条路径对应一个分层规则（即一个多路决策树的叶子节点）。选择过程通过精确的方差最小化二进制优化求解，目标是最小化分层抽样的估计量方差，约束包括连续比例分配或Neyman型最优分配下的分配约束。为降低优化规模，引入冗余候选路径约简和分配约束约简，删除对目标无贡献的路径。最终输出一棵浅层且可解释的多路决策树，树的分支条件定义分层，叶子节点对应不同的分层。训练目标与估计量方差直接关联，推理时根据树规则将样本分配到各层。该架构解决了传统分层抽样中分层构造依赖启发式、效果不稳定的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "方差缩减", "value": "可比或优于现有方法（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "真实数据集和模拟数据集上的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将分层抽样形式化为特征图上的路径选择问题，用最优多路决策树（OMST）表达分层规则。；提出精确的方差最小化二进制优化公式，支持连续比例分配和Neyman型最优分配。；引入监督最优分箱生成与结果相关的候选分裂点，提升数值特征的分层质量。；设计冗余候选路径和分配约束的约简程序，大幅缩小优化问题规模。

Abstract: Online controlled experiments, or A/B tests, are widely used to estimate causal effects on digital platforms. A central challenge is to improve experimental sensitivity, or statistical power, without increasing the experimental sample size. Stratified sampling is a classical variance reduction technique; however, its effectiveness depends critically on how the strata are constructed. We thus propose an optimization-based stratification framework for stratified sampling using optimal multi-way decision trees. Our method, called Optimal Multi-way Stratification Trees (OMST), formulates stratification as a path-selection problem over a feature graph. The selected paths define interpretable stratification rules and are optimized using an exact variance-minimizing binary optimization formulation under continuous proportional allocation and a Neyman-type optimal allocation. We incorporate supervised optimal binning to generate outcome-relevant candidate splits for numerical features. Furthermore, we introduce reduction procedures for redundant candidate paths and assignment constraints, substantially reducing the optimization problem size. Experiments on both a real-world and a simulated dataset demonstrate that OMST achieves comparable or superior variance reduction to existing methods while maintaining shallow and interpretable stratification trees.

</details>


### [141] [Perplexity Cost Understates What Activation Quantisation Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23125)
*Sathyanarayanan, Anish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文质疑常用聚合困惑度作为激活量化评估指标的充分性。通过12个模型、780组对比实验，发现困惑度虽能整体偏好保留更多归纳和检索能力的量化方案，但当困惑度仅上升1.2-1.5倍时，检索能力已大幅下降（0.554）而归纳仍保持较高（0.959）。进一步通过噪声控制与旋转基实验，证明损害主要源于坐标对齐而非误差幅度。旋转基量化能显著恢复归纳能力（0.001→0.980），但对检索恢复有限（0.694）。研究在AWQ部署配置下验证了该模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合困惑度（perplexity）能否准确反映激活量化对模型具体计算（如归纳和检索能力）的损害？

Method: 论文将激活量化视为对模型内部表示的一种变换。主要分析流程包括：1) 在不同模型家族（4个家族，12个模型）上进行激活量化，并使用聚合困惑度作为传统评估指标；2) 设计细粒度评估任务，分别测量归纳（induction）和检索（retrieval）能力在量化前后的保留率；3) 通过匹配的高斯噪声控制实验和仅随机化量化误差符号的实验，分离误差幅度与坐标对齐的影响；4) 在旋转基上进行量化，改变坐标对齐而不改变误差幅度，观察归纳和检索能力的变化；5) 在部署配置（如AWQ）下将激活推至4比特进行验证。数据流：输入为激活张量，量化模块施加量化变换，输出量化后的激活，随后模型前向传播并测量任务特定性能。训练目标未涉及，推理流程侧重于量化后模型的行为分析。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "induction accuracy retention", "value": "恢复至0.980", "setting": "旋转基量化，单块干预，3平均比特/ token"}, {"metric": "retrieval accuracy retention", "value": "恢复至0.694", "setting": "旋转基量化，单块干预，3平均比特/ token"}, {"metric": "induction accuracy retention", "value": "0.968", "setting": "端到端，4平均比特"}, {"metric": "retrieval accuracy retention", "value": "0.534", "setting": "端到端，4平均比特"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示困惑度作为聚合指标会掩盖量化对不同计算能力（归纳头与检索）的非均匀损害；通过高斯噪声控制和符号随机化实验证明量化误差的幅度并非损害主因，坐标对齐（旋转基）是关键；提出旋转基量化干预，在单块干预下将归纳能力从0.001恢复至0.980（3平均比特/ token）；在AWQ等部署配置下验证了激活4比特量化后的损害模式

Abstract: Activation quantisation is usually evaluated with an aggregate metric, perplexity, averaged over every token a model predicts. We ask whether that average identifies which computations a quantiser damages. Perplexity turns out to be a reliable aggregate signal: across 12 models from four families and 780 within-model comparisons, the arm perplexity prefers also retains more induction and more retrieval in all but 2.1 and 4.0 percent of cases respectively. But where perplexity has risen by only a factor of 1.2 to 1.5, induction still keeps 0.959 of its intact accuracy while retrieval has already fallen to 0.554, a gap the aggregate number does not surface. This gap has structure, not just size: a matched Gaussian-noise control of the same per-channel magnitude leaves it largely intact, and randomising only the sign of the quantisation error, every magnitude held fixed, is nearly as harmless, so magnitude alone does not explain the damage. Quantising in a rotated basis, which changes coordinate alignment without changing error magnitude, restores induction from 0.001 to 0.980 at three average bits per token in a single-block intervention, though retrieval recovers less completely at the same setting (0.694); end-to-end at four average bits, induction reaches 0.968 and retrieval 0.534. The pattern holds on two further models up to 32B parameters and, in the deployed configurations we tested, under AWQ once activations are pushed to 4 bits. A perplexity target bounds the average cost of a transformation applied to the activation; it does not, by itself, show which computations survived.

</details>


### [142] [Optimizers for Diffusion Models: A Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23055)
*Bolatov, Arman,Shulgin, Egor,Li, David,Shtanchaev, Abduragim,Stich, Sebastian U.,Panov, Maxim,Moulines, Eric,RichtÃ¡rik, Peter,TakÃ¡Ä, Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对离散扩散模型的优化器进行受控基准测试，在四种扩散形式上比较七种优化器，发现AdamW虽强但并非总是最优，且自回归预训练验证的优化器能有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在多个基准上已能匹配自回归语言模型，但其训练优化器的选择从未得到系统比较，新优化器也主要在自回归预训练上验证，迁移性未知。

Method: 论文构建了一个统一的离散扩散模型训练实验框架。输入是文本或图像数据，经过不同的扩散形式：掩码扩散（text8数据集）、均匀扩散（QM9分子数据集和通过高斯对偶的LM1B语言数据）以及图像高斯扩散（CelebA-64）。每种扩散形式定义了各自的加噪和去噪过程，模型架构基于Transformer或U-Net等标准网络。训练过程采用七种优化器：AdamW、Lion、Muon、SOAP、MARS、MARS-M和Schedule-Free。所有优化器共享相同的超参数搜索协议，涵盖学习率、权重衰减等关键参数的网格搜索或贝叶斯优化。每个任务的最优配置会在完整训练预算下用三个不同随机种子重新训练以验证稳定性。损失函数根据扩散形式不同，通常为去噪得分匹配或变分下界。推理阶段，模型从噪声开始迭代去噪生成样本，但论文主要关注训练优化器的比较，未深入涉及推理优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首个针对离散扩散模型的受控优化器基准，覆盖四种扩散形式（掩码扩散、均匀扩散、通过高斯对偶的LM1B、图像高斯扩散）；统一搜索协议比较七种优化器（AdamW、Lion、Muon、SOAP、MARS、MARS-M、Schedule-Free）；发现优化器最优选择随扩散形式变化，且自回归预训练验证的方法（Muon、MARS-M、SOAP）能迁移并击败调优后的AdamW；提供完整可复现的代码、运行记录和图表

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models now match autoregressive language models on several benchmarks, while the question of how best to train them has received far less attention: the optimizer is inherited from one paper to the next and never compared. New optimizers, meanwhile, are validated almost exclusively on autoregressive pretraining, a different objective on a different loss surface. We present a controlled optimizer benchmark across four diffusion formulations, to our knowledge the first for discrete diffusion: seven optimizers (AdamW, Lion, Muon, SOAP, MARS, MARS-M, Schedule-Free) on masked diffusion (text8), uniform diffusion (QM9, and LM1B through the Gaussian duality) and Gaussian diffusion on images (CelebA-64), each on a task with published reference values. Every optimizer receives the same search protocol, and every winner is retrained at the full budget with three seeds. AdamW is a strong default but not always the right choice: it is beaten by a resolved margin on two of the four tasks, and the winner changes with the formulation, so the optimizer deserves the same care as the rest of the training recipe. Notably, methods validated on autoregressive language model pretraining transfer well: Muon, MARS-M and SOAP each beat the tuned AdamW on at least one diffusion formulation. The benchmark, all runs and every figure are reproducible end to end from the released code at https://github.com/armanbolatov/diffusion-baselines.

</details>


### [143] [Token Utility Is Selection-Conditioned: Coupled Selection of Prompt Context and Response Supervision for Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22943)
*Wu, Can,Chen, Xinrui,Wu, Ou,Du, Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE提出选择条件化的令牌效用，通过验证引导的交互代理与预算化交替选择，协同选择提示上下文和响应监督，并采用结构感知投影生成连贯监督跨度。在三个模型家族上，数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随任务中整体优于对比选择方法，且数学推理优势随压缩率提升而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效LLM指令微调中，如何选择响应监督及其支持的提示上下文，避免独立评估提示与响应导致的“选择状态不匹配”。
