Method: BRIDGE包含三个主要阶段：首先，通过一个共享的验证方向交互代理（validation-directed interaction surrogate）预计算提示上下文和响应监督之间的交互分数。该代理利用验证集梯度方向，使得每个令牌的效用评估依赖于另一侧当前保留的子集状态，从而捕获选择条件化效用。其次，采用预算化交替选择（budgeted alternating selection）：在总预算约束下，交替固定一侧的保留子集，聚合预计算的交互分数以更新另一侧的条件分数，并据此选择下一轮保留子集，循环直至满足预算。该过程确保提示与响应的保留子集相互协调，解决了独立选择导致的状态不匹配问题。最后，结构感知投影（structure-aware projection）将条件化的响应分数映射为连贯的监督跨度（span-level），以保持训练监督的语义完整性。整个流程以离线方式运行，不修改模型架构，仅在指令微调的数据构建阶段进行令牌级选择，最终使用选定的提示-响应令牌对进行标准指令微调。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体选择方法性能", "value": "领先", "setting": "跨三个模型家族，数学推理、代码生成、指令跟随"}, {"metric": "数学推理优势（相对于独立选择）", "value": "随压缩率增加而增大", "setting": "数学推理任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出选择条件化的令牌效用概念，通过共享的验证方向交互代理，在对方保留子集状态下评估提示与响应。；预算化交替选择：基于当前对侧子集聚合预计算交互，更新条件分数，协调保留子集。；结构感知投影：将条件化响应分数转换为连贯的监督跨度。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) instruction tuning requires selecting response supervision with supporting prompt context. Existing methods typically value both sides separately, risking selection-state mismatch between valuation and retained training subsets. BRIDGE (Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection) captures selection-conditioned token utility through a shared validation-directed interaction surrogate valuing each side under the other's retained state. Budgeted alternating selection coordinates retained subsets by aggregating precomputed interactions over the current opposite-side subset to update conditional scores. Structure-aware projection converts conditional response scores into coherent supervision spans. Across three model families, BRIDGE leads compared selection methods overall in mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following. In mathematical reasoning, its advantage over independent selection grows with compression.

</details>


### [144] [COREM: Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping with Stateful Writeback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22487)
*Wang, Yan,Wang, Xiaochuan,Sun, Yuxiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COREM提出一种利用矩阵优化器状态单元间余弦关系重塑动量的方法，通过状态化写回使重塑动量持续影响优化。在CIFAR-10和enwik8上验证了中后期训练提升，且方形矩阵计算开销仅为Muon的13.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值优化器状态中存在的单元间关系未被独立条目处理所利用，探索如何利用这些关系提升优化性能。

Method: COREM将矩阵优化器动量状态划分为更新单元，计算单元间的余弦关系，并利用这些关系重塑动量。重塑后的动量被写回优化器状态（状态化写回），从而影响后续步骤的优化动态。该机制封装为通用单元关系变换抽象。在CIFAR-10 MLP和enwik8 Transformer上评估，与Muon对比。方形矩阵更新时，余弦关系变换的计算FLOPs约为Muon（5次Newton-Schulz迭代）的13.3%。谱诊断显示COREM增大熵有效秩、降低奇异能量集中度，同时保持各向异性谱。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终验证性能", "value": "优于Muon", "setting": "CIFAR-10，MLP模型"}, {"metric": "最终验证性能", "value": "与Muon相当", "setting": "enwik8，Transformer模型"}, {"metric": "变换FLOPs（方形矩阵更新）", "value": "约为Muon的13.3%", "setting": "Muon使用5次Newton-Schulz迭代"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 单元关系变换抽象：将矩阵优化器状态划分为更新单元，并计算单元间的余弦关系。；余弦关系动量重塑（COREM）：利用单元间余弦关系重塑动量，并将重塑后的状态写回优化器（状态化写回）。；状态化写回机制：使重塑后的动量影响当前及未来优化动态，而非仅当前更新。

Abstract: Matrix-valued optimizer states may contain relational structure that is not captured by treating their entries independently. We study whether relations within matrix-valued optimizer states can be exploited to improve optimization. To this end, we introduce a unit-relation-transform abstraction and instantiate it as COREM, a Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping method with stateful writeback. COREM partitions the momentum state into update units, computes cosine relations among them, and uses these relations to reshape the momentum before writing the transformed state back to the optimizer. This stateful mechanism allows the reshaped momentum to affect not only the current update but also future optimization dynamics. We evaluate COREM on CIFAR-10 with an MLP and on enwik8 with a Transformer. Compared with Muon, COREM shows lower early-stage step efficiency but stronger improvement in the mid-to-late stages of training, achieving better final validation performance on CIFAR-10 and comparable final performance on enwik8. Spectral diagnostics on enwik8 show that COREM consistently increases entropy effective rank and reduces the concentration of singular energy in dominant modes, while preserving an anisotropic spectrum. For square matrix updates, COREM requires approximately 13.3% of the transformation FLOPs of Muon with five Newton-Schulz iterations.

</details>


### [145] [Connected Content Retriever: Dense Graph Edge Features Powering Pre-Ranking at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22441)
*Gupta, Akhilesh,Ramanujam, Sudarshan Srinivasa,Mehta, Chirag Bhanuprasad,Beena, Reshma Asharaf,Das, Dhritiman,Tiwana, Birjodh Singh,Patel, Bhargavkumar Kanubhai,Lee, Mack,Tang, Renyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LinkedIn 提出 CC Retriever，将完整深度排序模型部署到 GPU 做 Feed 预排序，利用经济图谱的稠密图边特征与 sorted-search GPU join 原语，在 120ms p99 内对数万候选评分，模型参数量扩大 50 倍，在线 A/B 提升内容时长 2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LinkedIn Feed 预排序阶段，候选索引超过 10 亿，需要在 120ms p99 延迟预算内对数万条候选进行深度模型评分，传统 CPU 或浅层预排序模型难以承载更大参数量与稠密图亲和特征。

Method: 系统输入包括 viewer 的社交图谱（一度人脉的连接与关注、二度人脉的互动信号如点赞、评论、转发但非作者即 stranger viral）以及文档级特征。离线阶段从 LinkedIn 经济图谱中抽取 viewer-to-author 的稠密图亲和特征，并与人脉相关活动关联，形成超过 10 亿的候选索引。在线请求时，首先按 viewer 网络关系从 10 亿索引中筛选出约数万条候选活动。随后在 GPU 上执行所谓 sorted-search 原语，将 viewer-to-author 稠密图亲和特征与文档级特征进行 join，耗时 5-10ms。之后使用完整的深度排序模型对候选进行评分，该模型参数规模相比原预排序模型扩大 50 倍，全部在 GPU 上运行以满足 120ms p99 延迟预算，最终输出数百条最佳候选给后续排序层。训练目标未明确披露，推测为优化 Feed 互动或内容消费时长，系统通过在线 A/B 验证 +2.5% 内容时长提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Feed 内容时长（content time spent）", "value": "+2.5%", "setting": "LinkedIn Feed 在线 A/B 实验"}]}

Conclusion: 基于 GPU 的排序原语：sorted-search GPU primitive，将 viewer-to-author 稠密图亲和特征与文档级特征在 GPU 运行时进行 join，耗时 5-10ms。；将完整深度排序模型直接部署在 GPU 上执行预排序评分，替代浅层预排序模型。；利用 LinkedIn 经济图谱（一度/二度人脉、stranger viral 等参与信号）构建稠密图边特征用于预排序。；GPU 服务化使排序模型参数量扩展 50 倍，同时满足 120ms p99 延迟预算。

Abstract: In large-scale recommendation systems like the LinkedIn Feed, content generated by a member's network (connections and follows) makes up over 70% of impressions and engagement. It is therefore essential that the pre-ranking layer forwards the best possible few hundred candidates to the ranking layer. LinkedIn's professional knowledge graph carries engagement signals across both the first degree network (connections and follows) and the second-degree network: posts that a 1st-degree connection reacted to, commented on or reshared but did not author (a.k.a. stranger viral). Due to this fan out, the resulting candidate index exceeds one billion; selection of activities from the viewer's network narrows it down to roughly tens of thousands of activities that must be scored within a 120 ms p99 latency budget. We present Connected Content Retriever (CC Retriever), a pre-ranking system that scores these candidates with a full deep ranking model on GPUs at low latency. At its core is a sorted-search GPU primitive that joins dense graph affinity features (viewer to author) with document level features stored on the GPU at runtime in 5-10 ms. The shift to GPU served scoring enabled a 50x scale up of the ranking model's parameters and delivered a +2.5% lift in content time spent on the LinkedIn Feed in online experiments, significantly higher than the typical gains observed in LinkedIn Feed experiments. In this work, we describe the feature set we leverage from LinkedIn's economic graph and the model architecture used for scoring, with a particular emphasis on the online system that scales the stack.

</details>


### [146] [OpenBlock: Constructive and Verified Content Generation for Adaptive Tile-Matching Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22177)
*Jun, Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放的自适应三消游戏平台，核心是带验证门控的双轨内容生成（规则+学习）和自博弈强化学习放置智能体。研究发现高棋盘填充率下长条棋子无合法放置是主要失败模式，而非内容难度。14天线上灰度（48,000玩家）表明神经轨比纯规则轨提升次日留存1.8个百分点、会话时长7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三消拼图游戏每回合呈现哪些棋子会显著影响玩家体验与留存，但现有内容生成算法闭源且缺乏开放的难度自适应研究平台；同时需要保证生成内容的可放置性不因学习模型而退化。

Method: 系统采用双轨内容生成架构：一条是确定性的规则生成器，始终可用，用于保证基础内容供给；另一条是可选的基于学习（神经）的生成器。所有生成内容——无论来自规则轨还是学习轨——都必须经过统一的验证门控，该门控通过穷举顺序放置搜索来确认所交付的棋子集合完全可放置。验证门控确保学习轨不会破坏规则轨所具备的可构造性保证。论文还训练了一个自博弈强化学习放置智能体，其策略在游戏内进行放置决策，并通过辅助任务将每个形状的可放置性暴露给共享表示，以学习更有利于放置决策的特征表示。该智能体被用于诊断主导失败模式：在高棋盘填充率下，长条形状棋子失去大多数合法放置位置。仿真实验在控制填充率下量化了这一现象。在线部署采用灰度发布，比较仅规则轨与加入学习轨后的效果，采用样本比例验证和CUPED方差调整来评估留存和会话时长指标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "自博弈胜率", "value": "35.6%", "setting": "234,000+自博弈回合"}, {"metric": "中位得分", "value": "4200", "setting": "自博弈评估"}, {"metric": "长条棋子无合法放置的比例", "value": "33–56%", "setting": "棋盘填充率70–75%的受控模拟"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "次日留存率提升", "value": "1.8个百分点", "setting": "14天线上灰度，48,000玩家，样本比例验证，CUPED调整，对比仅规则轨"}, {"metric": "会话时长提升", "value": "7%", "setting": "14天线上灰度，48,000玩家，样本比例验证，CUPED调整，对比仅规则轨"}]}

Conclusion: 双轨内容生成架构：确定性规则生成器始终可用，可选学习生成器，二者均通过统一的验证门控。；基于穷举顺序放置搜索的验证门控，保证每个交付的棋子集合完全可放置，使学习轨不会降低规则轨的可构造性保证。；自博弈强化学习放置智能体，并辅以暴露每形状可放置性的辅助任务，用于诊断游戏主要失败模式。；识别出高棋盘填充率下长条形状棋子丢失绝大多数合法放置位置，是后期失败的主因，而非生成内容难度。；通过头对头消融证明每形状可放置性监督（而非聚合难度特征）驱动了表示增益。

Abstract: Tile-matching puzzle games serve hundreds of millions of players, yet the content-generation algorithms that decide which pieces to present at each turn remain proprietary, and no open platform exists for studying adaptive difficulty in this genre. We present an adaptive tile-matching platform whose central algorithmic contribution is a dual-track content-generation architecture: a deterministic rule-based generator that is always available, and an optional learned generator, both subject to a common verification gate that establishes, by exhaustive sequential-placement search, that every delivered piece set is fully placeable so the learned track can never degrade the constructive-feasibility guarantee of the rule track. A self-play reinforcement-learning placement agent, supervised by auxiliary tasks that expose per-shape placeability to shared representations, is used to diagnose the game's dominant failure mode: at high board fill, long-bar pieces lose the majority of their legal placements. Across 234,000+ self-play episodes the agent reaches a 35.6\% win rate (median score 4,200), and controlled simulation shows that at board fill rates of 70--75\%, 33--56\% of long-bar pieces have no legal placement, while spawn difficulty distributions are statistically indistinguishable between won and lost games---evidence that board-state degeneration, not content difficulty, drives late-game failure. Head-to-head ablations show that per-shape placeability supervision---not aggregate difficulty features---drives the representation gain, and a 14-day online gray rollout (48,000 players; sample-ratio verified, CUPED-adjusted) lifts day-1 retention by 1.8 percentage points and session duration by 7\% over the rule track alone, quantifying the neural track's asymmetric upside in live play.

</details>


### [147] [StepKV: Step-Aware KV Cache Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22158)
*Feng, Boyu,Liu, Jiahong,Li, Yifan,Yu, Wenhao,Qiu, Zexuan,Sun, Yuliang,Shen, Ming,Li, Xiang,Dai, Quanyu,King, Irwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepKV 提出步感知的 KV 缓存压缩，将推理步作为保留单元，结合步效用与 token 显著度全局排序，在多步智能体任务中低预算下比 token 级基线更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步 LLM 智能体推理中 KV 缓存随上下文线性增长导致存储与解码成本高；现有 token 级剪枝与推理步级单元不匹配，造成 Reasoning Continuity Disruption（早期关键观察或中间决策因注意力低被误删）。

Method: StepKV 将多步智能体产生的 KV 缓存条目与其生成推理步关联，使每个缓存条目带有步归属。压缩时，一方面从轨迹中导出信号（摘要未具体说明信号形式）估计每个推理步的效用，另一方面计算 token 级 saliency；两者组合成全局重要性分数，对所有可剪枝 token 进行全局排序，在给定 KV 预算下保留分数最高的条目，从而在 token 与步两个粒度同时保留关键信息。训练目标或优化细节未披露。推理时以压缩后的缓存进行解码，在低 KV 预算下维持多跳 QA 与长程网页推理任务的准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以推理步为第一类保留单元进行 KV 缓存压缩；将缓存条目关联到其生成步，并从轨迹信号估计步效用；结合步效用与 token 级 saliency 的全局重要性评分，在目标预算下保留 top-scoring 条目

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference, but the cache grows linearly with context length, increasing storage and decoding costs. KV cache compression mitigates this cost by retaining only a subset of cached tokens. This challenge is particularly important for multi-step LLM agents, where a query expands into trajectories of reasoning, tool interactions, and retrieved observations. Existing pruning methods typically treat the cache as a flat token stream and rank tokens by recency or attention saliency. This creates a mismatch between the unit of compression and the unit of reasoning: token-level pruning removes individual entries, whereas useful information in multi-step agents is often organized into reasoning steps with uneven and delayed importance. Consequently, an early observation or intermediate decision may receive little recent attention yet remain essential for later evidence synthesis. We term this failure mode Reasoning Continuity Disruption.These observations motivate KV cache compression that jointly considers token- and reasoning-step-level information. StepKV addresses this goal by treating reasoning steps as first-class retention units. It associates cache entries with their generating steps, estimates step utility from trajectory-derived signals, and combines this utility with token-level saliency. The resulting scores globally rank prunable tokens, from which StepKV retains the top-scoring entries under a target budget. StepKV thus provides a step-centric perspective for agent KV cache compression. Across multi-hop QA and long-horizon web reasoning tasks, StepKV sustains accuracy under low KV budgets where token-level baselines degrade sharply, offering a more robust efficiency-accuracy trade-off for multi-step agent inference.

</details>


### [148] [The Limits of Speculation: Bounding Speculative Decoding in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22156)
*Amankulov, Aidar,Mamatin, Denis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将MoE投机解码的预算选择建模为离线随机最短路径问题，构建Oracle分析验证成本，发现拒绝边界呈线性，揭示局部必要条件，为设计自适应在线启发式提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中投机解码的验证成本因输入依赖的专家加载而不稳定，如何为投机预算选择提供理论边界。

Method: 论文提出一个离线诊断框架：首先将投机预算选择建模为参考序列上的随机最短路径（SSP）问题，目标是最小化验证成本与投机收益的权衡。然后构建一个Oracle，通过反事实模拟来估计不同投机预算下MoE模型的验证成本，模拟过程中考虑专家加载的动态性。在Qwen3-Coder与EAGLE-3的配对实验中，Oracle在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中做出接受/拒绝决策，分析显示被拒绝的候选点构成严格的线性边界。该边界对应一个必要条件：边际期望成本的变化除以边际期望加速比（ΔE[Cost]/ΔE[a]）达到某个阈值。论文并未提出新的在线算法或部署系统，而是为未来设计自适应在线启发式方法提供数学参考点。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将投机预算选择形式化为离线随机最短路径（SSP）问题，构建基于反事实模拟的诊断Oracle以考虑MoE验证成本。；在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中分析Oracle决策，发现被拒绝的候选形成严格线性边界。；证明复杂全局优化在局部受必要条件支配：边际成本与期望进展之比达到平衡。

Abstract: Speculative decoding in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models faces the problem of unstable verification cost caused by input-dependent expert loading. To study the physics of this process, we formulate speculation-budget selection as an offline Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem over reference sequences and build a diagnostic Oracle that uses counterfactual simulation to account for MoE verification cost. A detailed analysis of the Oracle's decisions on the Qwen3-Coder and EAGLE-3 pairing, in the space of marginal deltas (Delta Space), shows that rejected candidates form a strict linear boundary. This result demonstrates that a complex global optimization is locally governed by a necessary condition balancing marginal cost against expected progress ($\frac{\Delta \mathbb{E}[Cost]}{\Delta \mathbb{E}[a]}$), providing a rigorous mathematical reference point for designing future adaptive online heuristics.

</details>


### [149] [SafeTune: A Unified Faithful Library for Auditing and Repairing Safety Drift in Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22153)
*Seth, Pratinav,Sadhu, Saisab,Kaushal, Anshul,Sankarapu, Vinay Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeTune是一个统一库，用于审计和修复微调LLM中的安全漂移，整合四种干预范式并提供共享的评估和部署工具，通过控制实验和金融、医疗部署案例展示其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型时出现的安全漂移问题，现有方法实现分散、生命周期阶段不兼容、评估协议不一致，难以采用和比较。

Method: SafeTune是一个源可用库，统一了四种干预范式：后处理权重恢复、安全约束微调、梯度遗忘和推理时引导。它提供配置驱动的统一工作流，保留各范式所需的独特输入和干预点。模块化注册表允许在不重新设计周围流水线的情况下添加新方法、基准、评判器、模型和微调领域。共享的可解释性、评估和部署实用程序支持安全漂移的表征、可行干预的评估以及校准或分层缓解。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 统一四种干预范式（后处理权重恢复、安全约束微调、梯度遗忘、推理时引导）的库；模块化注册表支持新方法、基准、评判器、模型和微调领域；配置驱动的统一工作流，保留各范式所需的不同输入和干预点；共享的可解释性、评估和部署工具

Abstract: Methods for addressing safety drift in fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) are scattered across incompatible implementations, lifecycle stages, and evaluation protocols, making them difficult to adopt and compare. We introduce SafeTune, a source-available library that unifies four intervention paradigms: post-hoc weight recovery, safety-constrained fine-tuning, gradient-based unlearning, and inference-time steering, alongside shared interpretability, evaluation, and deployment utilities. SafeTune provides a consistent configuration-driven workflow while preserving the distinct inputs and intervention points each paradigm requires. Its modular registry supports new methods, benchmarks, judges, models, and fine-tuning domains without redesigning the surrounding pipeline. We demonstrate SafeTune through controlled comparisons and finance and medical deployment case studies, showing how it characterizes safety drift, evaluates feasible interventions on common refusal-behavior and capability evaluations, and supports calibrated or layered mitigation.

</details>


### [150] [PRQuant: Permutation Residual Quantization for Low-Overhead Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22106)
*Wang, Peiran,Wang, Anqi,Zhao, Jiaying,Yang, Huiwen,Ming, Zhenyu,Wang, Rongqian,Yao, Yiwu,Tian, Kun,Yao, Xin,Zhang, Gong,Yang, Fan,Huang, Zhongyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRQuant是一种免训练的低比特量化框架，通过将导致量化误差的输入通道重排到连续尾部块，并离线构造静态权重残差，将分散的残差补偿转化为规则的尾部增强GEMM，从而在推理时无需在线收集操作，显著降低延迟。在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-30B模型上，相比MXFP4平均准确率分别提升1.24和0.55。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化线性层精度受少数异常值主导，现有平滑、旋转或残差方法虽缓解此问题却引入新的权重精度瓶颈，且大多为在线方法导致执行开销大。

Method: PRQuant是一个免训练、低开销的量化框架，用于线性层的低比特量化推理。流程如下：1）对权重应用AWQ风格的缩放（smoothing），以减轻激活异常值的影响；2）识别对权重量化误差贡献最大的输入通道，并将这些通道重排到连续的尾部块（permutation）；3）离线构造这些通道对应的残差权重子张量（static weight-side residual compensation）；4）推理时，由于尾部块在内存中连续，激活端可以直接使用尾部块，无需在线收集（gather）操作，同时将原本分散的残差补偿转化为规则的尾部增强GEMM（tail-augmented GEMM），从而大幅降低执行延迟。训练目标/优化：该方法免训练，不涉及梯度更新，完全依赖离线权重变换与静态残差。推理路径：输入激活经过AWQ缩放后，与重排后的权重进行GEMM，并额外累加尾部残差项，输出结果。模块之间数据流清晰：缩放->通道重排->残差子张量构造->推理时尾部增强GEMM。该设计解决了现有方法在线开销大以及引入新精度瓶颈的问题，在保持或提升精度的同时降低延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "提升1.24（相对MXFP4）", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型，五个下游基准平均"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "提升0.55（相对MXFP4）", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507模型，五个下游基准平均"}, {"metric": "down-projection重建误差", "value": "有效降低", "setting": "未披露具体数值与基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出PRQuant，免训练、低开销框架，结合通道重排与静态权重侧残差补偿；在AWQ风格缩放后识别对权重量化误差贡献最大的输入通道，将其重排至连续的尾部块，并离线构造对应的残差权重子张量；推理时利用连续结构使激活端无缝使用尾部块，无需昂贵的在线收集操作，将分散的残差补偿转化为规则的尾部增强GEMM，大幅降低延迟；消融实验表明平滑与残差补偿是数值提升的主要驱动因素，而重排提供一致的边际数值收益并实现硬件友好的连续布局，消除动态收集开销

Abstract: Accuracy of Low-bit quantization of linear layers is often dominated by a small number of outliers. Although existing methods, such as smoothing, rotation, or residual-based approaches, may mitigate this problem, they often introduce new accuracy bottlenecks to weights. Besides, most of these techniques are implemented as online approaches, which can result in heavy execution overheads. To address the afore-mentioned issues, We propose PRQuant (Permutation Residual Quantization), a training-free and low-overhead framework that combines channel reorganization with static weight-side residual compensation. After AWQ-style scaling, PRQuant identifies the input channels that contribute most to weight quantization error, permutes them into contiguous tail blocks, and constructs their residual weight sub-tensors offline. During inference, this contiguous structure enables the activation side to use tail blocks seamlessly without the expensive online gathering operation, and turns scattered residual compensation into a regular tail-augmented GEMM, substantially reducing latency. Experiments demonstrate that PRQuant effectively reduces down-projection reconstruction error. Ablation studies confirm that smoothing and residual compensation are the primary drivers of numerical improvement, while permutation provides a consistent marginal numerical benefit and, more importantly, enables a hardware-friendly contiguous layout that eliminates dynamic gathering overhead. Overall, PRQuant outperforms default MXFP4 and the evaluated PTQ baselines in average accuracy across five downstream benchmarks, improving over MXFP4 by 1.24 and 0.55 on Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, respectively.

</details>
